+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../types/frames";
+import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
+import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/registry";
+import { resampleBars } from "../../../chart/resampleBars";
+import { BINANCE_PERP, DXFEED_EQUITY, MINUTE_MS, barFrame, relativeVolumeCryptoFixture, relativeVolumeEquityFixture } from "../../../chart/validation/fixtures";
+import { nyLocalMinuteOfDay } from "../../killzones/killzones";
+import {
+  HOUR_MS,
+  createClockCache,
+  createRelativeVolumeEngine,
+  createRing,
+  readingOf,
+  requiredPriorOf,
+  resolveRvolClock,
+  ringCapacityOf,
+  ringInsert,
+  ringMean,
+  ringMedian,
+  ringRemove,
+  ringSupersede,
+  ringUpdate,
+  slotKeyOf,
+  usableVolume,
+  type RelativeVolumeMathSettings,
+} from "../relativeVolumeMath";
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
+  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
+});
+
+const T0 = Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2); // Monday 2026-03-02 00:00 UTC
+const EQUITY_SESSION_STARTS = relativeVolumeEquityFixture().labels.sessionStarts;
+
+function hourlyBar(day: number, hour: number, volume: number, closed = true): BarFrame {
+  return barFrame(T0 + day * 24 * HOUR_MS + hour * HOUR_MS, { open: 100, close: 100, high: 100, low: 100, volume }, BINANCE_PERP, HOUR_MS, closed);
+}
+
+/**
+ * The hand-computed three-session fixture of success criterion 1. Hourly UTC
+ * bars (24 slots a day), base volume 100 everywhere except slot 0:
+ * day 1: 100, day 2: 300, day 3: 200, day 4: 500.
+ */
+function threeSessionBars(): BarFrame[] {
+  const slotZero = [100, 300, 200, 500];
+  const bars: BarFrame[] = [];
+  for (let day = 0; day < 4; day += 1) {
+    for (let hour = 0; hour < 24; hour += 1) {
+      bars.push(hourlyBar(day, hour, hour === 0 ? slotZero[day] : 100));
+    }
+  }
+  return bars;
+}
+
+const SETTINGS: RelativeVolumeMathSettings = { sessions: 10, minSessions: 2, clock: "utc" };
+
+describe("relativeVolumeMath -- clock and slot key", () => {
+  it("auto resolves to New York for dxFeed and Databento and to UTC for Binance", () => {
+    expect(resolveRvolClock("auto", "DXFEED")).toBe("newYork");
+    expect(resolveRvolClock("auto", "DATABENTO")).toBe("newYork");
+    expect(resolveRvolClock("auto", "BINANCE")).toBe("utc");
+    expect(resolveRvolClock("newYork", "BINANCE")).toBe("newYork");
+    expect(resolveRvolClock("utc", "DXFEED")).toBe("utc");
+  });
+
+  it("keys a bar by its start minute of day with no division by bar length (hourly 09:30 keys 570, the 15:30 half hour keys 930)", () => {
+    const open = Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2, 14, 30); // 09:30 New York, EST
+    expect(slotKeyOf(open, "newYork")).toBe(570);
+    expect(slotKeyOf(open + 6 * HOUR_MS, "newYork")).toBe(930);
+    expect(slotKeyOf(open, "utc")).toBe(870);
+    expect(slotKeyOf(Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2, 0, 0), "utc")).toBe(0);
+    expect(slotKeyOf(Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2, 23, 59), "utc")).toBe(1_439);
+  });
+
+  it("agrees with the killzones New York clock through the DST change, with and without the hour cache", () => {
+    const cache = createClockCache();
+    const from = Date.UTC(2026, 2, 7, 0, 0);
+    for (let ts = from; ts < from + 3 * 24 * HOUR_MS; ts += 7 * MINUTE_MS) {
+      const expected = nyLocalMinuteOfDay(ts);
+      expect(slotKeyOf(ts, "newYork", cache)).toBe(expected);
+      expect(slotKeyOf(ts, "newYork")).toBe(expected);
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("the equity fixture's New York start-minute keys are identical before and after the DST change while the UTC keys differ", () => {
+    const starts = EQUITY_SESSION_STARTS;
+    for (const start of starts) expect(nyLocalMinuteOfDay(start)).toBe(570);
+    expect(slotKeyOf(starts[0], "newYork")).toBe(slotKeyOf(starts[5], "newYork"));
+    expect(slotKeyOf(starts[0], "utc")).toBe(870);
+    expect(slotKeyOf(starts[5], "utc")).toBe(810);
+  });
+
+  it("rejects an unusable timestamp and an unusable volume", () => {
+    expect(slotKeyOf(Number.NaN, "utc")).toBeNull();
+    expect(slotKeyOf(-1, "newYork")).toBeNull();
+    expect(usableVolume(0)).toBe(0);
+    expect(usableVolume(12.5)).toBe(12.5);
+    expect(usableVolume(-1)).toBeNull();
+    expect(usableVolume(Number.NaN)).toBeNull();
+    expect(usableVolume(Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)).toBeNull();
+    expect(usableVolume(undefined)).toBeNull();
+  });
+});
+
+describe("relativeVolumeMath -- observation rings", () => {
+  it("keeps the last N observations, reports the exact median (even count: mean of the two middle values) and the mean", () => {
+    const ring = createRing(3);
+    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 1, volume: 100 });
+    expect(ringMedian(ring)).toBe(100);
+    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 2, volume: 300 });
+    expect(ringMedian(ring)).toBe(200);
+    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 3, volume: 200 });
+    expect(ringMedian(ring)).toBe(200);
+    expect(ringMean(ring)).toBe(200);
+    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 4, volume: 900 });
+    expect(ring.items.map((item) => item.tsOpen)).toEqual([2, 3, 4]);
+    expect(ring.evictedByTail).toEqual({ tsOpen: 1, volume: 100 });
+    expect(ringMedian(ring)).toBe(300);
+    expect(ringMedian(createRing(2))).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it("a removal restores what the tail's insertion evicted; a supersede makes the eviction final", () => {
+    const ring = createRing(2);
+    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 1, volume: 10 });
+    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 2, volume: 20 });
+    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 3, volume: 30 });
+    expect(ringRemove(ring, 3)).toBe(true);
+    expect(ring.items).toEqual([{ tsOpen: 1, volume: 10 }, { tsOpen: 2, volume: 20 }]);
+    expect(ring.evictedByTail).toBeNull();
+    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 3, volume: 33 });
+    ringSupersede(ring);
+    expect(ring.evictedByTail).toBeNull();
+    expect(ringRemove(ring, 3)).toBe(true);
+    expect(ring.items).toEqual([{ tsOpen: 2, volume: 20 }]);
+    expect(ringRemove(ring, 99)).toBe(false);
+    expect(ringUpdate(ring, 2, 25)).toBe(true);
+    expect(ring.items[0].volume).toBe(25);
+    expect(ringUpdate(ring, 99, 1)).toBe(false);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("relativeVolumeMath -- hand-computed three-session fixture (success criterion 1)", () => {
+  it("median baseline, warm-up nulls and the rolling comparison take the exact hand-computed values", () => {
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
+    const bars = threeSessionBars();
+    const readings = bars.map((bar) => engine.onBar(bar));
+    // Day 1: nothing prior anywhere.
+    for (let index = 0; index < 24; index += 1) {
+      expect(readings[index].rvol).toBeNull();
+      expect(readings[index].status).toBe("learning");
+      if (index < 20) expect(readings[index].rolling).toBeNull();
+    }
+    // Day 2 slot 0 (300): one prior (100) < minSessions 2 -> learning; rolling over the 20 previous bars (all 100) = 3.
+    expect(readings[24].priorCount).toBe(1);
+    expect(readings[24].rvol).toBeNull();
+    expect(readings[24].status).toBe("learning");
+    expect(readings[24].rollingCount).toBe(20);
+    expect(readings[24].rolling).toBe(3);
+    // The first bar with 20 committed predecessors is index 20.
+    expect(readings[19].rolling).toBeNull();
+    expect(readings[20].rolling).toBe(1);
+    // Day 3 slot 0 (200): prior [100, 300], even count -> median 200 -> 1.0; rolling 2.0.
+    expect(readings[48].priorCount).toBe(2);
+    expect(readings[48].rvol).toBe(1);
+    expect(readings[48].slotMeanRatio).toBe(1);
+    expect(readings[48].rolling).toBe(2);
+    expect(readings[48].status).toBe("ready");
+    // Day 3 slot 1 (100): prior [100, 100] -> 1.0.
+    expect(readings[49].rvol).toBe(1);
+    // Day 4 slot 0 (500): prior [100, 300, 200] -> median 200 -> 2.5; rolling 5.
+    expect(readings[72].priorCount).toBe(3);
+    expect(readings[72].rvol).toBe(2.5);
+    expect(readings[72].slotMeanRatio).toBe(2.5);
+    expect(readings[72].rolling).toBe(5);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 300, 200, 500]);
+    expect(engine.rollingRing.items).toHaveLength(20);
+    for (const reading of readings) {
+      expect(reading.rvol === null || Number.isFinite(reading.rvol)).toBe(true);
+      expect(reading.rolling === null || Number.isFinite(reading.rolling)).toBe(true);
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("the forming bar grows toward its final value against the full-bar expectation and keeps its stored denominator after the close", () => {
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
+    const bars = threeSessionBars();
+    for (const bar of bars.slice(0, 72)) engine.onBar(bar);
+    const forming = (volume: number, closed: boolean) => engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, volume, closed));
+    const first = forming(100, false);
+    expect(first.rvol).toBe(0.5);
+    expect(first.status).toBe("forming");
+    expect(first.confirmed).toBe(false);
+    expect(engine.tail!.committed).toBe(false);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items).toHaveLength(3);
+    expect(forming(300, false).rvol).toBe(1.5);
+    const closed = forming(500, true);
+    expect(closed.rvol).toBe(2.5);
+    expect(closed.status).toBe("ready");
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 300, 200, 500]);
+    // A later closed dispatch with a different volume divides by the stored 200, never by a median that includes itself.
+    const corrected = forming(1_000, true);
+    expect(corrected.rvol).toBe(5);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 300, 200, 1_000]);
+    expect(engine.rollingRing.items[19].volume).toBe(1_000);
+    // Day 5 slot 0 then sees prior [100, 300, 200, 1000] -> median 250.
+    const next = engine.onBar(hourlyBar(4, 0, 500));
+    expect(next.priorCount).toBe(4);
+    expect(next.rvol).toBe(2);
+  });
+
+  it("a duplicate identical closed dispatch changes nothing", () => {
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
+    const bars = threeSessionBars();
+    for (const bar of bars.slice(0, 73)) engine.onBar(bar);
+    const before = JSON.stringify({ reading: engine.current, slot: engine.slotRing(0), rolling: engine.rollingRing });
+    engine.onBar(bars[72]);
+    expect(JSON.stringify({ reading: engine.current, slot: engine.slotRing(0), rolling: engine.rollingRing })).toBe(before);
+  });
+
+  it("a dispatch older than the current record is ignored", () => {
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
+    const bars = threeSessionBars();
+    for (const bar of bars.slice(0, 73)) engine.onBar(bar);
+    const current = engine.current;
+    expect(engine.onBar({ ...bars[10], volume: 9_999 })).toBe(current);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(600)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 100, 100]);
+  });
+
+  it("a bar superseded without ever closing contributes no observation to either ring and stays provisional (rule 7(b) withdrawn)", () => {
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
+    const bars = threeSessionBars();
+    for (const bar of bars.slice(0, 72)) engine.onBar(bar);
+    const open = engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, 400, false));
+    expect(open.confirmed).toBe(false);
+    expect(open.status).toBe("forming");
+    expect(open.rvol).toBe(2);
+    const next = engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 1, 100));
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 300, 200]);
+    expect(engine.rollingRing.items.some((item) => item.tsOpen === T0 + 3 * 24 * HOUR_MS)).toBe(false);
+    expect(engine.rollingRing.items[19]).toEqual({ tsOpen: T0 + 3 * 24 * HOUR_MS + HOUR_MS, volume: 100 });
+    // The superseded bar never entered the rolling mean either: the next bar's twenty predecessors are all 100.
+    expect(next.rolling).toBe(1);
+    // Day 5 slot 0 still sees three prior observations.
+    expect(engine.onBar(hourlyBar(4, 0, 200)).priorCount).toBe(3);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("relativeVolumeMath -- corrections and eviction rollback (amendments 1 and 2)", () => {
+  it("valid to invalid removes the observation and yields null; invalid to valid inserts it", () => {
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
+    for (const bar of threeSessionBars().slice(0, 72)) engine.onBar(bar);
+    engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, 500));
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items).toHaveLength(4);
+    const invalid = engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, Number.NaN));
+    expect(invalid.rvol).toBeNull();
+    expect(invalid.rolling).toBeNull();
+    expect(invalid.status).toBe("noVolume");
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 300, 200]);
+    expect(engine.rollingRing.items).toHaveLength(20);
+    expect(engine.rollingRing.items[19].tsOpen).toBe(T0 + 2 * 24 * HOUR_MS + 23 * HOUR_MS);
+    const valid = engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, 600));
+    expect(valid.rvol).toBe(3);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 300, 200, 600]);
+  });
+
+  it("valid, invalid, valid with both rings full restores the evicted observation and ends equal to a feed that only saw the final state", () => {
+    const settings: RelativeVolumeMathSettings = { sessions: 3, minSessions: 2, clock: "utc" };
+    const bars = threeSessionBars(); // 4 days: slot rings reach capacity 3 at day 4; the rolling ring is full after 20 bars
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(settings);
+    for (const bar of bars.slice(0, 72)) engine.onBar(bar);
+    const slotBefore = JSON.stringify(engine.slotRing(0)!.items);
+    const rollingBefore = JSON.stringify(engine.rollingRing.items);
+    engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, 500));
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([300, 200, 500]);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.evictedByTail).toEqual({ tsOpen: T0, volume: 100 });
+    expect(engine.rollingRing.evictedByTail).not.toBeNull();
+    engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, Number.NaN));
+    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.slotRing(0)!.items)).toBe(slotBefore);
+    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.rollingRing.items)).toBe(rollingBefore);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.evictedByTail).toBeNull();
+    const final = engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, 700));
+    engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 1, 100));
+    const clean = createRelativeVolumeEngine(settings);
+    for (const bar of bars.slice(0, 72)) clean.onBar(bar);
+    const cleanFinal = clean.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, 700));
+    clean.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 1, 100));
+    expect(final.rvol).toBe(cleanFinal.rvol);
+    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.slotRing(0))).toBe(JSON.stringify(clean.slotRing(0)));
+    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.rollingRing)).toBe(JSON.stringify(clean.rollingRing));
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.evictedByTail).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it("minSessions is clamped to sessions at read time, and sessions itself to 3..60", () => {
+    expect(requiredPriorOf({ sessions: 4, minSessions: 5, clock: "utc" })).toBe(4);
+    expect(requiredPriorOf({ sessions: 2, minSessions: 5, clock: "utc" })).toBe(3);
+    expect(requiredPriorOf({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "utc" })).toBe(3);
+    expect(requiredPriorOf({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 0, clock: "utc" })).toBe(1);
+    expect(ringCapacityOf({ sessions: 2, minSessions: 1, clock: "utc" })).toBe(3);
+    expect(ringCapacityOf({ sessions: 61, minSessions: 1, clock: "utc" })).toBe(60);
+    expect(createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 2, minSessions: 2, clock: "utc" }).rollingRing.capacity).toBe(20);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("relativeVolumeMath -- status precedence (rule 11)", () => {
+  const base = { tsOpen: 0, key: 0, supported: true, volume: 100, committed: true, inRings: true, confirmed: true };
+  const baseline = { slotMedian: 50, slotMean: 50, priorCount: 3, rollingMean: 50, rollingCount: 20 };
+
+  it("unsupported timeframe, then no usable volume, then learning, then no usable baseline, then forming, then ready", () => {
+    expect(readingOf({ ...base, supported: false, volume: null, baselineAtOpen: { ...baseline, priorCount: 0 } }, SETTINGS).status).toBe("unsupported");
+    expect(readingOf({ ...base, volume: null, baselineAtOpen: { ...baseline, priorCount: 0 } }, SETTINGS).status).toBe("noVolume");
+    const learning = readingOf({ ...base, baselineAtOpen: { ...baseline, priorCount: 1, slotMedian: 0 } }, SETTINGS);
+    expect(learning.status).toBe("learning");
+    expect(learning.rvol).toBeNull();
+    expect(learning.rolling).toBe(2);
+    const noBaseline = readingOf({ ...base, baselineAtOpen: { ...baseline, slotMedian: 0 } }, SETTINGS);
+    expect(noBaseline.status).toBe("noBaseline");
+    expect(noBaseline.rvol).toBeNull();
+    expect(readingOf({ ...base, confirmed: false, baselineAtOpen: baseline }, SETTINGS).status).toBe("forming");
+    const ready = readingOf({ ...base, baselineAtOpen: baseline }, SETTINGS);
+    expect(ready.status).toBe("ready");
+    expect(ready.rvol).toBe(2);
+    expect(ready.requiredPrior).toBe(2);
+  });
+
+  it("a timeframe longer than one hour yields null everywhere and files nothing", () => {
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
+    const fourHours = 4 * HOUR_MS;
+    for (let index = 0; index < 30; index += 1) {
+      const reading = engine.onBar(barFrame(T0 + index * fourHours, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: 100 }, BINANCE_PERP, fourHours));
+      expect(reading.status).toBe("unsupported");
+      expect(reading.rvol).toBeNull();
+      expect(reading.rolling).toBeNull();
+    }
+    expect(engine.slotCount).toBe(0);
+    expect(engine.rollingRing.items).toHaveLength(0);
+    const hourly = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
+    expect(hourly.onBar(hourlyBar(0, 0, 100)).status).toBe("learning");
+  });
+});
+
+describe("relativeVolumeMath -- gaps, early closes and the resampler", () => {
+  function equitySession(dayIndex: number, volumeOf: (minute: number) => number, minutes: readonly number[]): BarFrame[] {
+    const open = EQUITY_SESSION_STARTS[dayIndex];
+    return minutes.map((minute) => barFrame(open + minute * MINUTE_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: volumeOf(minute) }, DXFEED_EQUITY));
+  }
+
+  it("a session missing minutes 120..179 contributes no observations for those keys and the bar after the gap has an unchanged baseline", () => {
+    const all = Array.from({ length: 390 }, (_, minute) => minute);
+    const gapped = all.filter((minute) => minute < 120 || minute > 179);
+    const volumeOf = (minute: number) => 100 + minute;
+    const withGap = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 2, clock: "auto" });
+    const without = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 2, clock: "auto" });
+    for (const day of [0, 1]) {
+      for (const bar of equitySession(day, volumeOf, all)) {
+        withGap.onBar(bar);
+        without.onBar(bar);
+      }
+    }
+    let afterGap = null;
+    for (const bar of equitySession(2, volumeOf, gapped)) {
+      const reading = withGap.onBar(bar);
+      if (bar.ts_open === EQUITY_SESSION_STARTS[2] + 180 * MINUTE_MS) afterGap = reading;
+    }
+    let reference = null;
+    for (const bar of equitySession(2, volumeOf, all)) {
+      const reading = without.onBar(bar);
+      if (bar.ts_open === EQUITY_SESSION_STARTS[2] + 180 * MINUTE_MS) reference = reading;
+    }
+    for (let minute = 120; minute <= 179; minute += 1) {
+      expect(withGap.slotRing(570 + minute)!.items).toHaveLength(2);
+      expect(without.slotRing(570 + minute)!.items).toHaveLength(3);
+    }
+    expect(afterGap!.rvol).toBe(reference!.rvol);
+    expect(afterGap!.priorCount).toBe(2);
+    expect(withGap.slotRing(750)!.items).toHaveLength(3);
+    // The rolling ring sees the bar before the gap as the previous committed bar: no zero is ever filed.
+    expect(withGap.rollingRing.items.every((item) => item.volume > 0)).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("an early close at 13:00 enters its key as one observation and the next session's bar divides by the exact median (amendment 4)", () => {
+    // Hourly equity bars: keys 570, 630, 690, 750, 810, 870, 930. Slot 750 (12:30) is a full hour on
+    // ordinary days and a 30-minute bucket on the early-close day.
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 4, minSessions: 2, clock: "auto" });
+    const starts = EQUITY_SESSION_STARTS;
+    const hourlyVolume: Record<number, number[]> = { 750: [60, 62, 58] };
+    const feed = (day: number, hours: number, slot750: number) => {
+      for (let hour = 0; hour < hours; hour += 1) {
+        const ts = starts[day] + hour * HOUR_MS;
+        const key = 570 + hour * 60;
+        const volume = key === 750 ? slot750 : 50;
+        engine.onBar(barFrame(ts, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume }, DXFEED_EQUITY, HOUR_MS));
+      }
+    };
+    feed(0, 7, hourlyVolume[750][0]);
+    feed(1, 7, hourlyVolume[750][1]);
+    feed(2, 7, hourlyVolume[750][2]);
+    feed(3, 4, 30); // early close: the 12:30 bucket ends at 13:00 and is the session's last bar
+    expect(engine.slotRing(750)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([60, 62, 58, 30]);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(810)!.items).toHaveLength(3);
+    const reading = engine.onBar(barFrame(starts[4] + 3 * HOUR_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: 59 }, DXFEED_EQUITY, HOUR_MS));
+    // sorted [30, 58, 60, 62] -> median (58 + 60) / 2 = 59: the short bar moves the median from 60 to 59, it does not zero it.
+    expect(reading.priorCount).toBe(4);
+    expect(reading.rvol).toBe(59 / 59);
+    expect(reading.slotMeanRatio).toBe(59 / 52.5);
+  });
+
+  it("one session of 1m fixture bars through the real resampler to 1h ends on a 15:30 bar that keys 930 and is its own slot", () => {
+    const { bars } = relativeVolumeEquityFixture();
+    const hourly = resampleBars(bars.slice(0, 390), HOUR_MS);
+    expect(hourly).toHaveLength(7);
+    const last = hourly[hourly.length - 1];
+    expect(last.interval_ms).toBe(HOUR_MS); // the resampler reports the nominal interval even for the half-hour bucket
+    expect(last.closed).toBe(true);
+    expect(slotKeyOf(last.ts_open, "newYork")).toBe(930);
+    expect(hourly.map((bar) => slotKeyOf(bar.ts_open, "newYork"))).toEqual([570, 630, 690, 750, 810, 870, 930]);
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
+    for (let session = 0; session < 4; session += 1) {
+      for (const bar of resampleBars(bars.slice(session * 390, (session + 1) * 390), HOUR_MS)) engine.onBar(bar);
+    }
+    expect(engine.slotRing(930)!.items).toHaveLength(4);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(930)!.items.every((item) => slotKeyOf(item.tsOpen, "newYork") === 930)).toBe(true);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(870)!.items.every((item) => slotKeyOf(item.tsOpen, "newYork") === 870)).toBe(true);
+    expect(engine.slotCount).toBe(7);
+  });
+
+  it("the crypto fixture keeps at most 1,440 keys, every ring at or under capacity, across all 12,000 bars", () => {
+    const { bars } = relativeVolumeCryptoFixture();
+    expect(bars).toHaveLength(12_000);
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 3, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
+    let ready = 0;
+    for (const bar of bars) {
+      const reading = engine.onBar(bar);
+      if (reading.rvol !== null) ready += 1;
+    }
+    expect(engine.slotCount).toBe(1_440);
+    for (let key = 0; key < 1_440; key += 1) expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(3);
+    expect(engine.rollingRing.items).toHaveLength(20);
+    // Eight days and eight hours: the first three days learn, the rest are ready.
+    expect(ready).toBe(12_000 - 3 * 1_440);
+  });
+});

+import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { dirname } from "node:path";
+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS } from "../../../chart/candleLifecycle";
+import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
+import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/registry";
+import {
+  formatPooledReplayProbe,
+  formatReplayProbe,
+  formatRolloverProbe,
+  openLiveSession,
+  percentilesOf,
+  probeReplay,
+  probeReplayPooled,
+  probeRollover,
+  relativeVolumeCryptoFixture,
+  timedDef,
+} from "../../../chart/validation";
+import { RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, relativeVolumeIndicator } from "../relativeVolume";
+import { createRelativeVolumeEngine, ringMedian } from "../relativeVolumeMath";
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
+  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
+});
+
+/**
+ * Success criterion 4, measured the way amendment 9 freezes it: three untimed
+ * warm-up replays, five timed replays of the first 10,080 crypto bars, per-bar
+ * `onBar` plus `readOutput` samples pooled, p99 by nearest rank, no-op draw and
+ * marker namespaces (drawing is excluded). The forming-bar probe (ruling 5)
+ * warms every slot with all 10,080 bars first, then revises the next bar 500
+ * times crossing the unusual threshold on every revision, and reports its p99
+ * beside the replay p99. `probeReplay` and `probeRollover` add the protocol's
+ * real-runtime numbers. Set RVOL_PERF_OUT=<file> to keep the JSON.
+ */
+
+const P99_CEILING_MS = 0.05;
+const FORMING_REVISIONS = 500;
+
+describe("relativeVolume -- performance (success criterion 4, protocol check 6)", () => {
+  const { bars, labels } = relativeVolumeCryptoFixture();
+  const timing = bars.slice(labels.timingIndices.first, labels.timingIndices.last + 1);
+
+  it("pooled onBar plus readOutput p99 over the first 10,080 crypto bars is under 0.05 ms; the forming bar with warmed slots and threshold crossings is reported beside it", () => {
+    expect(timing).toHaveLength(10_080);
+    const pooled = probeReplayPooled(relativeVolumeIndicator, timing, RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, { warmupRuns: 3, timedRuns: 5 });
+    const replay = probeReplay(relativeVolumeIndicator, timing, RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
+    const rollover = probeRollover(relativeVolumeIndicator, timing, RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, { extraBars: 1_000 });
+
+    // Forming bar: every slot warmed on the full timing window, then the next bar revised 500 times,
+    // alternating 2.5x and 1.5x of its slot median so the marker appears and disappears on every revision.
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "utc" });
+    for (const bar of timing) engine.onBar(bar);
+    const next = bars[labels.timingIndices.last + 1];
+    const slotMedian = ringMedian(engine.slotRing(labels.minuteOfDay[labels.timingIndices.last + 1])!)!;
+    expect(engine.slotRing(labels.minuteOfDay[labels.timingIndices.last + 1])!.items).toHaveLength(7);
+    const { def: timed, samples } = timedDef(relativeVolumeIndicator);
+    const session = openLiveSession(timed, RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, {}, timing);
+    let hot = 0;
+    let formingWallMs = 0;
+    try {
+      samples.length = 0;
+      const started = performance.now();
+      for (let revision = 0; revision < FORMING_REVISIONS; revision += 1) {
+        const volume = Math.round(slotMedian * (revision % 2 === 0 ? 2.5 : 1.5));
+        session.dispatch({ bar: { ...next, volume, closed: revision === FORMING_REVISIONS - 1 } });
+        if (session.runtime.getMarkers(timed.id)!.some((call) => call.time === next.ts_open)) hot += 1;
+      }
+      formingWallMs = performance.now() - started;
+      const last = session.capture();
+      expect(last.faulted).toBe(false);
+      expect(last.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBeCloseTo(Math.round(slotMedian * 1.5) / slotMedian, 12);
+      expect(last.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).not.toBeNull();
+    } finally {
+      session.close();
+    }
+    const forming = percentilesOf(samples);
+    expect(forming.count).toBe(FORMING_REVISIONS);
+    expect(hot).toBe(FORMING_REVISIONS / 2);
+
+    const formingLine = `relativeVolume forming bar, slots warmed on ${timing.length} bars, ${FORMING_REVISIONS} revisions crossing the threshold each time (${hot} hot): onBar p50 ${forming.p50.toFixed(4)} ms p99 ${forming.p99.toFixed(4)} ms max ${forming.max.toFixed(3)} ms; wall ${formingWallMs.toFixed(0)} ms`;
+    const lines = [
+      formatPooledReplayProbe(pooled),
+      formatReplayProbe(replay),
+      `relativeVolume p99 side by side: replay (pooled) ${pooled.pooled.p99.toFixed(4)} ms, forming bar (warmed slots) ${forming.p99.toFixed(4)} ms`,
+      formingLine,
+      formatRolloverProbe(rollover),
+    ];
+    console.log(lines.join("\n"));
+    if (process.env.RVOL_PERF_OUT) {
+      mkdirSync(dirname(process.env.RVOL_PERF_OUT), { recursive: true });
+      writeFileSync(
+        process.env.RVOL_PERF_OUT,
+        `${JSON.stringify({ node: process.version, pooled, replay, rollover, forming: { warmedBars: timing.length, revisions: FORMING_REVISIONS, hotRevisions: hot, onBar: forming, wallMs: formingWallMs } }, null, 2)}\n`,
+      );
+    }
+    expect(pooled.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(pooled.pooled.count).toBe(10_080 * 5);
+    expect(pooled.pooled.p99).toBeLessThan(P99_CEILING_MS);
+    expect(replay.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(replay.onBar.p99).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.5);
+    expect(replay.wallMs).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2_000);
+    expect(forming.p99).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
+    // Bounded state: after the rollover every line holds exactly the retained bars and no marker is older than the oldest one.
+    expect(rollover.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(rollover.retainedBars).toBe(MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS);
+    expect(rollover.outputLengths).toEqual({ rvol: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS, rolling: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS });
+    expect(rollover.markersBeforeRetention).toBe(0);
+  }, 600_000);
+});

+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../types/frames";
+import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
+import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/registry";
+import { replayBars } from "../../../chart/replayBars";
+import {
+  checkArrivalsAgainstReplay,
+  checkDuplicateClosedIdempotent,
+  checkLiveReplayEquivalence,
+  checkRebuiltEquivalence,
+  checkTruncationInvariance,
+  BINANCE_PERP,
+  barFrame,
+  compressionBreakoutBars,
+  countMarkers,
+  countMarkersOver,
+  diffOutputs,
+  driveLive,
+  duplicateClosedArrivals,
+  formingRevisions,
+  liveArrivals,
+  openLiveSession,
+  relativeVolumeCryptoFixture,
+  relativeVolumeEquityFixture,
+  replayCapture,
+  sampledIndices,
+  summarizeEquivalence,
+  summarizeTruncation,
+  timeGapBars,
+  trendBars,
+  type Arrival,
+} from "../../../chart/validation";
+import { RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, relativeVolumeIndicator, type RelativeVolumeSettings } from "../relativeVolume";
+import { RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH, createRelativeVolumeEngine, slotKeyOf } from "../relativeVolumeMath";
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
+  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
+});
+
+/** The equivalence checks compare the rolling line too, so it is switched on. */
+const SETTINGS: RelativeVolumeSettings = { ...RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, showRolling: true };
+const EQUITY = relativeVolumeEquityFixture();
+const CRYPTO = relativeVolumeCryptoFixture();
+
+function noNaN(outputs: Readonly<Record<string, readonly (number | null)[]>>): void {
+  for (const values of Object.values(outputs)) {
+    for (const value of values) expect(value === null || Number.isFinite(value)).toBe(true);
+  }
+}
+
+describe("relativeVolume -- no future data: truncation invariance (protocol check 1)", () => {
+  it("passes on a sampled sweep of the equity fixture, numbers and confirmed markers", () => {
+    const report = checkTruncationInvariance(relativeVolumeIndicator, EQUITY.bars, SETTINGS, { indices: sampledIndices(EQUITY.bars.length, 97) });
+    console.log(summarizeTruncation(report));
+    expect(report.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(report.violations).toEqual([]);
+    expect(report.markerViolations).toEqual([]);
+    expect(report.ok).toBe(true);
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("passes at every cut of compressionBreakoutBars and trendBars", () => {
+    for (const { bars } of [compressionBreakoutBars(), trendBars({ count: 600 })]) {
+      const report = checkTruncationInvariance(relativeVolumeIndicator, bars, SETTINGS);
+      expect(report.checked).toBe(bars.length);
+      expect(report.ok).toBe(true);
+    }
+  }, 120_000);
+});
+
+describe("relativeVolume -- replay versus live equivalence (protocol check 2)", () => {
+  it("ends on the same committed values and markers whether fed live with forming revisions or replayed", () => {
+    const report = checkLiveReplayEquivalence(relativeVolumeIndicator, EQUITY.bars, SETTINGS, { revisionsPerBar: 5 });
+    console.log(summarizeEquivalence("relativeVolume equity fixture", report));
+    expect(report.mutations.filter((mutation) => mutation === "REPLACED_EDGE")).toHaveLength(EQUITY.bars.length * 5);
+    expect(report.outputsEqual).toBe(true);
+    expect(report.markersEqual).toBe(true);
+    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+    expect(report.live.markers.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    noNaN(report.live.outputs);
+    noNaN(report.replay.outputs);
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("a bar whose close never arrived contributes nothing: every other bar equals a replay without it, its own reading and marker stay provisional (rule 7(b) withdrawn)", () => {
+    // Every anomaly bar loses its closed dispatch: the forming revisions arrive, then the next bar.
+    const anomalyIndices = new Set(EQUITY.labels.anomalyIndices);
+    const arrivals: Arrival[] = [];
+    EQUITY.bars.forEach((bar, index) => {
+      const frames = formingRevisions(bar, 3);
+      if (anomalyIndices.has(index)) frames.pop();
+      for (const frame of frames) arrivals.push({ bar: frame });
+    });
+    const live = driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, arrivals, SETTINGS);
+    const without = EQUITY.bars.filter((_, index) => !anomalyIndices.has(index));
+    const replayWithout = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, without, SETTINGS);
+    // Outputs of the bars that did close equal a replay in which the never-closed bars do not exist at all.
+    let cursor = 0;
+    EQUITY.bars.forEach((_, index) => {
+      if (anomalyIndices.has(index)) {
+        expect(live.outputs.rvol[index]).not.toBeNull();
+        return;
+      }
+      expect(live.outputs.rvol[index]).toBe(replayWithout.outputs.rvol[cursor]);
+      expect(live.outputs.rolling[index]).toBe(replayWithout.outputs.rolling[cursor]);
+      cursor += 1;
+    });
+    expect(cursor).toBe(without.length);
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
+    for (const arrival of arrivals) engine.onBar(arrival.bar);
+    for (const index of anomalyIndices) {
+      const bar = EQUITY.bars[index];
+      expect(engine.slotRing(slotKeyOf(bar.ts_open, "newYork")!)!.items.some((item) => item.tsOpen === bar.ts_open)).toBe(false);
+      expect(engine.rollingRing.items.some((item) => item.tsOpen === bar.ts_open)).toBe(false);
+    }
+    const provisional = live.markers.filter((call) => call.color.startsWith("rgba("));
+    const confirmed = live.markers.filter((call) => !call.color.startsWith("rgba("));
+    expect(provisional.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    for (const call of provisional) expect(anomalyIndices.has(EQUITY.bars.findIndex((bar) => bar.ts_open === call.time))).toBe(true);
+    for (const call of confirmed) expect(anomalyIndices.has(EQUITY.bars.findIndex((bar) => bar.ts_open === call.time))).toBe(false);
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("a replay whose tail is a forming bar keeps the tail pending and provisional", () => {
+    const bars = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 5 * 390 + 100);
+    const tailOpen = { ...bars[bars.length - 1], closed: false, volume: Math.round(bars[bars.length - 1].volume / 2) } as BarFrame;
+    const withTail = [...bars.slice(0, -1), tailOpen];
+    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, withTail, SETTINGS);
+    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
+    for (const bar of withTail) engine.onBar(bar);
+    expect(engine.tail!.committed).toBe(false);
+    expect(engine.tail!.confirmed).toBe(false);
+    expect(engine.current!.status).toBe("forming");
+    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[withTail.length - 1]).toBe(engine.current!.rvol);
+    expect(engine.current!.rvol).not.toBeNull();
+    noNaN(capture.outputs);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("relativeVolume -- gap, duplicate and late-event behavior (protocol check 3)", () => {
+  const session5 = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 4 * 390 + 60);
+
+  it("duplicate closed bar is idempotent", () => {
+    const report = checkDuplicateClosedIdempotent(relativeVolumeIndicator, session5, SETTINGS, session5.length - 20);
+    expect(report.duplicateMutation).toBe("REPLACED_EDGE");
+    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+    const budget = countMarkersOver(relativeVolumeIndicator, duplicateClosedArrivals(EQUITY.bars, EQUITY.labels.anomalyIndices[0]).arrivals, SETTINGS);
+    expect(budget.total).toBe(countMarkers(relativeVolumeIndicator, EQUITY.bars, SETTINGS).total);
+  }, 60_000);
+
+  it("late bar rebuilds to the replay", () => {
+    const report = checkRebuiltEquivalence(relativeVolumeIndicator, session5, SETTINGS, 4 * 390 + 10);
+    expect(report.sawRebuilt).toBe(true);
+    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+    expect(report.live.outputs.rvol).toHaveLength(session5.length);
+  }, 60_000);
+
+  it("a correction to an older bar arrives as a REBUILT and every successor equals a fresh replay (amendment 1)", () => {
+    const corrected = 3 * 390 + 200;
+    const arrivals: Arrival[] = session5.map((bar) => ({ bar }));
+    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...session5[corrected], volume: session5[corrected].volume * 4 }, label: "correction" });
+    const report = checkArrivalsAgainstReplay(relativeVolumeIndicator, arrivals, SETTINGS);
+    expect(report.mutations[report.mutations.length - 1]).toBe("REBUILT");
+    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+    const correctedSeries = session5.map((bar, index) => (index === corrected ? { ...bar, volume: bar.volume * 4 } : bar));
+    const fresh = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, correctedSeries, SETTINGS);
+    expect(diffOutputs(report.live.outputs, fresh.outputs)).toEqual([]);
+    expect(diffOutputs(replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, session5, SETTINGS).outputs, fresh.outputs).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+  }, 60_000);
+
+  it("changed-volume closed replacement: both rings take the new volume, the ratio uses the stored denominator, the marker follows, and later bars match a replay that only saw the final volume", () => {
+    const index = 4 * 390 + 30;
+    const bar = session5[index];
+    const prefix = session5.slice(0, index);
+    const base = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
+    for (const earlier of prefix) base.onBar(earlier);
+    const denominator = base.onBar({ ...bar, volume: 100 }).rvol;
+    expect(denominator).not.toBeNull();
+    const median = 100 / denominator!;
+    const hotVolume = Math.ceil(median * 2.2);
+    const arrivals: Arrival[] = prefix.map((earlier) => ({ bar: earlier }));
+    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: 100 } });
+    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: hotVolume }, label: "closed again" });
+    for (const later of session5.slice(index + 1)) arrivals.push({ bar: later });
+    const report = checkArrivalsAgainstReplay(relativeVolumeIndicator, arrivals, SETTINGS);
+    expect(report.mutations[index + 1]).toBe("REPLACED_EDGE");
+    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+    expect(report.live.outputs.rvol[index]).toBeCloseTo(hotVolume / median, 12);
+    expect(report.live.markers.some((call) => call.time === bar.ts_open)).toBe(true);
+    // Back below the threshold on a third close: the marker disappears and the ratio still uses the stored median.
+    const back = driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, [...arrivals.slice(0, index + 2), { bar: { ...bar, volume: 100 } }], SETTINGS);
+    expect(back.outputs.rvol[index]).toBe(denominator);
+    expect(back.markers.some((call) => call.time === bar.ts_open)).toBe(false);
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
+    for (const earlier of prefix) engine.onBar(earlier);
+    engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: 100 });
+    engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: hotVolume });
+    const key = slotKeyOf(bar.ts_open, "newYork")!;
+    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items[engine.slotRing(key)!.items.length - 1]).toEqual({ tsOpen: bar.ts_open, volume: hotVolume });
+    expect(engine.rollingRing.items[RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH - 1]).toEqual({ tsOpen: bar.ts_open, volume: hotVolume });
+    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items.filter((item) => item.tsOpen === bar.ts_open)).toHaveLength(1);
+  }, 60_000);
+
+  it("valid to invalid to valid with both rings full (14 prior per slot) equals a replay that saw only the final state (amendment 2)", () => {
+    const index = 14 * 390 + 45; // session 15: every slot already holds 10 observations, the rolling ring 20
+    const bar = EQUITY.bars[index];
+    const prefix = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, index);
+    const arrivals: Arrival[] = prefix.map((earlier) => ({ bar: earlier }));
+    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: bar.volume + 1 } });
+    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: Number.NaN }, label: "invalid" });
+    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: bar.volume * 3 }, label: "valid again" });
+    for (const later of EQUITY.bars.slice(index + 1, index + 40)) arrivals.push({ bar: later });
+    const report = checkArrivalsAgainstReplay(relativeVolumeIndicator, arrivals, SETTINGS);
+    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
+    for (const earlier of prefix) engine.onBar(earlier);
+    const key = slotKeyOf(bar.ts_open, "newYork")!;
+    const slotBefore = JSON.stringify(engine.slotRing(key)!.items);
+    const rollingBefore = JSON.stringify(engine.rollingRing.items);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items).toHaveLength(10);
+    engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: bar.volume + 1 });
+    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.evictedByTail).not.toBeNull();
+    const invalid = engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: Number.NaN });
+    expect(invalid.rvol).toBeNull();
+    expect(invalid.status).toBe("noVolume");
+    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.slotRing(key)!.items)).toBe(slotBefore);
+    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.rollingRing.items)).toBe(rollingBefore);
+    const valid = engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: bar.volume * 3 });
+    expect(valid.rvol).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items).toHaveLength(10);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items[9]).toEqual({ tsOpen: bar.ts_open, volume: bar.volume * 3 });
+  }, 60_000);
+
+  it("time gap: missing minutes file nothing, nothing before the gap changes, the rolling comparison carries across", () => {
+    const { bars, labels } = timeGapBars({ count: 600 });
+    const gapped = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, bars, SETTINGS);
+    const prefix = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, bars.slice(0, labels.gapAfterIndex + 1), SETTINGS);
+    expect(diffOutputs(gapped.outputs, prefix.outputs, 0, labels.gapAfterIndex)).toEqual([]);
+    const after = labels.firstBarAfterGapIndex;
+    const previous = bars.slice(after - RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH, after);
+    const mean = previous.reduce((sum, bar) => sum + bar.volume, 0) / RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH;
+    expect(gapped.outputs.rolling[after]).toBeCloseTo(bars[after].volume / mean, 9);
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
+    for (const bar of bars) engine.onBar(bar);
+    for (let missing = 1; missing <= labels.missingBars; missing += 1) {
+      const key = slotKeyOf(bars[labels.gapAfterIndex].ts_open + missing * 60_000, "utc")!;
+      expect(engine.slotRing(key)).toBeUndefined();
+    }
+    noNaN(gapped.outputs);
+  });
+});

+describe("relativeVolume -- warm-up, empty input and NaN (protocol check 4)", () => {
+  it("warm-up is null, never NaN or zero: the first ratio appears when a slot has minSessions prior observations", () => {
+    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 4 * 390), SETTINGS);
+    for (let index = 0; index < 3 * 390; index += 1) expect(capture.outputs.rvol[index]).toBeNull();
+    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[3 * 390]).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[3 * 390]).not.toBe(0);
+    for (let index = 0; index < RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH; index += 1) expect(capture.outputs.rolling[index]).toBeNull();
+    expect(capture.outputs.rolling[RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH]).not.toBeNull();
+    noNaN(capture.outputs);
+    const shorter = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 3 * 390), SETTINGS);
+    expect(shorter.outputs.rvol.every((value) => value === null)).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("handles an empty bar array", () => {
+    const { perBar } = replayBars(relativeVolumeIndicator, [], RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
+    expect(perBar.rvol).toEqual([]);
+    expect(perBar.rolling).toEqual([]);
+    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, [], RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
+    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it("a bar with no usable volume yields null and never poisons later bars", () => {
+    const bars = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 4 * 390 + 5).map((bar, index) => (index === 4 * 390 + 2 ? ({ ...bar, volume: Number.NaN } as BarFrame) : bar));
+    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, bars, SETTINGS);
+    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[4 * 390 + 2]).toBeNull();
+    expect(capture.outputs.rolling[4 * 390 + 2]).toBeNull();
+    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[4 * 390 + 3]).not.toBeNull();
+    noNaN(capture.outputs);
+  });
+
+  it("the rolling line is off by default: its output is null while the table value is still computed", () => {
+    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 100), RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
+    expect(capture.outputs.rolling.every((value) => value === null)).toBe(true);
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
+    let last = null;
+    for (const bar of EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 100)) last = engine.onBar(bar);
+    expect(last!.rolling).not.toBeNull();
+  });
+});

+describe("relativeVolume -- rollover and reconnect (ruling 2): the latest ratio can change when a replay holds fewer prior observations than live state did", () => {
+  /** Eleven days of one-minute crypto: 100 per minute for five days, then 300, the last bar 450. */
+  function elevenDays(): BarFrame[] {
+    const t0 = Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2);
+    return Array.from({ length: 11 * 1_440 }, (_, index) =>
+      barFrame(t0 + index * 60_000, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: index === 11 * 1_440 - 1 ? 450 : index < 5 * 1_440 ? 100 : 300 }, BINANCE_PERP),
+    );
+  }
+
+  it("rollover and reconnect: live 2.25x against ten prior; a replay of the retained seven days and an older duplicate (REBUILT) both read 1.5x; a settings update that changes no recompute key changes nothing", () => {
+    const bars = elevenDays();
+    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, []);
+    try {
+      for (const bar of bars) session.dispatch({ bar });
+      expect(session.bars).toHaveLength(10_080);
+      const live = session.capture();
+      const lastOpen = bars[bars.length - 1].ts_open;
+      expect(live.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(2.25);
+      expect(live.markers.some((call) => call.time === lastOpen && !call.color.startsWith("rgba("))).toBe(true);
+      // A reconnect replays the retained bars: six prior observations at 300 for the last slot, median 300.
+      const retained = [...session.bars];
+      const replay = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, retained, SETTINGS);
+      expect(replay.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(1.5);
+      expect(replay.markers.some((call) => call.time === lastOpen)).toBe(false);
+      // An update that changes no recompute key must not replay: every output and marker stays.
+      session.updateSettings({ ...SETTINGS });
+      expect(session.capture().outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(2.25);
+      expect(session.capture().markers).toEqual(live.markers);
+      // An identical closed duplicate of an older retained bar is a REBUILT and replays the retained bars.
+      session.dispatch({ bar: { ...retained[100] } });
+      expect(session.mutations.at(-1)).toBe("REBUILT");
+      expect(session.capture().outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(1.5);
+      expect(session.capture().markers.some((call) => call.time === lastOpen)).toBe(false);
+    } finally {
+      session.close();
+    }
+  }, 120_000);
+});
+
+describe("relativeVolume -- crypto fixture: rollover and REBUILT equivalence at sessions = 3 (amendment 7)", () => {
+  const settings: RelativeVolumeSettings = { ...SETTINGS, sessions: 3, minSessions: 3 };
+
+  it("a REBUILT replay over the retained 10,080 bars reproduces live outputs for every bar whose full lookback lies within them", () => {
+    const retained = CRYPTO.bars.slice(CRYPTO.labels.rebuiltFromIndex);
+    expect(retained).toHaveLength(10_080);
+    const live = driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, CRYPTO.bars.map((bar) => ({ bar })), settings);
+    expect(live.bars).toHaveLength(10_080);
+    expect(live.bars[0].ts_open).toBe(retained[0].ts_open);
+    const replay = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, retained, settings);
+    const priorByKey = new Map<number, number>();
+    let compared = 0;
+    let withheld = 0;
+    retained.forEach((bar, index) => {
+      const key = slotKeyOf(bar.ts_open, "utc")!;
+      const prior = priorByKey.get(key) ?? 0;
+      priorByKey.set(key, prior + 1);
+      if (prior >= 3 && index >= RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH) {
+        compared += 1;
+        expect(replay.outputs.rvol[index]).toBe(live.outputs.rvol[index]);
+        expect(replay.outputs.rolling[index]).toBe(live.outputs.rolling[index]);
+        expect(live.outputs.rvol[index]).not.toBeNull();
+      } else if (replay.outputs.rvol[index] !== live.outputs.rvol[index]) {
+        withheld += 1;
+      }
+    });
+    console.log(`crypto REBUILT equivalence: ${compared} bars compared equal, ${withheld} early retained bars differ because live state kept observations from discarded bars`);
+    expect(compared).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(compared).toBe(10_080 - 3 * 1_440);
+    expect(withheld).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    noNaN(live.outputs);
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("the runtime's own REBUILT path on the retained bars equals a clean replay, with a non-zero count of compared bars", () => {
+    const retained = CRYPTO.bars.slice(CRYPTO.labels.rebuiltFromIndex);
+    const report = checkRebuiltEquivalence(relativeVolumeIndicator, retained, settings, 5_000);
+    expect(report.sawRebuilt).toBe(true);
+    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+    const compared = report.live.outputs.rvol.filter((value) => value !== null).length;
+    expect(compared).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("live versus replay equivalence holds on the first 10,080 crypto bars with forming revisions", () => {
+    const report = checkLiveReplayEquivalence(relativeVolumeIndicator, CRYPTO.bars.slice(0, 10_080), settings, { revisionsPerBar: 2 });
+    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+    expect(liveArrivals(CRYPTO.bars.slice(0, 2), 2)).toHaveLength(6);
+  }, 120_000);
+});

+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
+import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../../chart/draw/registry";
+import { countMarkers, judgeBudget, relativeVolumeEquityFixture, replayCapture, summarizeBudget } from "../../../../chart/validation";
+import { RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, relativeVolumeIndicator } from "../../relativeVolume";
+import { referenceSeries, type ReferenceRow } from "./reference";
+
+// Validation loop 1, check 7 and Codex item 7: the baseline comparison recounted
+// from the validator's batch reference (not from the engine under test), with
+// the published counts pinned exactly and the false-positive rate divided by
+// eligible non-anomaly bars as the code gate required.
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
+  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
+});
+
+const THRESHOLD = 2;
+type Method = "timeOfDay" | "sameSlotMean" | "rolling";
+
+interface Count {
+  eligible: number;
+  detections: number;
+  anomaliesHit: number;
+  falsePositives: number;
+  predictableEligible: number;
+  predictableFalsePositives: number;
+}
+
+function ratioFor(row: ReferenceRow, method: Method): number | null {
+  return method === "timeOfDay" ? row.rvol : method === "sameSlotMean" ? row.slotMeanRatio : row.rolling;
+}
+
+function recount(fixture: ReturnType<typeof relativeVolumeEquityFixture>): Record<Method, Count> {
+  const rows = referenceSeries(fixture.bars, { sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "newYork" });
+  const anomalies = new Set(fixture.labels.anomalyIndices);
+  const predictable = new Set(fixture.labels.predictableSlotIndices);
+  const counts: Record<Method, Count> = {
+    timeOfDay: { eligible: 0, detections: 0, anomaliesHit: 0, falsePositives: 0, predictableEligible: 0, predictableFalsePositives: 0 },
+    sameSlotMean: { eligible: 0, detections: 0, anomaliesHit: 0, falsePositives: 0, predictableEligible: 0, predictableFalsePositives: 0 },
+    rolling: { eligible: 0, detections: 0, anomaliesHit: 0, falsePositives: 0, predictableEligible: 0, predictableFalsePositives: 0 },
+  };
+  for (const index of fixture.labels.evaluationIndices) {
+    const row = rows[index];
+    if (row.rvol === null || row.slotMeanRatio === null || row.rolling === null) continue;
+    for (const method of ["timeOfDay", "sameSlotMean", "rolling"] as const) {
+      const count = counts[method];
+      const detected = ratioFor(row, method)! >= THRESHOLD;
+      count.eligible += 1;
+      if (predictable.has(index)) count.predictableEligible += 1;
+      if (!detected) continue;
+      count.detections += 1;
+      if (anomalies.has(index)) count.anomaliesHit += 1;
+      else {
+        count.falsePositives += 1;
+        if (predictable.has(index)) count.predictableFalsePositives += 1;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return counts;
+}
+
+/** The code gate's denominator: eligible non-anomaly bars, not eligible minus hits. */
+function falsePositiveRate(count: Count, anomaliesInEligible: number): number {
+  return count.falsePositives / (count.eligible - anomaliesInEligible);
+}
+
+describe("baseline comparison recounted from the validator's reference", () => {
+  const fixture = relativeVolumeEquityFixture();
+  const counts = recount(fixture);
+
+  it("pins the published equity-fixture table: 13/12/1 time of day, 12/12/0 same-slot mean, 32/12/20 rolling with 19 predictable-slot false positives", () => {
+    expect(counts.timeOfDay).toEqual({ eligible: 4_680, detections: 13, anomaliesHit: 12, falsePositives: 1, predictableEligible: 240, predictableFalsePositives: 0 });
+    expect(counts.sameSlotMean).toEqual({ eligible: 4_680, detections: 12, anomaliesHit: 12, falsePositives: 0, predictableEligible: 240, predictableFalsePositives: 0 });
+    expect(counts.rolling).toEqual({ eligible: 4_680, detections: 32, anomaliesHit: 12, falsePositives: 20, predictableEligible: 240, predictableFalsePositives: 19 });
+  });
+
+  it("meets the brief's acceptance bounds", () => {
+    expect(counts.timeOfDay.anomaliesHit).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(11);
+    expect(counts.timeOfDay.anomaliesHit).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(counts.rolling.anomaliesHit - 1);
+    const todPredictable = counts.timeOfDay.predictableFalsePositives / counts.timeOfDay.predictableEligible;
+    const rollingPredictable = counts.rolling.predictableFalsePositives / counts.rolling.predictableEligible;
+    expect(todPredictable).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.1);
+    expect(todPredictable).toBeLessThanOrEqual(rollingPredictable / 2);
+    expect(rollingPredictable).toBeCloseTo(19 / 240, 12);
+    // Overall false-positive rates on eligible non-anomaly bars (4,668).
+    expect(falsePositiveRate(counts.timeOfDay, 12)).toBeCloseTo(1 / 4_668, 12);
+    expect(falsePositiveRate(counts.rolling, 12)).toBeCloseTo(20 / 4_668, 12);
+  });
+
+  it("the indicator's own markers on the fixture are exactly the 13 time-of-day detections", () => {
+    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, fixture.bars, RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
+    const rows = referenceSeries(fixture.bars, { sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "newYork" });
+    const detections = fixture.bars.map((bar, index) => ({ bar, index })).filter(({ index }) => rows[index].rvol !== null && rows[index].rvol! >= THRESHOLD);
+    expect(detections).toHaveLength(13);
+    expect(capture.markers.map((call) => call.time).sort((a, b) => a - b)).toEqual(detections.map(({ bar }) => bar.ts_open));
+    for (const anomaly of fixture.labels.anomalyIndices) expect(capture.markers.some((call) => call.time === fixture.bars[anomaly].ts_open)).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("pins the control experiment: flat seasonality makes the three methods agree except for the time-of-day method's one noise bar", () => {
+    const control = recount(relativeVolumeEquityFixture({ flatSeasonality: true }));
+    expect(control.timeOfDay).toEqual({ eligible: 4_680, detections: 13, anomaliesHit: 12, falsePositives: 1, predictableEligible: 240, predictableFalsePositives: 0 });
+    expect(control.sameSlotMean).toEqual({ eligible: 4_680, detections: 12, anomaliesHit: 12, falsePositives: 0, predictableEligible: 240, predictableFalsePositives: 0 });
+    expect(control.rolling).toEqual({ eligible: 4_680, detections: 12, anomaliesHit: 12, falsePositives: 0, predictableEligible: 240, predictableFalsePositives: 0 });
+  });
+
+  it("marker budget on the frozen fixture: 13 over 4,680 evaluated bars, 0.28 per 100, 2.8 per 1,000, with non-zero eligible outputs", () => {
+    const budget = countMarkers(relativeVolumeIndicator, fixture.bars, RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
+    const eligible = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, fixture.bars, RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS).outputs.rvol.filter((value) => value !== null).length;
+    expect(eligible).toBe(4_680);
+    expect(budget.total).toBe(13);
+    expect((budget.total / fixture.labels.evaluationIndices.length) * 100).toBeCloseTo(0.2778, 3);
+    expect(budget.maxPerBar).toBe(1);
+    expect(judgeBudget(budget, 40, 3).ok).toBe(true);
+    console.log(summarizeBudget("relativeVolume frozen equity fixture", budget, judgeBudget(budget, 40, 3)));
+  });
+});

+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../../types/frames";
+import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
+import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../../chart/draw/registry";
+import { mergeChartBar } from "../../../../chart/chartData";
+import {
+  BINANCE_PERP,
+  barFrame,
+  countMarkers,
+  judgeBudget,
+  openLiveSession,
+  percentilesOf,

+import { medianOf } from "./reference";

+// Validation loop 1: the validator's own reproductions of Codex's G3 items
+// (PM/log/validation/relativeVolume/codex-g3-round1.md), each judged against
+// the PM's ruling. Items the ruling accepts as behaviour are pinned as such;
+// items the builder must fix are in rules.test.ts under "amended brief".

+afterEach(() => {
+  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
+  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
+});

+const SETTINGS: RelativeVolumeSettings = { ...RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, showRolling: true };
+const T0 = Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2);
+
+function minuteBars(count: number, volumeAt: (index: number) => number): BarFrame[] {
+  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => barFrame(T0 + index * MINUTE_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: volumeAt(index) }, BINANCE_PERP));
+}

+/** Codex items 1 and 2: eleven days of one-minute crypto, 100 then 300 per minute, last bar 450. */
+const ELEVEN_DAYS = minuteBars(11 * 1_440, (index) => (index === 11 * 1_440 - 1 ? 450 : index < 5 * 1_440 ? 100 : 300));
+
+describe("Codex item 1: a colour change replays and can change a number (reproduced on ce8d11c; ruling: builder must fix, applied in fix round 1: the pickers are gone and a settings update that changes no recompute key never replays)", () => {
+  it("a settings update carrying only a stale colour key leaves the latest ratio at 2.25x and keeps its confirmed marker", () => {
+    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, []);
+    try {
+      for (const bar of ELEVEN_DAYS) session.dispatch({ bar });
+      expect(session.bars).toHaveLength(10_080);
+      const before = session.capture();
+      expect(before.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(2.25);
+      expect(before.markers.some((call) => call.time === ELEVEN_DAYS[ELEVEN_DAYS.length - 1].ts_open)).toBe(true);
+      session.updateSettings({ ...SETTINGS, rollingColor: "#123456" });
+      const after = session.capture();
+      // Live state holds ten prior observations for the slot (five at 100, five at 300: median 200); a replay over
+      // the retained seven days would hold six (median 300) and read 1.5x. On ce8d11c this update replayed; it no longer does.
+      expect(after.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(2.25);
+      expect(after.outputs).toEqual(before.outputs);
+      expect(after.markers).toEqual(before.markers);
+    } finally {
+      session.close();
+    }
+  }, 120_000);
+});
+
+describe("Codex item 2: default lookback 10 exceeds one-minute crypto retention (reproduced; ruling: accepted, document, add this test)", () => {
+  it("after eleven days live, a replay of the retained bars and an unchanged older duplicate (REBUILT) both change the latest ratio", () => {
+    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, []);
+    try {
+      for (const bar of ELEVEN_DAYS) session.dispatch({ bar });
+      const live = session.capture();
+      const retained = [...session.bars];
+      const replay = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, retained, SETTINGS);
+      expect(live.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(2.25);
+      expect(replay.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(1.5);
+      // Prior observations within the retained seven days for the last slot: days 5..10 only.
+      const slotVolumes = retained.filter((bar, index) => index < retained.length - 1 && bar.ts_open % (24 * HOUR_MS) === retained[retained.length - 1].ts_open % (24 * HOUR_MS)).map((bar) => bar.volume);
+      expect(slotVolumes).toHaveLength(6);
+      expect(450 / medianOf(slotVolumes)).toBe(1.5);
+      // An identical closed duplicate of an older retained bar is classified REBUILT and replays.
+      const older = retained[100];

+      expect(mergeChartBar(probe, { ...older })).toBe("REBUILT");
+      session.dispatch({ bar: { ...older } });
+      expect(session.mutations.at(-1)).toBe("REBUILT");
+      expect(session.capture().outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(1.5);
+    } finally {
+      session.close();
+    }
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("the doc's claim that ten fits one-minute crypto is false: 10,080 retained minutes hold at most six prior observations per minute of day", () => {
+    expect(Math.floor(10_080 / 1_440)).toBe(7);

+    const fixture = relativeVolumeCryptoFixture();
+    const retained = fixture.bars.slice(fixture.labels.rebuiltFromIndex);
+    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, retained, SETTINGS);
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "utc" });
+    let maxPrior = 0;

+    expect(capture.outputs.rvol.filter((value) => value !== null).length).toBe(10_080 - 3 * 1_440);
+  });
+});

+  });

+      expect(capture.outputs.rvol[index]).toBe(reference[index].rvol);
+      expect(capture.outputs.rolling[index]).toBe(reference[index].rolling);
+      if (reference[index].rvol !== null) ready += 1;

+    expect(ready).toBe(12 * 390);
+  });

+    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, CRYPTO.bars, SETTINGS);

+  });

+    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, EQUITY.bars, SETTINGS);

+    EQUITY.bars.forEach((bar, index) => {
+      const key = nyLocalMinuteOfDay(bar.ts_open);
+      const earlier = (seen.get(key) ?? []).slice(-10);
+      const rvol = capture.outputs.rvol[index];
+      if (rvol !== null) {
+        expect(earlier.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
+        expect(rvol * medianOf(earlier)).toBeCloseTo(bar.volume, 9);

+    });
+  });

+    let previousKey: number | null = null;

+        jumps += 1;

+        expect(key).toBe(180);
+        expect(bar.ts_open).toBe(Date.UTC(2026, 2, 8, 7, 0));
+      }
+      previousKey = key;
+    }
+    expect(jumps).toBe(1);

+      expect(grid["0,0"].text).toMatch(/^RVOL \d+\.\d× · 10 prior$/);
+    } finally {
+      session.close();
+    }
+  });
+});

+    expect(report.checked).toBe(indices.length);
+    expect(report.ok).toBe(true);
+  }, 300_000);
+});

+import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../../types/frames";
+import { nyLocalMinuteOfDay } from "../../../killzones/killzones";
+
+/**
+ * Validator's from-scratch batch reference for Relative Volume (Time of Day).
+ * Written from the brief alone, not from `relativeVolumeMath.ts`: every bar is
+ * keyed by its start minute of day, its denominator is the median of the last
+ * `sessions` volumes filed under that key by STRICTLY EARLIER closed bars, the
+ * rolling comparison is the mean of the previous 20 closed bars, and nothing
+ * is incremental. Agreement with the incremental engine on the frozen fixtures
+ * is the independent evidence for the stored denominator and the clock.
+ */
+
+export interface ReferenceSettings {
+  sessions: number;
+  minSessions: number;
+  clock: "newYork" | "utc";
+}
+
+export interface ReferenceRow {
+  key: number;
+  priorCount: number;
+  slotMedian: number | null;
+  slotMean: number | null;
+  rollingMean: number | null;
+  rvol: number | null;
+  slotMeanRatio: number | null;
+  rolling: number | null;
+}
+
+export function referenceKey(tsOpen: number, clock: "newYork" | "utc"): number {
+  return clock === "utc" ? Math.floor((tsOpen % 86_400_000) / 60_000) : nyLocalMinuteOfDay(tsOpen);
+}
+
+export function medianOf(values: readonly number[]): number {
+  const sorted = [...values].sort((a, b) => a - b);
+  const middle = sorted.length >> 1;
+  return sorted.length % 2 === 1 ? sorted[middle] : (sorted[middle - 1] + sorted[middle]) / 2;
+}
+
+function meanOf(values: readonly number[]): number {
+  let sum = 0;
+  for (const value of values) sum += value;
+  return sum / values.length;
+}
+
+function ratio(volume: number, denominator: number | null): number | null {
+  if (denominator === null || !(denominator > 0)) return null;
+  const value = volume / denominator;
+  return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
+}
+
+/** Every bar is treated as closed; a bar with an unusable volume reads null and files nothing. */
+export function referenceSeries(bars: readonly BarFrame[], settings: ReferenceSettings): ReferenceRow[] {
+  const bySlot = new Map<number, number[]>();
+  const committed: number[] = [];
+  const rows: ReferenceRow[] = [];
+  const required = Math.max(1, Math.min(settings.minSessions, settings.sessions));
+  for (const bar of bars) {
+    const key = referenceKey(bar.ts_open, settings.clock);
+    const prior = (bySlot.get(key) ?? []).slice(-settings.sessions);
+    const slotMedian = prior.length > 0 ? medianOf(prior) : null;
+    const slotMean = prior.length > 0 ? meanOf(prior) : null;
+    const previous = committed.slice(-20);
+    const rollingMean = previous.length === 20 ? meanOf(previous) : null;
+    const usable = Number.isFinite(bar.volume) && bar.volume >= 0;
+    const row: ReferenceRow = {
+      key,
+      priorCount: prior.length,
+      slotMedian,
+      slotMean,
+      rollingMean,
+      rvol: usable && prior.length >= required ? ratio(bar.volume, slotMedian) : null,
+      slotMeanRatio: usable && prior.length >= required ? ratio(bar.volume, slotMean) : null,
+      rolling: usable ? ratio(bar.volume, rollingMean) : null,
+    };
+    rows.push(row);
+    if (usable) {
+      const slot = bySlot.get(key);
+      if (slot) slot.push(bar.volume);
+      else bySlot.set(key, [bar.volume]);
+      committed.push(bar.volume);
+    }
+  }
+  return rows;
+}
