+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../../types/frames";
+import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
+import { resetDrawRegistryForTests, tablesForOwners } from "../../../../chart/draw/registry";
+import type { TableData } from "../../../../chart/draw/table";
+import { HISTORICAL_INDICATOR_POLICIES, INDICATOR_REGISTRY, historicalIndicatorAvailability } from "../../../../chart/indicatorRegistry";
+import { resampleBars } from "../../../../chart/resampleBars";
+import {
+  BINANCE_PERP,
+  DXFEED_EQUITY,
+  barFrame,
+  driveLive,
+  equityUnsidedBars,
+  openLiveSession,
+  relativeVolumeEquityFixture,
+  replayCapture,
+  type Arrival,
+} from "../../../../chart/validation";
+import { nyLocalMinuteOfDay } from "../../../killzones/killzones";
+import { RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, RELATIVE_VOLUME_SETTINGS_SCHEMA, relativeVolumeIndicator, type RelativeVolumeSettings } from "../../relativeVolume";
+import { HOUR_MS, MINUTE_MS, RVOL_MIN_SESSIONS_SETTING, createRelativeVolumeEngine } from "../../relativeVolumeMath";
+import { medianOf } from "./reference";
+
+// Validation loop 1: the brief's rules as amended by the PM's rulings on the
+// Codex G3 gate (PM/cycle1/brief-rvol-clock.md, "Rulings"). Tests under
+// "amended brief" describe the required behaviour and FAIL against build
+// ce8d11c until the builder's fix round; they are the settling tests.
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
+  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
+});
+
+const SETTINGS: RelativeVolumeSettings = { ...RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, showRolling: true };
+const EQUITY = relativeVolumeEquityFixture();
+const T0 = Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2);
+
+function hourly(day: number, hour: number, volume: number, closed = true): BarFrame {
+  return barFrame(T0 + day * 24 * HOUR_MS + hour * HOUR_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume }, BINANCE_PERP, HOUR_MS, closed);
+}
+
+function tableTexts(owner: string): string[] {
+  const grid = (tablesForOwners([owner])[0].data as TableData).committedGrid;
+  return ["0,0", "1,0", "2,0"].map((key) => grid[key]?.text ?? "");
+}
+
+describe("status precedence (rule 11) through the runtime's table, including the median-zero case the builder did not drive", () => {
+  it("unsupported, no usable volume, learning, no usable baseline, forming, ready, in that order", () => {
+    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, []);
+    const owner = () => session.runtime.getDrawOwnerId(relativeVolumeIndicator.id)!;
+    try {
+      // Hourly UTC bars, slot 0 only carries information: zero volume on days 1..3, so its median is 0 on day 4.
+      for (let day = 0; day < 3; day += 1) {
+        for (let hour = 0; hour < 24; hour += 1) session.dispatch({ bar: hourly(day, hour, hour === 0 ? 0 : 100) });
+      }
+      expect(tableTexts(owner())[2]).toBe("Learning · 2 of 3 prior");
+      session.dispatch({ bar: hourly(3, 0, 50) });
+      expect(tableTexts(owner())).toEqual(["RVOL — · 3 prior", "Rolling 0.5× · 20 bars", "No usable baseline"]);
+      expect(session.capture().outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBeNull();
+      session.dispatch({ bar: hourly(3, 1, 70, false) });
+      expect(tableTexts(owner())).toEqual(["RVOL 0.7× · 3 prior", "Rolling 0.7× · 20 bars", "Forming · vs full bar"]);
+      session.dispatch({ bar: hourly(3, 1, Number.NaN, false) });
+      expect(tableTexts(owner())).toEqual(["RVOL — · 3 prior", "Rolling — · 20 bars", "No usable volume"]);
+      session.dispatch({ bar: hourly(3, 1, 120) });
+      expect(tableTexts(owner())).toEqual(["RVOL 1.2× · 3 prior", "Rolling 1.2× · 20 bars", ""]);
+      const twoHour = barFrame(T0 + 4 * 24 * HOUR_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: Number.NaN }, BINANCE_PERP, 2 * HOUR_MS);
+      session.dispatch({ bar: twoHour });
+      // Unsupported wins over an unusable volume.
+      expect(tableTexts(owner())).toEqual(["RVOL — · 0 prior", "Rolling — · 20 bars", "Needs 1m to 1h"]);
+      expect(session.capture().faulted).toBe(false);
+    } finally {
+      session.close();
+    }
+  });
+});
+
+describe("older-than-tail dispatch, duplicate close and the forming tail, through the indicator def", () => {
+  it("a dispatch older than the tail changes no output, no ring and no marker", () => {
+    const bars = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 4 * 390 + 30);
+    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, []);
+    try {
+      for (const bar of bars) session.dispatch({ bar });
+      const before = session.capture();
+      // The runtime would classify this REBUILT; drive the def directly to reach the engine's own guard.
+      const older = { ...bars[3 * 390 + 5], volume: 9_999_999, closed: true };
+      const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
+      for (const bar of bars) engine.onBar(bar);
+      const snapshot = JSON.stringify({ tail: engine.tail, slot: engine.slotRing(575), rolling: engine.rollingRing });
+      const reading = engine.onBar(older);
+      expect(reading).toBe(engine.current);
+      expect(JSON.stringify({ tail: engine.tail, slot: engine.slotRing(575), rolling: engine.rollingRing })).toBe(snapshot);
+      expect(session.capture()).toEqual(before);
+    } finally {
+      session.close();
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("a replay whose tail is forming leaves the tail provisional: marker at 50 percent alpha, status Forming, nothing filed", () => {
+    const bars = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 4 * 390 + 10);
+    const last = bars[bars.length - 1];
+    const prior = EQUITY.bars.filter((bar, index) => index < bars.length - 1 && nyLocalMinuteOfDay(bar.ts_open) === nyLocalMinuteOfDay(last.ts_open)).map((bar) => bar.volume);
+    const median = medianOf(prior.slice(-10));
+    const tail = { ...last, closed: false, volume: Math.ceil(median * 2.5) } as BarFrame;
+    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, [...bars.slice(0, -1), tail]);
+    try {
+      const capture = session.capture();
+      expect(capture.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBeCloseTo(tail.volume / median, 12);
+      const marker = capture.markers.find((call) => call.time === tail.ts_open);
+      expect(marker).toMatchObject({ shape: "circle", position: "aboveBar", color: "rgba(37,99,235,0.5)" });
+      const owner = session.runtime.getDrawOwnerId(relativeVolumeIndicator.id)!;
+      expect(tableTexts(owner)[2]).toBe("Forming · vs full bar");
+      const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
+      for (const bar of [...bars.slice(0, -1), tail]) engine.onBar(bar);
+      expect(engine.tail!.committed).toBe(false);
+      expect(engine.slotRing(nyLocalMinuteOfDay(tail.ts_open))!.items.some((item) => item.tsOpen === tail.ts_open)).toBe(false);
+    } finally {
+      session.close();
+    }
+  });
+});
+
+describe("corrections through the runtime (amendments 1 and 2), judged against a feed that only saw the final state", () => {
+  const index = 14 * 390 + 100; // session 15: every slot holds 10, the rolling ring 20
+  const bar = EQUITY.bars[index];
+  const prefix = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, index);
+  const suffix = EQUITY.bars.slice(index + 1, index + 30);
+
+  function finalStateFeed(finalVolume: number): ReturnType<typeof driveLive> {
+    return driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, [...prefix, { ...bar, volume: finalVolume } as BarFrame, ...suffix].map((b) => ({ bar: b })), SETTINGS);
+  }
+
+  it("valid(a) → valid(b) → invalid → valid(c) → next bars equals a feed that only ever saw c", () => {
+    const arrivals: Arrival[] = prefix.map((b) => ({ bar: b }));
+    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: 500 } }, { bar: { ...bar, volume: 900 } }, { bar: { ...bar, volume: Number.NaN } }, { bar: { ...bar, volume: 3_000 } });
+    for (const later of suffix) arrivals.push({ bar: later });
+    const corrected = driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, arrivals, SETTINGS);
+    const clean = finalStateFeed(3_000);
+    expect(corrected.outputs).toEqual(clean.outputs);
+    expect(corrected.markers).toEqual(clean.markers);
+    expect(corrected.outputs.rvol[index]).toBeGreaterThan(2);
+  });
+
+  it("valid → invalid, then the next bar arrives: the observation stays out and what its insertion evicted stays restored", () => {
+    const arrivals: Arrival[] = prefix.map((b) => ({ bar: b }));
+    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: 500 } }, { bar: { ...bar, volume: Number.NaN } });
+    for (const later of suffix) arrivals.push({ bar: later });
+    const corrected = driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, arrivals, SETTINGS);
+    const clean = finalStateFeed(Number.NaN);
+    expect(corrected.outputs).toEqual(clean.outputs);
+    expect(corrected.outputs.rvol[index]).toBeNull();
+    // The same slot one session earlier still counts: ten prior observations for the next bar of this slot would have been 10 either way.
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
+    for (const b of prefix) engine.onBar(b);
+    const key = nyLocalMinuteOfDay(bar.ts_open);
+    const before = engine.slotRing(key)!.items.map((item) => item.tsOpen);
+    engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: 500 });
+    engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: Number.NaN });
+    engine.onBar(suffix[0]);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items.map((item) => item.tsOpen)).toEqual(before);
+    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.evictedByTail).toBeNull();
+  });
+});
+
+describe("equity unsided volume (check 9) and Historical Mode policy (check 10)", () => {
+  it("reads only volume: outputs are identical with sides present, partially present or absent", () => {
+    // Four UTC days of one-minute bars, so every minute of day has three prior observations by day four.
+    const { bars } = equityUnsidedBars({ count: 4 * 1_440 + 300, identity: DXFEED_EQUITY });
+    const stripped = bars.map((bar) => {
+      const { buy_volume: _buy, sell_volume: _sell, ...rest } = bar;
+      return rest as BarFrame;
+    });
+    const sided = bars.map((bar) => ({ ...bar, buy_volume: bar.volume * 0.6, sell_volume: bar.volume * 0.4 }) as BarFrame);
+    const settings: RelativeVolumeSettings = { ...SETTINGS, clock: "utc" };
+    const a = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, bars, settings).outputs;
+    expect(replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, stripped, settings).outputs).toEqual(a);
+    expect(replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, sided, settings).outputs).toEqual(a);
+    expect(a.rvol.some((value) => value !== null)).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("OHLCV_POLICY row, appended last in the registry, and a candle-only replay with no extras yields real ratios on every archived instrument", () => {
+    expect(INDICATOR_REGISTRY[INDICATOR_REGISTRY.length - 1].id).toBe("relativeVolume");
+    expect(HISTORICAL_INDICATOR_POLICIES.relativeVolume).toEqual({ requiredCapabilities: ["ohlcv"] });
+    for (const provider of ["BINANCE", "DXFEED", "DATABENTO"] as const) {
+      expect(historicalIndicatorAvailability("relativeVolume", new Set(["ohlcv"]), provider)).toEqual({ available: true, reason: null });
+    }
+    expect(historicalIndicatorAvailability("relativeVolume", new Set(["buy_volume"]), "BINANCE").available).toBe(false);
+    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, EQUITY.bars, RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
+    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(capture.outputs.rvol.slice(3 * 390).every((value) => value !== null && Number.isFinite(value))).toBe(true);
+    expect(capture.markers.length).toBe(13);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("the resampler's 15:30 hourly bar", () => {
+  it("keys 930 through the def and divides only by earlier 15:30 bars, never by 15:00 bars", () => {
+    const hourly: BarFrame[] = [];
+    for (let session = 0; session < 5; session += 1) hourly.push(...resampleBars(EQUITY.bars.slice(session * 390, (session + 1) * 390), HOUR_MS));
+    expect(hourly).toHaveLength(35);
+    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, hourly, SETTINGS);
+    const lastHalfHour = hourly[34];
+    expect(nyLocalMinuteOfDay(lastHalfHour.ts_open)).toBe(930);
+    expect(lastHalfHour.interval_ms).toBe(HOUR_MS);
+    const earlier930 = hourly.filter((bar, index) => index < 34 && nyLocalMinuteOfDay(bar.ts_open) === 930).map((bar) => bar.volume);
+    const earlier870 = hourly.filter((bar, index) => index < 34 && nyLocalMinuteOfDay(bar.ts_open) === 870).map((bar) => bar.volume);
+    expect(earlier930).toHaveLength(4);
+    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[34]).toBeCloseTo(lastHalfHour.volume / medianOf(earlier930), 12);
+    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[34]).not.toBeCloseTo(lastHalfHour.volume / medianOf(earlier870), 3);
+    // The half-hour bucket is smaller than the full 15:00 hour even with the closing surge, so the two denominators differ.
+    expect(medianOf(earlier930) / medianOf(earlier870)).toBeLessThan(0.9);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("amended brief (PM rulings on Codex G3): FAIL against ce8d11c until the builder's fix round", () => {
+  it("rule 7(b) withdrawn: a bar superseded without ever closing contributes NO observation to either ring", () => {
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 2, clock: "utc" });
+    for (let day = 0; day < 2; day += 1) for (let hour = 0; hour < 24; hour += 1) engine.onBar(hourly(day, hour, 60));
+    engine.onBar(hourly(2, 0, 30, false)); // half the bucket arrived, then the source went quiet
+    engine.onBar(hourly(2, 1, 60)); // the next bucket supersedes it
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([60, 60]);
+    expect(engine.rollingRing.items.some((item) => item.tsOpen === T0 + 2 * 24 * HOUR_MS)).toBe(false);
+    const next = engine.onBar(hourly(3, 0, 60));
+    expect(next.priorCount).toBe(2);
+    expect(next.rvol).toBe(1);
+  });
+
+  it("rule 7(b) withdrawn, through the runtime: a resampled bucket truncated by a source gap is not committed when the next bucket arrives", () => {
+    // Source minutes: day 1 hours 0..2 complete; day 2 hour 0 has only its first 30 minutes, then minutes resume at hour 1.
+    const minutes: BarFrame[] = [];
+    const push = (day: number, hour: number, minute: number) =>
+      minutes.push(barFrame(T0 + day * 24 * HOUR_MS + hour * HOUR_MS + minute * MINUTE_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: 1 }, BINANCE_PERP));
+    for (let hour = 0; hour < 3; hour += 1) for (let minute = 0; minute < 60; minute += 1) push(0, hour, minute);
+    for (let minute = 0; minute < 30; minute += 1) push(1, 0, minute);
+    for (let hour = 1; hour < 3; hour += 1) for (let minute = 0; minute < 60; minute += 1) push(1, hour, minute);
+    for (let hour = 0; hour < 3; hour += 1) for (let minute = 0; minute < 60; minute += 1) push(2, hour, minute);
+    const resampled = resampleBars(minutes, HOUR_MS);
+    const truncated = resampled.find((bar) => bar.ts_open === T0 + 24 * HOUR_MS)!;
+    expect(truncated).toMatchObject({ volume: 30, closed: false });
+    const live = driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, resampled.map((bar) => ({ bar })), { ...SETTINGS, minSessions: 2 });
+    const day3hour0 = resampled.findIndex((bar) => bar.ts_open === T0 + 2 * 24 * HOUR_MS);
+    // Amended rule: prior observations for slot 0 are [60] only, below minSessions 2, so day 3 hour 0 is still learning (null), not 60 / median([60, 30]) = 1.33.
+    expect(live.outputs.rvol[day3hour0]).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it("sessions has a minimum of 3 in the schema and in the engine's clamp", () => {
+    const field = RELATIVE_VOLUME_SETTINGS_SCHEMA.find((candidate) => candidate.kind !== "staticHint" && candidate.key === "sessions");
+    expect(field && field.kind === "numeric" ? field.min : null).toBe(3);
+    expect(RVOL_MIN_SESSIONS_SETTING).toBe(3);
+    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 2, minSessions: 2, clock: "utc" });
+    for (const volume of [100, 1_000, 250, 100]) engine.onBar(hourly(engine.tail === null ? 0 : (engine.tail.tsOpen - T0) / (24 * HOUR_MS) + 1, 0, volume));
+    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.capacity).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
+  });
+
+  it("a cosmetic setting never changes a number: changing a colour after a rollover leaves every output and marker as it was", () => {
+    // Eleven days of one-minute bars: 100 per minute for five days, then 300, the last bar 450 (Codex item 1).
+    const bars: BarFrame[] = [];
+    for (let index = 0; index < 11 * 1_440; index += 1) {
+      const volume = index === 11 * 1_440 - 1 ? 450 : index < 5 * 1_440 ? 100 : 300;
+      bars.push(barFrame(T0 + index * MINUTE_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume }, BINANCE_PERP));
+    }
+    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, []);
+    try {
+      for (const bar of bars) session.dispatch({ bar });
+      const before = session.capture();
+      expect(before.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(2.25);
+      // Every setting the schema calls cosmetic, changed one at a time; if the builder removes the pickers the loop is empty and the rule holds trivially.
+      const cosmetic = RELATIVE_VOLUME_SETTINGS_SCHEMA.filter((field) => field.kind !== "staticHint" && field.affects === "cosmetic");
+      for (const field of cosmetic) {
+        if (field.kind === "staticHint") continue;
+        const key = field.key as keyof RelativeVolumeSettings;
+        const value = field.kind === "color" ? "#123456" : field.kind === "boolean" ? !SETTINGS[key] : SETTINGS[key];
+        session.updateSettings({ ...SETTINGS, [key]: value });
+        const after = session.capture();
+        expect(after.outputs.rvol.at(-1), `after changing cosmetic ${String(key)}`).toBe(2.25);
+        expect(after.outputs.rvol).toEqual(before.outputs.rvol);
+        expect(after.markers.map((call) => call.time)).toEqual(before.markers.map((call) => call.time));
+        session.updateSettings(SETTINGS);
+      }
+    } finally {
+      session.close();
+    }
+  });
+});

+# Relative Volume (Time of Day)
+
+[Handbook](../../README.md) · [Indicator index](README.md) ·
+[Data for indicator authors](data-for-indicator-authors.md) ·
+[Setting presets](setting-presets.md)
+
+Code: `apps/web/src/lib/indicators/relative-volume/` (`relativeVolume.ts` is
+the registry definition, `relativeVolumeMath.ts` the pure machinery, `tests/`
+the evidence). Registry id `relativeVolume`, label "Relative Volume (Time of
+Day)", category technical, own pane. The code wins over this page.
+
+## The question it answers
+
+"Is this bar's volume unusual compared with the same minute of the day on
+recent sessions, or is it just the open, the close or the London/New York
+overlap being its normal self?"
+
+A rolling-average relative volume compares a bar with the twenty bars before
+it, so the first minute of a cash session always looks loud and the lunch hour
+always looks dead. This indicator compares a bar only with bars that started at
+the same minute of the day on earlier sessions. High relative volume says
+nothing about direction or follow-through; the colours are purple and blue so
+nothing on the pane reads as buy or sell. The baseline is short and noisy, so
+the claim is "unusual relative to the last N matching bars at this minute", not
+"unusual" in any absolute sense. Descriptive only.
+
+## Calculation
+
+One bar at a time, incrementally; `onBar` never rebuilds from an array.
+
+1. **Clock.** The `clock` setting: `auto` is New York for the dxFeed and
+   Databento providers and UTC for Binance; `newYork` and `utc` force it. The
+   New York minute of day comes from the killzones helper
+   (`nyLocalMinuteOfDay`), cached once per UTC hour; there is no new
+   time-zone code.
+2. **Slot key.** The bar's start minute of day in that clock (0..1439). There
+   is no division by bar length: an hourly equity bar starting 09:30 keys 570
+   and the 15:30 half-hour bar keys 930, so they never collide. At most 1,440
+   keys exist.
+3. **Observation.** The bar's `volume`. A volume that is not a finite number
+   at or above zero contributes nothing and the bar reads `null`.
+4. **Baseline.** Per slot key, a ring of the last `sessions` committed
+   observations (default 10, range 3..60) and their median (even count: mean
+   of the two middle values). Median, not mean: the median limits one news
+   day's influence on tomorrow's baseline instead of letting it inflate the
+   denominator. The minimum is three because with two observations the
+   even-count median is a mean. The ring capacity is the only bound.
+5. **Rolling comparison.** A ring of the last 20 committed bars; its mean is
+   the plain relative-volume baseline, shown for comparison.
+6. **Read before write.** When a bar first arrives, the slot median, the count
+   of prior observations and the rolling mean are read and stored on the bar
+   record. Every later recomputation of that bar (a forming revision, a
+   corrected close) divides by those stored values. A bar never sees its own
+   volume in its denominator.
+7. **Commit.** One observation per `ts_open`, filed into both rings on the
+   bar's first dispatch with `closed: true`. Only a closed bar is ever filed:
+   a bar that is superseded by a newer bar without ever closing (a close that
+   never arrived, a resampled bucket cut short by a source gap) contributes
+   **nothing** to either ring and its own ratio stays provisional. Missing
+   data contributes nothing. A later closed dispatch of the same bar with a
+   different volume updates the observation in place; a valid-to-invalid
+   correction removes it and restores whatever its insertion evicted;
+   invalid-to-valid re-inserts it. Corrections are tail-only: a correction to
+   an older bar reaches the indicator as the runtime's full replay, which
+   recreates this state from scratch.
+8. **Outputs.** `rvol = volume / slot median`, `null` while the slot has fewer
+   than `minSessions` prior observations (default 3, clamped to `sessions`)
+   or the median is zero. `rolling = volume / rolling mean`, `null` until 20
+   bars are committed. The forming bar is divided by the full-bar
+   expectation, so it grows toward its final value during the bar.
+9. **Fail closed.** Any non-finite intermediate yields `null`; nothing ever
+   emits `NaN`.
+
+Lookback counts **matching bars, not calendar sessions**: a missing slot
+stretches the lookback across more sessions, and the autumn New York DST
+repeat can supply two observations for one local minute on crypto under the
+`newYork` clock. The table therefore says "7 prior" and the control is
+"Lookback (prior bars)".
+
+## Data and the clock rule
+
+Reads `bar.ts_open`, `bar.interval_ms`, `bar.volume`, `bar.closed` and
+`bar.provider` (the provider only picks the clock). No sided volume, no ticks,
+no order book, no wall clock. Missing bars (gaps in `ts_open`) contribute
+nothing, never a zero; the rolling comparison carries across a gap because it
+counts committed bars, not minutes.
+
+Timeframes longer than one hour cannot be keyed by minute of day: every output
+is `null` and the table says `Needs 1m to 1h`. There is no `timeframeGate`, so
+the pane and the table stay on the chart to say so.
+
+The resampler reports the nominal `interval_ms` even for a shortened bucket
+(the 15:30 equity half hour, an early-close session's last bar), so the
+indicator cannot know a bar's real duration and does **no duration
+normalisation**. The start-minute key already compares the 15:30 bar only with
+other 15:30 bars. The one unhandled case is an early-close session, whose last
+bar is shorter than its slot's usual bars; it enters as one observation among
+`sessions` and the median limits its influence without removing it (on the
+test fixture one 30-minute bar among three full hours moves the median from
+60 to 59).
+
+## Default view
+
+Own pane with `autoscaleRange` 0..4 and dotted reference lines at 1, 2 and 3.
+Those lines are **fixed reference marks**: they do not move when "Unusual
+above" is changed; the exact ratio is in the table.
+
+- One line, `rvol`, width 2: purple `#8672b3` at or above 1, blue `#2563eb`
+  when at or above the "unusual" threshold, grey `#787b86` below 1.
+- One fill between the line and 1, `rgba(134,114,179,0.15)`, above 1 only.
+- A circle above the bar when the bar is unusual (section "Alert conditions").
+- The rolling comparison line (width 1, grey) is off by default; its value is
+  always in the table.
+- While a bar is forming, its line colour, marker and table value use the same
+  hues at 50 percent alpha; they settle at the close.
+- Table, top right, three rows: `RVOL 2.4× · 7 prior` coloured like the line
+  (the count is the prior observations in the denominator, excluding this
+  bar), `Rolling 1.1× · 20 bars`, and a status row. Unavailable values show
+  `—`. Before warm-up the pane is empty, never zero.
+
+Status row, first match wins: `Needs 1m to 1h`, `No usable volume`,
+`Learning · 1 of 3 prior`, `No usable baseline` (median 0), `Forming · vs full
+bar`, and blank when the bar is ready and closed. The `draw.table` handle has
+no way to drop a row once written, so the blank status row stays as an empty
+strip when ready; removing it entirely needs a `TableHandle` change in
+`apps/web/src/lib/chart/draw/table.ts`.
+
+## Controls
+
+Inputs tab (two primary controls, each with a one-line hint):
+
+| Key | Label | Default | Range | Effect |
+|---|---|---|---|---|
+| `sessions` | Lookback (prior bars) | 10 | 3..60, step 1 | Ring capacity per slot. Replays every bar. |
+| `hotThreshold` | Unusual above (×) | 2 | 1..10, step 0.5 | Colours the line and marks the bar. Replays every bar. |
+
+Advanced group (collapsed at the end of the Inputs tab):
+
+| Key | Label | Default | Range | Effect |
+|---|---|---|---|---|
+| `minSessions` | Minimum prior bars | 3 | 1..20, step 1 | Prior observations a slot needs before a ratio shows; clamped to the lookback. Replays. |
+| `clock` | Clock | Automatic | Automatic / New York / UTC | Which minute of day a bar is filed under. Replays. |
+
+Visibility tab:
+
+| Key | Label | Default | Effect |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| `showRolling` | Show rolling 20 | off | Draws the rolling comparison line. Replays. |
+| `showMarkers` | Show unusual markers | on | Draws the circles. Replays. |
+
+Every setting is `recompute`: `hotThreshold`, `showMarkers` and `showRolling`
+change which bars are coloured or marked, which cannot be reclassified from a
+stored colour alone, so they replay (well under a second for 10,080 bars).
+There is no cosmetic setting and the colours are fixed in this version. The
+reason is a rule, not a shortcut: a cosmetic setting must never change a
+number, and a replay over the retained bars can (section "Replay and
+Historical Mode"), so no setting is allowed to trigger one unless it changes
+the mathematics. Every key is in the schema, so [presets](setting-presets.md)
+work without any extra code.
+
+## Alert conditions
+
+There is no notification channel; an alert condition is a marker plus a table
+row.
+
+**Unusual volume.** Rule: `rvol >= hotThreshold` (default 2) on the bar of the
+current call. It fires on that bar, as a circle above the bar in the unusual
+colour, size 1, enlarged to size 2 when `rvol >= 3` (with a threshold above 3
+every drawn marker is large). The marker is a pure function of the bar's
+current ratio: it appears while the bar forms (at 50 percent alpha), **confirms
+at the bar close**, and a correction crossing the threshold in either direction
+adds or removes it on the next dispatch; a forming revision redraws rather than
+duplicates. A bar whose close never arrived keeps its provisional marker. The
+table's first row carries the same ratio in the same colour.
+
+Fixture rates, both quoted because they differ by design:
+
+- Frozen 15-session equity fixture at the default threshold: 13 markers over
+  the 4,680 evaluated bars (sessions 4..15), 0.28 per 100 bars (2.2 per
+  1,000), at most one per bar; the twelve labelled anomalies plus one noise
+  bar.
+- Session-wide volume shift (three days at 100 per minute, then a day at
+  250): 1,440 of 1,440 eligible bars are marked, 1,000 per 1,000. That is
+  correct behaviour for a descriptive tool: every bar of such a session is
+  unusual relative to the prior sessions at its own minute, and no cooldown
+  hides it. A one-hour 3× burst on an ordinary day marks exactly those 60
+  bars.
+
+The tool is classified as a descriptive annotation under the protocol's 200
+per 1,000 ceiling on typical data; the frozen fixture sits at 2.2 per 1,000.
+
+## Warm-up
+
+A slot shows a ratio once it holds `minSessions` prior observations (three by
+default), so a fresh symbol shows `Learning · n of 3 prior` for three sessions
+at every minute. The rolling comparison needs 20 committed bars. The chart
+retains 10,080 bars: about seven UTC days of one-minute crypto, about 25
+regular sessions of one-minute equities. After a reload, one-minute crypto
+therefore has **at most six prior observations per minute** until the chart
+has run for ten days; the default lookback of ten fills only as the live
+session keeps running, and a 60-bar lookback never fills on one-minute bars.
+Hourly bars warm up after three sessions as well, since the key is the start
+minute, not the bar count.
+
+## Replay and Historical Mode
+
+Candle-only, policy `OHLCV_POLICY`: it works on every archived instrument and
+on equities (equity candles are regular session only, so the New York keys run
+570..959). A clean replay and a live feed with forming revisions end on the
+same committed numbers and the same confirmed markers; the validation tests
+pin this on the equity and crypto fixtures (`relativeVolumeValidation.test.ts`).
+
+One documented difference: live state keeps observations from bars the chart
+has already discarded past its 10,080-bar retention, while a replay starts
+from the retained bars. Live-versus-replay equivalence therefore holds for
+every bar whose full lookback lies within the retained series; on the 12,000-bar
+crypto fixture at a lookback of 3 that is 5,760 of the 10,080 retained bars,
+and the first 1,920 retained bars differ by design. On one-minute crypto with
+the default lookback of ten this means a rebuild or a reconnect (a late bar,
+a snapshot, any `REBUILT` replay) **can change the latest ratio**: after
+eleven live days a bar at 450 against ten prior observations of 100 and 300
+reads 2.25× live and 1.5× after a replay that holds only the six retained
+prior observations at 300. The test `rollover and reconnect` pins exactly
+this. A bar that never closed before the next bar arrived is never filed; it
+keeps its provisional styling live and a replay of the retained bars does not
+contain it as an observation either, so the two agree.
+
+Truncation invariance (no future data) holds at every cut: a bar's committed
+output and confirmed marker never change when later bars are added.
+
+## Baseline comparison
+
+Three methods at the same threshold (2.0) on the same eligible bars, all using
+volumes, on the frozen equity fixture from the validation harness
+(`relativeVolumeEquityFixture()`: 15 sessions of 390 one-minute bars from
+Monday 2026-03-02 09:30 New York across the spring DST change, U-shaped
+expected volume, lognormal noise, twelve 2.5× anomalies in sessions 4..15 at
+minutes 90..330; predictable slots are minutes 0..9 and 380..389). Eligible
+bars are those of sessions 4..15 where all three methods are ready; readiness
+is read from actual prior observations per key. A false positive is a
+detection on an eligible non-anomaly bar. Reproduced by
+`tests/relativeVolumeBaseline.test.ts`, which prints this table.
+
+| Method | Eligible bars | Detections | Anomalies hit | Recall | False positives | FP rate | Predictable-slot bars | Predictable-slot FPs | Predictable-slot FP rate |
+|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
+| Time of day (slot median, this indicator) | 4680 | 13 | 12/12 | 100.00% | 1 | 0.02% | 240 | 0 | 0.00% |
+| Same-slot mean | 4680 | 12 | 12/12 | 100.00% | 0 | 0.00% | 240 | 0 | 0.00% |
+| Rolling 20 mean (plain RVOL) | 4680 | 32 | 12/12 | 100.00% | 20 | 0.43% | 240 | 19 | 7.92% |
+
+False-positive rates divide by the eligible non-anomaly bars (4,668).
+
+What the tool shows that the baseline hides: the rolling baseline fires on 19
+of the 240 opening and closing minutes of the evaluated sessions, where volume
+is always loud, and the time-of-day baseline fires on none of them, at the same
+recall. Acceptance: recall 12/12 (required at least 11/12 and at least rolling
+minus one); predictable-slot false-positive rate 0.00 percent (required at
+most 10 percent and at most half of rolling's 7.92 percent). The same-slot
+mean is as good as the median on this fixture, which has one anomaly per slot
+at most; the median's advantage, one news day not inflating tomorrow's
+baseline, needs repeated anomalies in one slot and is not measured here.
+
+Control experiment (reported, not a gate): the same fixture with flat
+seasonality (`E(m) = V`) and twelve anomalies drawn from every minute. When
+the clock explains nothing, the three methods agree on the anomalies and the
+rolling baseline no longer misfires; the time-of-day method keeps one noise
+false positive.
+
+| Method | Eligible bars | Detections | Anomalies hit | Recall | False positives | FP rate | Predictable-slot bars | Predictable-slot FPs | Predictable-slot FP rate |
+|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
+| Time of day (slot median) | 4680 | 13 | 12/12 | 100.00% | 1 | 0.02% | 240 | 0 | 0.00% |
+| Same-slot mean | 4680 | 12 | 12/12 | 100.00% | 0 | 0.00% | 240 | 0 | 0.00% |
+| Rolling 20 mean | 4680 | 12 | 12/12 | 100.00% | 0 | 0.00% | 240 | 0 | 0.00% |
+
+## Performance and bounded state
+
+Measured on this container (4 cores, Node 24.21.0, vitest) with the
+harness's pooled probe (three untimed warm-up replays, five timed replays of
+the first 10,080 bars of the frozen crypto fixture, `onBar` plus `readOutput`
+per bar with no-op draw and marker namespaces, p99 by nearest rank; drawing is
+excluded): p50 0.001 ms, p99 0.004 ms, against the brief's 0.05 ms ceiling.
+The real-runtime activation replay of the same bars took 43 ms wall with an
+`onBar` p99 of 0.005 ms. The forming-bar probe warms every slot with all
+10,080 fixture bars first and then revises the next bar 500 times, crossing
+the unusual threshold on every revision so the marker appears and disappears
+each time: `onBar` p50 0.0024 ms, p99 0.0298 ms, max 0.184 ms (the validator
+measured p99 0.0317 ms, max 0.085 ms), reported beside the pooled replay p99
+of 0.0041 ms by `relativeVolumePerf.test.ts` and in its JSON. After rolling
+1,000 bars past the retention cap every output line holds exactly 10,080
+entries, no marker is older than the oldest retained bar and the heap grows
+about 630 to 660 bytes per bar (the harness floor is about 370).
+State is at most 1,440 slot rings of `sessions` observations, one ring of 20,
+and one tail record.
+
+## Limitations
+
+- Needs `minSessions` prior observations per slot: one-minute crypto retains
+  seven UTC days, one-minute equities about 25 sessions; a fresh symbol shows
+  "learning" for three sessions, and after a reload one-minute crypto has at
+  most six prior observations per minute until the chart has run for ten days.
+- A bar that never closed contributes nothing; a session-wide volume shift
+  marks every bar of that session (section "Alert conditions").
+- Colours are fixed in this version; there is no cosmetic setting.
+- Holidays and early closes are ordinary sessions; an early-close session's
+  short last bar is one observation among `sessions` (section "Data").
+- No duration normalisation (nominal `interval_ms`).
+- Equity candles are regular session only, so no pre-market or after-hours
+  slots exist.
+- Weekend behaviour on crypto is not separated: a Saturday 14:00 bar is
+  compared with weekday 14:00 bars. A weekend slot family is deferred.
+- The ratio is unit-free; volume units are the provider's (Binance base
+  asset, futures contracts, equity shares).
+- Descriptive only: high relative volume says nothing about direction or
+  follow-through, and nothing here is a prediction.
+- The status row cannot be removed once written (section "Default view").
+- No tooltip: an SDK indicator has no hover today, so everything a trader needs
+  is in the table and on the reference lines.
+
+## Tests
+
+`apps/web/src/lib/indicators/relative-volume/tests/`:
+`relativeVolumeMath.test.ts` (hand-computed three-session fixture, rings,
+corrections, eviction rollback, clock, gap, early close, resampler, crypto
+retention), `relativeVolume.test.ts` (identity, policy, schema, colours, table
+copy, markers, replace semantics), `relativeVolumeValidation.test.ts` (the
+protocol checks through the real runtime: truncation invariance, live versus
+replay, duplicate, REBUILT, corrections, time gap, warm-up, empty input, no
+NaN, the crypto rollover and REBUILT equivalence at lookback 3),
+`relativeVolumeBaseline.test.ts` (the comparison table, the control
+experiment, the marker budget) and `relativeVolumePerf.test.ts` (the pooled
+probe, activation replay, rollover and forming-bar probes; `RVOL_PERF_OUT`
+keeps the JSON).

     1	import type { BarFrame } from "../../../types/frames";
     2	import { nyLocalMinuteOfDay } from "../killzones/killzones";
     3	
     4	/**
     5	 * Relative Volume by Time of Day: the pure, incremental machinery.
     6	 *
     7	 * One observation per committed bar is filed under the bar's start minute of
     8	 * day (the "slot key") in a ring of the last `sessions` observations, and in a
     9	 * second ring of the last 20 committed bars. A bar's ratio divides its volume
    10	 * by the slot ring's median read *before* the bar's own observation entered
    11	 * the ring (`baselineAtOpen`), so a bar never sees itself in its denominator
    12	 * and every later revision of that bar divides by the same number.
    13	 *
    14	 * Corrections are tail-only: a closed dispatch of the newest bar with a
    15	 * different volume updates the observation in place, a valid-to-invalid
    16	 * correction removes it (restoring whatever its insertion evicted), and
    17	 * invalid-to-valid re-inserts it. A correction to any older bar reaches the
    18	 * indicator as a full replay, which recreates this state from scratch.
    19	 *
    20	 * Only a closed bar is ever filed. A bar that is superseded by a newer bar
    21	 * without ever closing (a close that never arrived, a resampled bucket cut
    22	 * short by a source gap) contributes no observation to either ring and its
    23	 * own reading stays provisional: missing data contributes nothing.
    24	 *
    25	 * Exported so that a later indicator (Squeeze Breakout Context) can import the
    26	 * slot-median machinery as its volume confirmation.
    27	 */
    28	
    29	export const MINUTE_MS = 60_000;
    30	export const HOUR_MS = 3_600_000;
    31	export const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
    32	export const MINUTES_PER_DAY = 1_440;
    33	/** Time of day needs an intraday bar: anything longer than one hour yields null. */
    34	export const RVOL_MAX_INTERVAL_MS = HOUR_MS;
    35	export const RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH = 20;
    36	export const RVOL_DEFAULT_SESSIONS = 10;
    37	/** Three, not two: with two observations the even-count median is a mean, and one news day would halve tomorrow's ratio. */
    38	export const RVOL_MIN_SESSIONS_SETTING = 3;
    39	export const RVOL_MAX_SESSIONS_SETTING = 60;
    40	export const RVOL_DEFAULT_MIN_SESSIONS = 3;
    41	
    42	export type RvolClock = "auto" | "newYork" | "utc";
    43	export type ResolvedRvolClock = "newYork" | "utc";
    44	
    45	export interface RelativeVolumeMathSettings {
    46	  /** Ring capacity per slot key: the last N matching bars (3..60). */
    47	  sessions: number;
    48	  /** Prior observations a slot needs before a ratio is shown; clamped to `sessions` at read time. */
    49	  minSessions: number;
    50	  clock: RvolClock;
    51	}
    52	
    53	/** `auto` is New York for the dxFeed and Databento providers and UTC for Binance. */
    54	export function resolveRvolClock(clock: RvolClock, provider: BarFrame["provider"]): ResolvedRvolClock {
    55	  if (clock === "newYork" || clock === "utc") return clock;
    56	  return provider === "DXFEED" || provider === "DATABENTO" ? "newYork" : "utc";
    57	}
    58	
    59	/**
    60	 * One-entry cache of the New York minute at the start of a UTC hour. The New
    61	 * York offset is constant inside any UTC hour (both DST changes fall on an
    62	 * hour boundary), so a chronological series needs one `Intl` call per hour.
    63	 */
    64	export interface ClockCache {
    65	  hourStart: number;
    66	  minuteAtHourStart: number;
    67	}
    68	
    69	export function createClockCache(): ClockCache {
    70	  return { hourStart: Number.NaN, minuteAtHourStart: 0 };
    71	}
    72	
    73	/** The bar's start minute of day (0..1439) in the resolved clock, or null for an unusable timestamp. */
    74	export function slotKeyOf(tsOpen: number, clock: ResolvedRvolClock, cache?: ClockCache): number | null {
    75	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(tsOpen) || tsOpen < 0) return null;
    76	  if (clock === "utc") return Math.floor((tsOpen % DAY_MS) / MINUTE_MS);
    77	  const hourStart = Math.floor(tsOpen / HOUR_MS) * HOUR_MS;
    78	  let minuteAtHourStart: number;
    79	  if (cache !== undefined && cache.hourStart === hourStart) {
    80	    minuteAtHourStart = cache.minuteAtHourStart;
    81	  } else {
    82	    minuteAtHourStart = nyLocalMinuteOfDay(hourStart);
    83	    if (cache !== undefined) {
    84	      cache.hourStart = hourStart;
    85	      cache.minuteAtHourStart = minuteAtHourStart;
    86	    }
    87	  }
    88	  return (minuteAtHourStart + Math.floor((tsOpen - hourStart) / MINUTE_MS)) % MINUTES_PER_DAY;
    89	}
    90	
    91	/** A finite volume at or above zero; anything else contributes no observation. */
    92	export function usableVolume(volume: unknown): number | null {
    93	  return typeof volume === "number" && Number.isFinite(volume) && volume >= 0 ? volume : null;
    94	}
    95	
    96	export interface Observation {
    97	  tsOpen: number;
    98	  volume: number;
    99	}
   100	
   101	export interface ObservationRing {
   102	  readonly capacity: number;
   103	  /** Oldest first; never longer than `capacity`. */
   104	  readonly items: Observation[];
   105	  /** What the newest insertion pushed out, kept until that tail is superseded so a correction can restore it. */
   106	  evictedByTail: Observation | null;
   107	}
   108	
   109	export function createRing(capacity: number): ObservationRing {
   110	  const size = Number.isFinite(capacity) ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(capacity)) : 1;
   111	  return { capacity: size, items: [], evictedByTail: null };
   112	}
   113	
   114	export function ringInsert(ring: ObservationRing, observation: Observation): void {
   115	  ring.items.push(observation);
   116	  if (ring.items.length > ring.capacity) ring.evictedByTail = ring.items.shift() ?? null;
   117	}
   118	
   119	/** Changes the volume of the observation filed under `tsOpen` in place; false when none is filed. */
   120	export function ringUpdate(ring: ObservationRing, tsOpen: number, volume: number): boolean {
   121	  for (let index = ring.items.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
   122	    if (ring.items[index].tsOpen === tsOpen) {
   123	      ring.items[index].volume = volume;
   124	      return true;
   125	    }
   126	  }
   127	  return false;
   128	}
   129	
   130	/** Removes the observation filed under `tsOpen` and restores what its insertion evicted. */
   131	export function ringRemove(ring: ObservationRing, tsOpen: number): boolean {
   132	  for (let index = ring.items.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
   133	    if (ring.items[index].tsOpen !== tsOpen) continue;
   134	    ring.items.splice(index, 1);
   135	    if (ring.evictedByTail !== null) {
   136	      ring.items.unshift(ring.evictedByTail);
   137	      ring.evictedByTail = null;
   138	    }
   139	    return true;
   140	  }
   141	  return false;
   142	}
   143	
   144	/** The tail that inserted into this ring has been superseded by a newer bar: its eviction is final. */
   145	export function ringSupersede(ring: ObservationRing): void {
   146	  ring.evictedByTail = null;
   147	}
   148	
   149	/** Median of the ring's volumes (mean of the two middle values for an even count); null when empty. */
   150	export function ringMedian(ring: ObservationRing, scratch: number[] = []): number | null {
   151	  const count = ring.items.length;
   152	  if (count === 0) return null;
   153	  scratch.length = count;
   154	  for (let index = 0; index < count; index += 1) {
   155	    // Insertion sort: the ring holds at most 60 values and this runs once per bar.
   156	    const value = ring.items[index].volume;
   157	    let position = index;
   158	    while (position > 0 && scratch[position - 1] > value) {
   159	      scratch[position] = scratch[position - 1];
   160	      position -= 1;
   161	    }
   162	    scratch[position] = value;
   163	  }
   164	  const middle = count >> 1;
   165	  const median = count % 2 === 1 ? scratch[middle] : (scratch[middle - 1] + scratch[middle]) / 2;
   166	  return Number.isFinite(median) ? median : null;
   167	}
   168	
   169	export function ringMean(ring: ObservationRing): number | null {
   170	  const count = ring.items.length;
   171	  if (count === 0) return null;
   172	  let sum = 0;
   173	  for (let index = 0; index < count; index += 1) sum += ring.items[index].volume;
   174	  const mean = sum / count;
   175	  return Number.isFinite(mean) ? mean : null;
   176	}
   177	
   178	/** What the baselines were before this bar's observation existed: read once, stored on the bar record. */
   179	export interface BaselineAtOpen {
   180	  slotMedian: number | null;
   181	  slotMean: number | null;
   182	  priorCount: number;
   183	  rollingMean: number | null;
   184	  rollingCount: number;
   185	}
   186	
   187	export interface TailRecord {
   188	  tsOpen: number;
   189	  /** Start minute of day in the resolved clock; null when the bar cannot be keyed. */
   190	  key: number | null;
   191	  /** False for a timeframe longer than one hour or an unkeyable bar: nothing is filed, every output is null. */
   192	  supported: boolean;
   193	  /** Last known volume, null when the latest dispatch carried no usable volume. */
   194	  volume: number | null;
   195	  baselineAtOpen: BaselineAtOpen;
   196	  /** The commit event (the first closed dispatch of this bar) has happened. */
   197	  committed: boolean;
   198	  /** An observation for this bar currently sits in both rings. */
   199	  inRings: boolean;
   200	  /** A closed dispatch has been seen: outputs are confirmed, not provisional. */
   201	  confirmed: boolean;
   202	}
   203	
   204	export type RvolStatus = "unsupported" | "noVolume" | "learning" | "noBaseline" | "forming" | "ready";
   205	
   206	export interface RvolReading {
   207	  tsOpen: number;
   208	  key: number | null;
   209	  volume: number | null;
   210	  /** volume / median of the slot ring as it stood when this bar opened. */
   211	  rvol: number | null;
   212	  /** volume / mean of the last 20 committed bars as they stood when this bar opened. */
   213	  rolling: number | null;
   214	  /** volume / mean of the same slot observations the median uses (the baseline comparison's second method). */
   215	  slotMeanRatio: number | null;
   216	  priorCount: number;
   217	  rollingCount: number;
   218	  /** `minSessions` clamped to `sessions`: what `priorCount` must reach. */
   219	  requiredPrior: number;
   220	  confirmed: boolean;
   221	  status: RvolStatus;
   222	}
   223	
   224	export interface RelativeVolumeEngine {
   225	  readonly settings: RelativeVolumeMathSettings;
   226	  /** Feeds one dispatch of a bar and returns the reading for the bar of this call (the tail). */
   227	  onBar(bar: BarFrame): RvolReading;
   228	  readonly current: RvolReading | null;
   229	  readonly tail: TailRecord | null;
   230	  readonly rollingRing: ObservationRing;
   231	  slotRing(key: number): ObservationRing | undefined;
   232	  readonly slotCount: number;
   233	}
   234	
   235	/** The slot ring capacity: `sessions` brought into 3..60. */
   236	export function ringCapacityOf(settings: RelativeVolumeMathSettings): number {
   237	  if (!Number.isFinite(settings.sessions)) return RVOL_DEFAULT_SESSIONS;
   238	  return Math.min(RVOL_MAX_SESSIONS_SETTING, Math.max(RVOL_MIN_SESSIONS_SETTING, Math.floor(settings.sessions)));
   239	}
   240	
   241	export function requiredPriorOf(settings: RelativeVolumeMathSettings): number {
   242	  const minimum = Number.isFinite(settings.minSessions) ? Math.floor(settings.minSessions) : RVOL_DEFAULT_MIN_SESSIONS;
   243	  return Math.max(1, Math.min(minimum, ringCapacityOf(settings)));
   244	}
   245	
   246	function ratioOf(volume: number, denominator: number | null): number | null {
   247	  if (denominator === null || !(denominator > 0)) return null;
   248	  const ratio = volume / denominator;
   249	  return Number.isFinite(ratio) ? ratio : null;
   250	}
   251	
   252	/** The reading for a tail record: rule 9 (outputs) and rule 11 (status precedence, first match wins). */
   253	export function readingOf(tail: TailRecord, settings: RelativeVolumeMathSettings): RvolReading {
   254	  const requiredPrior = requiredPriorOf(settings);
   255	  const base = tail.baselineAtOpen;
   256	  const reading: RvolReading = {
   257	    tsOpen: tail.tsOpen,
   258	    key: tail.key,
   259	    volume: tail.volume,
   260	    rvol: null,
   261	    rolling: null,
   262	    slotMeanRatio: null,
   263	    priorCount: base.priorCount,
   264	    rollingCount: base.rollingCount,
   265	    requiredPrior,
   266	    confirmed: tail.confirmed,
   267	    status: "ready",
   268	  };
   269	  if (!tail.supported) {
   270	    reading.status = "unsupported";
   271	    return reading;
   272	  }
   273	  if (tail.volume === null) {
   274	    reading.status = "noVolume";
   275	    return reading;
   276	  }
   277	  reading.rolling = base.rollingCount >= RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH ? ratioOf(tail.volume, base.rollingMean) : null;
   278	  if (base.priorCount < requiredPrior) {
   279	    reading.status = "learning";
   280	    return reading;
   281	  }
   282	  reading.slotMeanRatio = ratioOf(tail.volume, base.slotMean);
   283	  reading.rvol = ratioOf(tail.volume, base.slotMedian);
   284	  if (reading.rvol === null) {
   285	    reading.status = "noBaseline";
   286	    return reading;
   287	  }
   288	  reading.status = tail.confirmed ? "ready" : "forming";
   289	  return reading;
   290	}
   291	
   292	export function createRelativeVolumeEngine(settings: RelativeVolumeMathSettings): RelativeVolumeEngine {
   293	  const capacity = ringCapacityOf(settings);
   294	  const slots = new Map<number, ObservationRing>();
   295	  const rolling = createRing(RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH);
   296	  const clockCache = createClockCache();
   297	  const scratch: number[] = [];
   298	  let tail: TailRecord | null = null;
   299	  let current: RvolReading | null = null;
   300	
   301	  function slotFor(key: number): ObservationRing {
   302	    let ring = slots.get(key);
   303	    if (ring === undefined) {
   304	      ring = createRing(capacity);
   305	      slots.set(key, ring);
   306	    }
   307	    return ring;
   308	  }
   309	
   310	  function readBaseline(key: number | null): BaselineAtOpen {
   311	    const slot = key === null ? undefined : slots.get(key);
   312	    return {
   313	      slotMedian: slot === undefined ? null : ringMedian(slot, scratch),
   314	      slotMean: slot === undefined ? null : ringMean(slot),
   315	      priorCount: slot === undefined ? 0 : slot.items.length,
   316	      rollingMean: ringMean(rolling),
   317	      rollingCount: rolling.items.length,
   318	    };
   319	  }
   320	
   321	  /** Rule 7(a): the commit event for the tail on its first closed dispatch, filing its volume when it is usable. */
   322	  function commit(record: TailRecord): void {
   323	    record.committed = true;
   324	    if (!record.supported || record.key === null || record.volume === null) return;
   325	    const observation = { tsOpen: record.tsOpen, volume: record.volume };
   326	    ringInsert(slotFor(record.key), observation);
   327	    ringInsert(rolling, { tsOpen: record.tsOpen, volume: record.volume });
   328	    record.inRings = true;
   329	  }
   330	
   331	  /** A later dispatch of a committed tail: update in place, remove (valid to invalid) or insert (invalid to valid). */
   332	  function reconcile(record: TailRecord): void {
   333	    if (!record.supported || record.key === null) return;
   334	    const slot = slotFor(record.key);
   335	    if (record.volume !== null && record.inRings) {
   336	      ringUpdate(slot, record.tsOpen, record.volume);
   337	      ringUpdate(rolling, record.tsOpen, record.volume);
   338	    } else if (record.volume !== null) {
   339	      ringInsert(slot, { tsOpen: record.tsOpen, volume: record.volume });
   340	      ringInsert(rolling, { tsOpen: record.tsOpen, volume: record.volume });
   341	      record.inRings = true;
   342	    } else if (record.inRings) {
   343	      ringRemove(slot, record.tsOpen);
   344	      ringRemove(rolling, record.tsOpen);
   345	      record.inRings = false;
   346	    }
   347	  }
   348	
   349	  function supersede(record: TailRecord): void {
   350	    if (record.key !== null) {
   351	      const slot = slots.get(record.key);
   352	      if (slot !== undefined) ringSupersede(slot);
   353	    }
   354	    ringSupersede(rolling);
   355	  }
   356	
   357	  function onBar(bar: BarFrame): RvolReading {
   358	    const tsOpen = bar.ts_open;
   359	    if (tail !== null && tsOpen < tail.tsOpen) {
   360	      // A dispatch older than the current record: the runtime replays those, so here it is ignored.
   361	      return current ?? readingOf(tail, settings);
   362	    }
   363	    if (tail === null || tsOpen > tail.tsOpen) {
   364	      // A tail superseded without ever closing files nothing (rule 7(b) was withdrawn): only a closed bar is an observation.
   365	      if (tail !== null) supersede(tail);
   366	      const intervalOk = Number.isFinite(bar.interval_ms) && bar.interval_ms > 0 && bar.interval_ms <= RVOL_MAX_INTERVAL_MS;
   367	      const key = intervalOk ? slotKeyOf(tsOpen, resolveRvolClock(settings.clock, bar.provider), clockCache) : null;
   368	      tail = {
   369	        tsOpen,
   370	        key,
   371	        supported: intervalOk && key !== null,
   372	        volume: usableVolume(bar.volume),
   373	        baselineAtOpen: readBaseline(key),
   374	        committed: false,
   375	        inRings: false,
   376	        confirmed: false,
   377	      };
   378	    } else {
   379	      tail.volume = usableVolume(bar.volume);
   380	    }
   381	    if (bar.closed === true) tail.confirmed = true;
   382	    if (!tail.committed) {
   383	      if (bar.closed === true) commit(tail); // rule 7(a)
   384	    } else {
   385	      reconcile(tail);
   386	    }
   387	    current = readingOf(tail, settings);
   388	    return current;
   389	  }
   390	
   391	  return {
   392	    settings,
   393	    onBar,
   394	    get current() {
   395	      return current;
   396	    },
   397	    get tail() {
   398	      return tail;
   399	    },
   400	    rollingRing: rolling,
   401	    slotRing: (key) => slots.get(key),
   402	    get slotCount() {
   403	      return slots.size;
   404	    },
   405	  };
   406	}

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../types/frames";
import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/registry";

const T0 = Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2); // Monday 2026-03-02 00:00 UTC
const EQUITY_SESSION_STARTS = relativeVolumeEquityFixture().labels.sessionStarts;

function hourlyBar(day: number, hour: number, volume: number, closed = true): BarFrame {
  return barFrame(T0 + day * 24 * HOUR_MS + hour * HOUR_MS, { open: 100, close: 100, high: 100, low: 100, volume }, BINANCE_PERP, HOUR_MS, closed);
}

/**
 * The hand-computed three-session fixture of success criterion 1. Hourly UTC
 * bars (24 slots a day), base volume 100 everywhere except slot 0:
 * day 1: 100, day 2: 300, day 3: 200, day 4: 500.
 */
function threeSessionBars(): BarFrame[] {
  const slotZero = [100, 300, 200, 500];
  const bars: BarFrame[] = [];
  for (let day = 0; day < 4; day += 1) {
    for (let hour = 0; hour < 24; hour += 1) {
      bars.push(hourlyBar(day, hour, hour === 0 ? slotZero[day] : 100));
    }
  }
  return bars;
}

const SETTINGS: RelativeVolumeMathSettings = { sessions: 10, minSessions: 2, clock: "utc" };

describe("relativeVolumeMath -- clock and slot key", () => {
  it("auto resolves to New York for dxFeed and Databento and to UTC for Binance", () => {
    expect(resolveRvolClock("auto", "DXFEED")).toBe("newYork");
    expect(resolveRvolClock("auto", "DATABENTO")).toBe("newYork");
    expect(resolveRvolClock("auto", "BINANCE")).toBe("utc");
    expect(resolveRvolClock("newYork", "BINANCE")).toBe("newYork");
    expect(resolveRvolClock("utc", "DXFEED")).toBe("utc");
  });

  it("keys a bar by its start minute of day with no division by bar length (hourly 09:30 keys 570, the 15:30 half hour keys 930)", () => {
    const open = Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2, 14, 30); // 09:30 New York, EST
    expect(slotKeyOf(open, "newYork")).toBe(570);
    expect(slotKeyOf(open + 6 * HOUR_MS, "newYork")).toBe(930);
    expect(slotKeyOf(open, "utc")).toBe(870);
    expect(slotKeyOf(Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2, 0, 0), "utc")).toBe(0);
    expect(slotKeyOf(Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2, 23, 59), "utc")).toBe(1_439);
  });

  it("agrees with the killzones New York clock through the DST change, with and without the hour cache", () => {
    const cache = createClockCache();
    const from = Date.UTC(2026, 2, 7, 0, 0);
    for (let ts = from; ts < from + 3 * 24 * HOUR_MS; ts += 7 * MINUTE_MS) {
      const expected = nyLocalMinuteOfDay(ts);
      expect(slotKeyOf(ts, "newYork", cache)).toBe(expected);
      expect(slotKeyOf(ts, "newYork")).toBe(expected);
    }
  });

  it("the equity fixture's New York start-minute keys are identical before and after the DST change while the UTC keys differ", () => {
    const starts = EQUITY_SESSION_STARTS;
    for (const start of starts) expect(nyLocalMinuteOfDay(start)).toBe(570);
    expect(slotKeyOf(starts[0], "newYork")).toBe(slotKeyOf(starts[5], "newYork"));
    expect(slotKeyOf(starts[0], "utc")).toBe(870);
    expect(slotKeyOf(starts[5], "utc")).toBe(810);
  });

  it("rejects an unusable timestamp and an unusable volume", () => {
    expect(slotKeyOf(Number.NaN, "utc")).toBeNull();
    expect(slotKeyOf(-1, "newYork")).toBeNull();
    expect(usableVolume(0)).toBe(0);
    expect(usableVolume(12.5)).toBe(12.5);
    expect(usableVolume(-1)).toBeNull();
    expect(usableVolume(Number.NaN)).toBeNull();
    expect(usableVolume(Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)).toBeNull();
    expect(usableVolume(undefined)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("relativeVolumeMath -- observation rings", () => {
  it("keeps the last N observations, reports the exact median (even count: mean of the two middle values) and the mean", () => {
    const ring = createRing(3);
    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 1, volume: 100 });
    expect(ringMedian(ring)).toBe(100);
    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 2, volume: 300 });
    expect(ringMedian(ring)).toBe(200);
    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 3, volume: 200 });
    expect(ringMedian(ring)).toBe(200);
    expect(ringMean(ring)).toBe(200);
    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 4, volume: 900 });
    expect(ring.items.map((item) => item.tsOpen)).toEqual([2, 3, 4]);
    expect(ring.evictedByTail).toEqual({ tsOpen: 1, volume: 100 });
    expect(ringMedian(ring)).toBe(300);
    expect(ringMedian(createRing(2))).toBeNull();
  });

  it("a removal restores what the tail's insertion evicted; a supersede makes the eviction final", () => {
    const ring = createRing(2);
    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 1, volume: 10 });
    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 2, volume: 20 });
    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 3, volume: 30 });
    expect(ringRemove(ring, 3)).toBe(true);
    expect(ring.items).toEqual([{ tsOpen: 1, volume: 10 }, { tsOpen: 2, volume: 20 }]);
    expect(ring.evictedByTail).toBeNull();
    ringInsert(ring, { tsOpen: 3, volume: 33 });
    ringSupersede(ring);
    expect(ring.evictedByTail).toBeNull();
    expect(ringRemove(ring, 3)).toBe(true);
    expect(ring.items).toEqual([{ tsOpen: 2, volume: 20 }]);
    expect(ringRemove(ring, 99)).toBe(false);
    expect(ringUpdate(ring, 2, 25)).toBe(true);
    expect(ring.items[0].volume).toBe(25);
    expect(ringUpdate(ring, 99, 1)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("relativeVolumeMath -- hand-computed three-session fixture (success criterion 1)", () => {
  it("median baseline, warm-up nulls and the rolling comparison take the exact hand-computed values", () => {
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
    const bars = threeSessionBars();
    const readings = bars.map((bar) => engine.onBar(bar));
    // Day 1: nothing prior anywhere.
    for (let index = 0; index < 24; index += 1) {
      expect(readings[index].rvol).toBeNull();
      expect(readings[index].status).toBe("learning");
      if (index < 20) expect(readings[index].rolling).toBeNull();
    }
    // Day 2 slot 0 (300): one prior (100) < minSessions 2 -> learning; rolling over the 20 previous bars (all 100) = 3.
    expect(readings[24].priorCount).toBe(1);
    expect(readings[24].rvol).toBeNull();
    expect(readings[24].status).toBe("learning");
    expect(readings[24].rollingCount).toBe(20);
    expect(readings[24].rolling).toBe(3);
    // The first bar with 20 committed predecessors is index 20.
    expect(readings[19].rolling).toBeNull();
    expect(readings[20].rolling).toBe(1);
    // Day 3 slot 0 (200): prior [100, 300], even count -> median 200 -> 1.0; rolling 2.0.
    expect(readings[48].priorCount).toBe(2);
    expect(readings[48].rvol).toBe(1);
    expect(readings[48].slotMeanRatio).toBe(1);
    expect(readings[48].rolling).toBe(2);
    expect(readings[48].status).toBe("ready");
    // Day 3 slot 1 (100): prior [100, 100] -> 1.0.
    expect(readings[49].rvol).toBe(1);
    // Day 4 slot 0 (500): prior [100, 300, 200] -> median 200 -> 2.5; rolling 5.
    expect(readings[72].priorCount).toBe(3);
    expect(readings[72].rvol).toBe(2.5);
    expect(readings[72].slotMeanRatio).toBe(2.5);
    expect(readings[72].rolling).toBe(5);
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 300, 200, 500]);
    expect(engine.rollingRing.items).toHaveLength(20);
    for (const reading of readings) {
      expect(reading.rvol === null || Number.isFinite(reading.rvol)).toBe(true);
      expect(reading.rolling === null || Number.isFinite(reading.rolling)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it("the forming bar grows toward its final value against the full-bar expectation and keeps its stored denominator after the close", () => {
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
    const bars = threeSessionBars();
    for (const bar of bars.slice(0, 72)) engine.onBar(bar);
    const forming = (volume: number, closed: boolean) => engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, volume, closed));
    const first = forming(100, false);
    expect(first.rvol).toBe(0.5);
    expect(first.status).toBe("forming");
    expect(first.confirmed).toBe(false);
    expect(engine.tail!.committed).toBe(false);
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(forming(300, false).rvol).toBe(1.5);
    const closed = forming(500, true);
    expect(closed.rvol).toBe(2.5);
    expect(closed.status).toBe("ready");
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 300, 200, 500]);
    // A later closed dispatch with a different volume divides by the stored 200, never by a median that includes itself.
    const corrected = forming(1_000, true);
    expect(corrected.rvol).toBe(5);
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 300, 200, 1_000]);
    expect(engine.rollingRing.items[19].volume).toBe(1_000);
    // Day 5 slot 0 then sees prior [100, 300, 200, 1000] -> median 250.
    const next = engine.onBar(hourlyBar(4, 0, 500));
    expect(next.priorCount).toBe(4);
    expect(next.rvol).toBe(2);
  });

  it("a duplicate identical closed dispatch changes nothing", () => {
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
    const bars = threeSessionBars();
    for (const bar of bars.slice(0, 73)) engine.onBar(bar);
    const before = JSON.stringify({ reading: engine.current, slot: engine.slotRing(0), rolling: engine.rollingRing });
    engine.onBar(bars[72]);
    expect(JSON.stringify({ reading: engine.current, slot: engine.slotRing(0), rolling: engine.rollingRing })).toBe(before);
  });

  it("a dispatch older than the current record is ignored", () => {
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
    const bars = threeSessionBars();
    for (const bar of bars.slice(0, 73)) engine.onBar(bar);
    const current = engine.current;
    expect(engine.onBar({ ...bars[10], volume: 9_999 })).toBe(current);
    expect(engine.slotRing(600)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 100, 100]);
  });

  it("a bar superseded without ever closing contributes no observation to either ring and stays provisional (rule 7(b) withdrawn)", () => {
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
    const bars = threeSessionBars();
    for (const bar of bars.slice(0, 72)) engine.onBar(bar);
    const open = engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, 400, false));
    expect(open.confirmed).toBe(false);
    expect(open.status).toBe("forming");
    expect(open.rvol).toBe(2);
    const next = engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 1, 100));
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 300, 200]);
    expect(engine.rollingRing.items.some((item) => item.tsOpen === T0 + 3 * 24 * HOUR_MS)).toBe(false);
    expect(engine.rollingRing.items[19]).toEqual({ tsOpen: T0 + 3 * 24 * HOUR_MS + HOUR_MS, volume: 100 });
    // The superseded bar never entered the rolling mean either: the next bar's twenty predecessors are all 100.
    expect(next.rolling).toBe(1);
    // Day 5 slot 0 still sees three prior observations.
    expect(engine.onBar(hourlyBar(4, 0, 200)).priorCount).toBe(3);
  });
});

describe("relativeVolumeMath -- corrections and eviction rollback (amendments 1 and 2)", () => {
  it("valid to invalid removes the observation and yields null; invalid to valid inserts it", () => {
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
    for (const bar of threeSessionBars().slice(0, 72)) engine.onBar(bar);
    engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, 500));
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items).toHaveLength(4);
    const invalid = engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, Number.NaN));
    expect(invalid.rvol).toBeNull();
    expect(invalid.rolling).toBeNull();
    expect(invalid.status).toBe("noVolume");
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 300, 200]);
    expect(engine.rollingRing.items).toHaveLength(20);
    expect(engine.rollingRing.items[19].tsOpen).toBe(T0 + 2 * 24 * HOUR_MS + 23 * HOUR_MS);
    const valid = engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, 600));
    expect(valid.rvol).toBe(3);
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([100, 300, 200, 600]);
  });

  it("valid, invalid, valid with both rings full restores the evicted observation and ends equal to a feed that only saw the final state", () => {
    const settings: RelativeVolumeMathSettings = { sessions: 3, minSessions: 2, clock: "utc" };
    const bars = threeSessionBars(); // 4 days: slot rings reach capacity 3 at day 4; the rolling ring is full after 20 bars
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(settings);
    for (const bar of bars.slice(0, 72)) engine.onBar(bar);
    const slotBefore = JSON.stringify(engine.slotRing(0)!.items);
    const rollingBefore = JSON.stringify(engine.rollingRing.items);
    engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, 500));
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([300, 200, 500]);
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.evictedByTail).toEqual({ tsOpen: T0, volume: 100 });
    expect(engine.rollingRing.evictedByTail).not.toBeNull();
    engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, Number.NaN));
    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.slotRing(0)!.items)).toBe(slotBefore);
    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.rollingRing.items)).toBe(rollingBefore);
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.evictedByTail).toBeNull();
    const final = engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, 700));
    engine.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 1, 100));
    const clean = createRelativeVolumeEngine(settings);
    for (const bar of bars.slice(0, 72)) clean.onBar(bar);
    const cleanFinal = clean.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 0, 700));
    clean.onBar(hourlyBar(3, 1, 100));
    expect(final.rvol).toBe(cleanFinal.rvol);
    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.slotRing(0))).toBe(JSON.stringify(clean.slotRing(0)));
    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.rollingRing)).toBe(JSON.stringify(clean.rollingRing));
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.evictedByTail).toBeNull();
  });

  it("minSessions is clamped to sessions at read time, and sessions itself to 3..60", () => {
    expect(requiredPriorOf({ sessions: 4, minSessions: 5, clock: "utc" })).toBe(4);
    expect(requiredPriorOf({ sessions: 2, minSessions: 5, clock: "utc" })).toBe(3);
    expect(requiredPriorOf({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "utc" })).toBe(3);
    expect(requiredPriorOf({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 0, clock: "utc" })).toBe(1);
    expect(ringCapacityOf({ sessions: 2, minSessions: 1, clock: "utc" })).toBe(3);
    expect(ringCapacityOf({ sessions: 61, minSessions: 1, clock: "utc" })).toBe(60);
    expect(createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 2, minSessions: 2, clock: "utc" }).rollingRing.capacity).toBe(20);
  });
});

describe("relativeVolumeMath -- status precedence (rule 11)", () => {
  const base = { tsOpen: 0, key: 0, supported: true, volume: 100, committed: true, inRings: true, confirmed: true };
  const baseline = { slotMedian: 50, slotMean: 50, priorCount: 3, rollingMean: 50, rollingCount: 20 };

  it("unsupported timeframe, then no usable volume, then learning, then no usable baseline, then forming, then ready", () => {
    expect(readingOf({ ...base, supported: false, volume: null, baselineAtOpen: { ...baseline, priorCount: 0 } }, SETTINGS).status).toBe("unsupported");
    expect(readingOf({ ...base, volume: null, baselineAtOpen: { ...baseline, priorCount: 0 } }, SETTINGS).status).toBe("noVolume");
    const learning = readingOf({ ...base, baselineAtOpen: { ...baseline, priorCount: 1, slotMedian: 0 } }, SETTINGS);
    expect(learning.status).toBe("learning");
    expect(learning.rvol).toBeNull();
    expect(learning.rolling).toBe(2);
    const noBaseline = readingOf({ ...base, baselineAtOpen: { ...baseline, slotMedian: 0 } }, SETTINGS);
    expect(noBaseline.status).toBe("noBaseline");
    expect(noBaseline.rvol).toBeNull();
    expect(readingOf({ ...base, confirmed: false, baselineAtOpen: baseline }, SETTINGS).status).toBe("forming");
    const ready = readingOf({ ...base, baselineAtOpen: baseline }, SETTINGS);
    expect(ready.status).toBe("ready");
    expect(ready.rvol).toBe(2);
    expect(ready.requiredPrior).toBe(2);
  });

  it("a timeframe longer than one hour yields null everywhere and files nothing", () => {
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
    const fourHours = 4 * HOUR_MS;
    for (let index = 0; index < 30; index += 1) {
      const reading = engine.onBar(barFrame(T0 + index * fourHours, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: 100 }, BINANCE_PERP, fourHours));
      expect(reading.status).toBe("unsupported");
      expect(reading.rvol).toBeNull();
      expect(reading.rolling).toBeNull();
    }
    expect(engine.slotCount).toBe(0);
    expect(engine.rollingRing.items).toHaveLength(0);
    const hourly = createRelativeVolumeEngine(SETTINGS);
    expect(hourly.onBar(hourlyBar(0, 0, 100)).status).toBe("learning");
  });
});

describe("relativeVolumeMath -- gaps, early closes and the resampler", () => {
  function equitySession(dayIndex: number, volumeOf: (minute: number) => number, minutes: readonly number[]): BarFrame[] {
    const open = EQUITY_SESSION_STARTS[dayIndex];
    return minutes.map((minute) => barFrame(open + minute * MINUTE_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: volumeOf(minute) }, DXFEED_EQUITY));
  }

  it("a session missing minutes 120..179 contributes no observations for those keys and the bar after the gap has an unchanged baseline", () => {
    const all = Array.from({ length: 390 }, (_, minute) => minute);
    const gapped = all.filter((minute) => minute < 120 || minute > 179);
    const volumeOf = (minute: number) => 100 + minute;
    const withGap = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 2, clock: "auto" });
    const without = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 2, clock: "auto" });
    for (const day of [0, 1]) {
      for (const bar of equitySession(day, volumeOf, all)) {
        withGap.onBar(bar);
        without.onBar(bar);
      }
    }
    let afterGap = null;
    for (const bar of equitySession(2, volumeOf, gapped)) {
      const reading = withGap.onBar(bar);
      if (bar.ts_open === EQUITY_SESSION_STARTS[2] + 180 * MINUTE_MS) afterGap = reading;
    }
    let reference = null;
    for (const bar of equitySession(2, volumeOf, all)) {
      const reading = without.onBar(bar);
      if (bar.ts_open === EQUITY_SESSION_STARTS[2] + 180 * MINUTE_MS) reference = reading;
    }
    for (let minute = 120; minute <= 179; minute += 1) {
      expect(withGap.slotRing(570 + minute)!.items).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(without.slotRing(570 + minute)!.items).toHaveLength(3);
    }
    expect(afterGap!.rvol).toBe(reference!.rvol);
    expect(afterGap!.priorCount).toBe(2);
    expect(withGap.slotRing(750)!.items).toHaveLength(3);
    // The rolling ring sees the bar before the gap as the previous committed bar: no zero is ever filed.
    expect(withGap.rollingRing.items.every((item) => item.volume > 0)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("an early close at 13:00 enters its key as one observation and the next session's bar divides by the exact median (amendment 4)", () => {
    // Hourly equity bars: keys 570, 630, 690, 750, 810, 870, 930. Slot 750 (12:30) is a full hour on
    // ordinary days and a 30-minute bucket on the early-close day.
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 4, minSessions: 2, clock: "auto" });
    const starts = EQUITY_SESSION_STARTS;
    const hourlyVolume: Record<number, number[]> = { 750: [60, 62, 58] };
    const feed = (day: number, hours: number, slot750: number) => {
      for (let hour = 0; hour < hours; hour += 1) {
        const ts = starts[day] + hour * HOUR_MS;
        const key = 570 + hour * 60;
        const volume = key === 750 ? slot750 : 50;
        engine.onBar(barFrame(ts, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume }, DXFEED_EQUITY, HOUR_MS));
      }
    };
    feed(0, 7, hourlyVolume[750][0]);
    feed(1, 7, hourlyVolume[750][1]);
    feed(2, 7, hourlyVolume[750][2]);
    feed(3, 4, 30); // early close: the 12:30 bucket ends at 13:00 and is the session's last bar
    expect(engine.slotRing(750)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([60, 62, 58, 30]);
    expect(engine.slotRing(810)!.items).toHaveLength(3);
    const reading = engine.onBar(barFrame(starts[4] + 3 * HOUR_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: 59 }, DXFEED_EQUITY, HOUR_MS));
    // sorted [30, 58, 60, 62] -> median (58 + 60) / 2 = 59: the short bar moves the median from 60 to 59, it does not zero it.
    expect(reading.priorCount).toBe(4);
    expect(reading.rvol).toBe(59 / 59);
    expect(reading.slotMeanRatio).toBe(59 / 52.5);
  });

  it("one session of 1m fixture bars through the real resampler to 1h ends on a 15:30 bar that keys 930 and is its own slot", () => {
    const { bars } = relativeVolumeEquityFixture();
    const hourly = resampleBars(bars.slice(0, 390), HOUR_MS);
    expect(hourly).toHaveLength(7);
    const last = hourly[hourly.length - 1];
    expect(last.interval_ms).toBe(HOUR_MS); // the resampler reports the nominal interval even for the half-hour bucket
    expect(last.closed).toBe(true);
    expect(slotKeyOf(last.ts_open, "newYork")).toBe(930);
    expect(hourly.map((bar) => slotKeyOf(bar.ts_open, "newYork"))).toEqual([570, 630, 690, 750, 810, 870, 930]);
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
    for (let session = 0; session < 4; session += 1) {
      for (const bar of resampleBars(bars.slice(session * 390, (session + 1) * 390), HOUR_MS)) engine.onBar(bar);
    }
    expect(engine.slotRing(930)!.items).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(engine.slotRing(930)!.items.every((item) => slotKeyOf(item.tsOpen, "newYork") === 930)).toBe(true);
    expect(engine.slotRing(870)!.items.every((item) => slotKeyOf(item.tsOpen, "newYork") === 870)).toBe(true);
    expect(engine.slotCount).toBe(7);
  });

  it("the crypto fixture keeps at most 1,440 keys, every ring at or under capacity, across all 12,000 bars", () => {
    const { bars } = relativeVolumeCryptoFixture();
    expect(bars).toHaveLength(12_000);
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 3, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
    let ready = 0;
    for (const bar of bars) {
      const reading = engine.onBar(bar);
      if (reading.rvol !== null) ready += 1;
    }
    expect(engine.slotCount).toBe(1_440);
    for (let key = 0; key < 1_440; key += 1) expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(3);
    expect(engine.rollingRing.items).toHaveLength(20);
    // Eight days and eight hours: the first three days learn, the rest are ready.
    expect(ready).toBe(12_000 - 3 * 1_440);
  });
});
