const ELEVEN_DAYS = minuteBars(11 * 1_440, (index) => (index === 11 * 1_440 - 1 ? 450 : index < 5 * 1_440 ? 100 : 300));

describe("Codex item 1: a colour change replays and can change a number (reproduced on ce8d11c; ruling: builder must fix, applied in fix round 1: the pickers are gone and a settings update that changes no recompute key never replays)", () => {
  it("a settings update carrying only a stale colour key leaves the latest ratio at 2.25x and keeps its confirmed marker", () => {
    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, []);
    try {
      for (const bar of ELEVEN_DAYS) session.dispatch({ bar });
      expect(session.bars).toHaveLength(10_080);
      const before = session.capture();
      expect(before.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(2.25);
      expect(before.markers.some((call) => call.time === ELEVEN_DAYS[ELEVEN_DAYS.length - 1].ts_open)).toBe(true);
      session.updateSettings({ ...SETTINGS, rollingColor: "#123456" });
      const after = session.capture();
      // Live state holds ten prior observations for the slot (five at 100, five at 300: median 200); a replay over
      // the retained seven days would hold six (median 300) and read 1.5x. On ce8d11c this update replayed; it no longer does.
      expect(after.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(2.25);
      expect(after.outputs).toEqual(before.outputs);
      expect(after.markers).toEqual(before.markers);
    } finally {
      session.close();
    }
  }, 120_000);
});

describe("Codex item 2: default lookback 10 exceeds one-minute crypto retention (reproduced; ruling: accepted, document, add this test)", () => {
  it("after eleven days live, a replay of the retained bars and an unchanged older duplicate (REBUILT) both change the latest ratio", () => {
    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, []);
    try {
      for (const bar of ELEVEN_DAYS) session.dispatch({ bar });
      const live = session.capture();
      const retained = [...session.bars];
      const replay = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, retained, SETTINGS);
      expect(live.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(2.25);
      expect(replay.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(1.5);
      // Prior observations within the retained seven days for the last slot: days 5..10 only.
      const slotVolumes = retained.filter((bar, index) => index < retained.length - 1 && bar.ts_open % (24 * HOUR_MS) === retained[retained.length - 1].ts_open % (24 * HOUR_MS)).map((bar) => bar.volume);
      expect(slotVolumes).toHaveLength(6);
      expect(450 / medianOf(slotVolumes)).toBe(1.5);
      // An identical closed duplicate of an older retained bar is classified REBUILT and replays.
      const older = retained[100];
      const probe: BarFrame[] = [...retained];
      expect(mergeChartBar(probe, { ...older })).toBe("REBUILT");
      session.dispatch({ bar: { ...older } });
      expect(session.mutations.at(-1)).toBe("REBUILT");
      expect(session.capture().outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(1.5);
    } finally {
      session.close();
    }
  }, 120_000);

  it("the doc's claim that ten fits one-minute crypto is false: 10,080 retained minutes hold at most six prior observations per minute of day", () => {
    expect(Math.floor(10_080 / 1_440)).toBe(7);
    // Seven retained days means a bar on the eighth retained day sees six earlier bars of its minute, never ten.
    const fixture = relativeVolumeCryptoFixture();
    const retained = fixture.bars.slice(fixture.labels.rebuiltFromIndex);
    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, retained, SETTINGS);
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "utc" });

  });

    try {
      samples.length = 0;
      const next = bars[labels.timingIndices.last + 1];

        session.dispatch({ bar: { ...next, volume, closed: revision === 499 } });

        else cold += 1;

      expect(hot).toBe(250);
      expect(cold).toBe(250);

      console.log(`forming bar, warmed slots, 500 threshold crossings: onBar p50 ${forming.p50.toFixed(4)} ms p99 ${forming.p99.toFixed(4)} ms max ${forming.max.toFixed(3)} ms`);
      expect(forming.count).toBe(500);

    } finally {
      session.close();
    }
  }, 120_000);

import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../../types/frames";
import { nyLocalMinuteOfDay } from "../../../killzones/killzones";

/**
 * Validator's from-scratch batch reference for Relative Volume (Time of Day).
 * Written from the brief alone, not from `relativeVolumeMath.ts`: every bar is
 * keyed by its start minute of day, its denominator is the median of the last
 * `sessions` volumes filed under that key by STRICTLY EARLIER closed bars, the
 * rolling comparison is the mean of the previous 20 closed bars, and nothing
 * is incremental. Agreement with the incremental engine on the frozen fixtures
 * is the independent evidence for the stored denominator and the clock.
 */

export interface ReferenceSettings {
  sessions: number;
  minSessions: number;
  clock: "newYork" | "utc";
}

export interface ReferenceRow {
  key: number;
  priorCount: number;
  slotMedian: number | null;
  slotMean: number | null;
  rollingMean: number | null;
  rvol: number | null;
  slotMeanRatio: number | null;
  rolling: number | null;
}

export function referenceKey(tsOpen: number, clock: "newYork" | "utc"): number {
  return clock === "utc" ? Math.floor((tsOpen % 86_400_000) / 60_000) : nyLocalMinuteOfDay(tsOpen);
}

export function medianOf(values: readonly number[]): number {
  const sorted = [...values].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const middle = sorted.length >> 1;
  return sorted.length % 2 === 1 ? sorted[middle] : (sorted[middle - 1] + sorted[middle]) / 2;
}

function meanOf(values: readonly number[]): number {
  let sum = 0;
  for (const value of values) sum += value;
  return sum / values.length;
}

function ratio(volume: number, denominator: number | null): number | null {
  if (denominator === null || !(denominator > 0)) return null;
  const value = volume / denominator;
  return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
}

/** Every bar is treated as closed; a bar with an unusable volume reads null and files nothing. */
export function referenceSeries(bars: readonly BarFrame[], settings: ReferenceSettings): ReferenceRow[] {
  const bySlot = new Map<number, number[]>();
  const committed: number[] = [];
  const rows: ReferenceRow[] = [];
  const required = Math.max(1, Math.min(settings.minSessions, settings.sessions));
  for (const bar of bars) {
    const key = referenceKey(bar.ts_open, settings.clock);
    const prior = (bySlot.get(key) ?? []).slice(-settings.sessions);
    const slotMedian = prior.length > 0 ? medianOf(prior) : null;
    const slotMean = prior.length > 0 ? meanOf(prior) : null;
    const previous = committed.slice(-20);
    const rollingMean = previous.length === 20 ? meanOf(previous) : null;
    const usable = Number.isFinite(bar.volume) && bar.volume >= 0;
    const row: ReferenceRow = {
      key,
      priorCount: prior.length,
      slotMedian,
      slotMean,
      rollingMean,
      rvol: usable && prior.length >= required ? ratio(bar.volume, slotMedian) : null,
      slotMeanRatio: usable && prior.length >= required ? ratio(bar.volume, slotMean) : null,
      rolling: usable ? ratio(bar.volume, rollingMean) : null,
    };
    rows.push(row);
    if (usable) {
      const slot = bySlot.get(key);
      if (slot) slot.push(bar.volume);
      else bySlot.set(key, [bar.volume]);
      committed.push(bar.volume);
    }
  }
  return rows;
}

const OWNER = "test:relativeVolume";

function openState(settings: RelativeVolumeSettings = RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS): RelativeVolumeState {
  return withDrawOwnerScope(OWNER, () => relativeVolumeIndicator.createState(settings));
}

function ctxOf(state: RelativeVolumeState, bar: BarFrame, settings: RelativeVolumeSettings, replace: boolean, markers: IndicatorBarCtx<RelativeVolumeState, RelativeVolumeSettings>["markers"], fills: (number | null)[][] = []): IndicatorBarCtx<RelativeVolumeState, RelativeVolumeSettings> {
  return {
    bar,
    state,
    settings,
    replace,
    replaying: false,
    drawNow: true,
    trades: [],
    ta: {},
    draw: {
      fill: (a, b) => {
        fills.push([a, b]);
      },
    },
    markers,
    pane: { paneIndex: 0 },
  };
}

function pendingTexts(): string[] {
  const [table] = tablesForOwners([OWNER]);
  const grid = (table.data as TableData).pendingGrid;
  return Object.keys(grid)
    .sort()
    .map((key) => grid[key].text);
}

describe("relativeVolume -- settings schema (UX standard part 2)", () => {
  const fields = RELATIVE_VOLUME_SETTINGS_SCHEMA.filter((field) => field.kind !== "staticHint");
  const keyOf = (field: (typeof fields)[number]) => String(field.key);
  const labelOf = (field: (typeof fields)[number]) => ("label" in field ? field.label : "");
  const affectsOf = (field: (typeof fields)[number]) => field.affects;

  it("covers every settings key once, with step > 0 on every numeric field", () => {
    const keys = fields.map(keyOf);
    expect([...keys].sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS).sort());
    for (const field of fields) {
      if (field.kind === "numeric" || field.kind === "slider") expect(field.step).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });

  it("has two primary controls on the Inputs tab, the clock and minimum under Advanced, toggles and colours under Visibility", () => {
    const primary = fields.filter((field) => field.tab === undefined || field.tab === "inputs");
    expect(primary.map(keyOf)).toEqual(["sessions", "hotThreshold"]);
    expect(primary.map(labelOf)).toEqual(["Lookback (prior bars)", "Unusual above (×)"]);
    const advanced = fields.filter((field) => field.tab === "advanced");
    expect(advanced.map(keyOf)).toEqual(["minSessions", "clock"]);
    expect(advanced.map(labelOf)).toEqual(["Minimum prior bars", "Clock"]);
    const visibility = fields.filter((field) => field.tab === "visibility");
    expect(visibility.map(keyOf)).toEqual(["showRolling", "showMarkers"]);
    expect(visibility.map(labelOf)).toEqual(["Show rolling 20", "Show unusual markers"]);
    const clock = advanced.find((field) => field.kind === "select");
    expect(clock && clock.kind === "select" ? clock.options.map((option) => option.label) : []).toEqual(["Automatic", "New York", "UTC"]);
  });

  it("every setting changes the math, the colouring of history or a marker, so every setting replays; there is no cosmetic setting and the colours are fixed", () => {
    const affects = Object.fromEntries(fields.map((field) => [keyOf(field), affectsOf(field)]));
    expect(affects).toEqual({
      sessions: "recompute",
      hotThreshold: "recompute",
      minSessions: "recompute",
      clock: "recompute",
      showRolling: "recompute",
      showMarkers: "recompute",
    });
    expect(fields.some((field) => field.kind === "color")).toBe(false);
    const sessions = fields.find((field) => keyOf(field) === "sessions");
    expect(sessions && sessions.kind === "numeric" ? [sessions.min, sessions.max, sessions.step] : null).toEqual([3, 60, 1]);
    for (const line of relativeVolumeIndicator.lines) expect(typeof line.recolor).toBe("function");
    const hints = RELATIVE_VOLUME_SETTINGS_SCHEMA.filter((field) => field.kind === "staticHint");
    for (const hint of hints) {
      expect(hint.kind === "staticHint" ? hint.text.endsWith("Replays every bar.") : false).toBe(true);
      expect(hint.tab).toBeUndefined();
    }
  });

  it("defaults are the brief's", () => {
    expect(RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS).toEqual({
      sessions: 10,
      hotThreshold: 2,
      minSessions: 3,
      clock: "auto",
      showRolling: false,
      showMarkers: true,
    });
  });
});

describe("relativeVolume -- colours, table copy and status precedence", () => {
  const settings = RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS;

  it("colours the line purple at or above 1, blue when unusual, grey below 1, and at 50 percent alpha while forming", () => {

  });

    expect(statusText(reading({ status: "noBaseline", rvol: null }))).toBe("No usable baseline");
    expect(statusText(null)).toBe("");
    expect(relativeVolumeTableRows(reading({ rolling: null, rollingCount: 5 }), settings)[1].text).toBe("Rolling — · 20 bars");
    for (const row of relativeVolumeTableRows(learning, settings)) expect(row.text.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(30);
  });

  it("the status row follows the brief's precedence on real bars: unsupported timeframe first, then no volume, learning, forming, ready", () => {
    const { bars } = relativeVolumeEquityFixture();

    const state = openState();
    const capture = captureMarkers(() => 0);
    relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(state, fourHour, settings, false, capture.markers));
    expect(pendingTexts()).toEqual(["RVOL — · 0 prior", "Rolling — · 20 bars", "Needs 1m to 1h"]);

    expect(pendingTexts()[2]).toBe("Learning · 2 of 3 prior");

    relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(fresh, bars[3 * 390], settings, false, capture.markers));
    for (const bar of bars.slice(3 * 390 + 1, 3 * 390 + 5)) relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(fresh, bar, settings, false, capture.markers));
    relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(fresh, { ...ready, closed: false, volume: 10 }, settings, false, capture.markers));
    expect(pendingTexts()[2]).toBe("Forming · vs full bar");
    relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(fresh, { ...ready, closed: true, volume: Number.NaN }, settings, true, capture.markers));
    expect(pendingTexts()[2]).toBe("No usable volume");
    relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(fresh, ready, settings, true, capture.markers));
    expect(pendingTexts()[2]).toBe("");
    expect(pendingTexts()[0]).toMatch(/^RVOL \d+\.\d× · 3 prior$/);
  });
});

describe("relativeVolume -- markers, fill and replace semantics", () => {
  const settings = RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS;
  const { bars } = relativeVolumeEquityFixture();
  const warm = bars.slice(0, 3 * 390 + 40);

  function warmed(state: RelativeVolumeState, markers: IndicatorBarCtx<RelativeVolumeState, RelativeVolumeSettings>["markers"], fills: (number | null)[][] = []) {
    for (const bar of warm) relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(state, bar, settings, false, markers, fills));
  }

  it("draws one circle above the bar when hot, size 2 from 3x, provisional at 50 percent alpha, nothing otherwise", () => {
    const state = openState();
    const fills: (number | null)[][] = [];
    let time = 0;
    const capture = captureMarkers(() => time);
    warmed(state, capture.markers, fills);
    const next = bars[warm.length];
    time = next.ts_open;
    capture.reset();
    relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(state, { ...next, closed: false, volume: 1 }, settings, false, capture.markers, fills));
    const quiet = relativeVolumeIndicator.readOutput(state).rvol!;
    expect(quiet).toBeLessThan(2);
    expect(capture.calls).toEqual([]);
    const denominator = 1 / quiet;
    relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(state, { ...next, closed: false, volume: Math.ceil(denominator * 2.1) }, settings, true, capture.markers, fills));
    expect(capture.calls).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(capture.calls[0]).toMatchObject({ shape: "circle", position: "aboveBar", color: "rgba(37,99,235,0.5)", size: 1, time: next.ts_open });
    capture.reset();
    relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(state, { ...next, closed: true, volume: Math.ceil(denominator * 3.2) }, settings, true, capture.markers, fills));
    expect(capture.calls).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(capture.calls[0]).toMatchObject({ shape: "circle", position: "aboveBar", color: RVOL_HOT_COLOR, size: 2 });
    expect(fills[fills.length - 1]).toEqual([relativeVolumeIndicator.readOutput(state).rvol, 1]);
    expect(RVOL_FILL_COLOR_ABOVE).toBe("rgba(134,114,179,0.15)");
  });

  it("showMarkers off draws no marker while the ratio is unchanged; a threshold above 3 makes every drawn marker large", () => {
    const quietSettings = { ...settings, showMarkers: false };
    const state = openState(quietSettings);
    const capture = captureMarkers(() => 0);
    const fills: (number | null)[][] = [];
    for (const bar of warm) relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(state, bar, quietSettings, false, capture.markers, fills));
    const next = bars[warm.length];
    relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(state, { ...next, volume: next.volume * 10 }, quietSettings, false, capture.markers, fills));
    expect(relativeVolumeIndicator.readOutput(state).rvol).toBeGreaterThan(3);
    expect(capture.calls).toEqual([]);

    const strict = { ...settings, hotThreshold: 4 };
    const strictState = openState(strict);
    for (const bar of warm) relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(strictState, bar, strict, false, capture.markers, fills));
    relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(strictState, { ...next, volume: next.volume * 10 }, strict, false, capture.markers, fills));
    expect(capture.calls).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(capture.calls[0].size).toBe(2);
  });

  it("a replace:true revision never commits: the slot ring is untouched until the close", () => {
    const state = openState();
    const capture = captureMarkers(() => 0);
    warmed(state, capture.markers);
    const next = bars[warm.length];
    const ringBefore = JSON.stringify(state.engine.slotRing(570 + 40)!.items);
    for (const share of [0.2, 0.5, 0.8]) {
      relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(state, { ...next, closed: false, volume: Math.round(next.volume * share) }, settings, share !== 0.2, capture.markers));
      expect(JSON.stringify(state.engine.slotRing(570 + 40)!.items)).toBe(ringBefore);
    }
    relativeVolumeIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(state, next, settings, true, capture.markers));
    expect(state.engine.slotRing(570 + 40)!.items).toHaveLength(4);
  });
});

  replace: boolean,
  replaying: boolean,
  drawNow: boolean,
  barIndex: number,
  barCount: number,
  trades: readonly TradeFrame[] = EMPTY_TRADES,
  tradeAvailability: TradeAvailability = "HISTORICAL_UNAVAILABLE",
): IndicatorBarCtx<unknown, unknown> {
  record.pendingFillPoints.clear();
  record.pendingMarkerCalls = [];

  return {
    bar,
    trades,
    tradeAvailability,
    liquidity: (record.liquidity?.atTime
      ? record.liquidity.atTime(bar.ts_open + bar.interval_ms)
      : record.liquidity) ?? undefined,
    l2: record.l2?.atTime?.(bar.ts_open + bar.interval_ms) ?? record.l2 ?? undefined,
    bandHistory: record.bandHistory ?? undefined,
    ticks: record.ticks ?? undefined,
    state: record.state,
    settings: record.settings,
    replace,
    replaying,
    drawNow,
    barIndex,
    barCount,
    ta: EMPTY_NAMESPACE,
    draw: {
      fill(valueA, valueB, options, key = DEFAULT_FILL_KEY) {
        record.fills = true;
        record.pendingFillPoints.set(
          key,
          valueA !== null && valueB !== null
            ? { time: bar.ts_open, valueA, valueB }
            : null,
        );
        if (options) record.fillOptionsByKey.set(key, options);
      },
    },
    markers: {
      plotshape(options) {
        record.pendingMarkerCalls.push(plotShapeCall(bar.ts_open, options));
      },
      plotchar(options) {
        record.pendingMarkerCalls.push(plotCharCall(bar.ts_open, options));
      },
    },
    pane: { paneIndex: 0 },
  };
}

/**
 * Commits this bar's draw.fill/markers.plotshape/plotchar/table.cell calls,
 * once per bar-transition (never once per call inside onBar) -- the same
 * discipline pushOutput/overwriteLastOutput already give the numeric line
 * channel, extended to the per-bar declarative draw channels.
 */
function commitDrawFlush(
  record: ActivationRecord,
  bar: BarFrame,
  replace: boolean,
): void {
  for (const [key, points] of record.fillPointsByKey) {
    const point = record.pendingFillPoints.get(key) ?? null;
    if (replace) overwriteLastFillPoint(points, point);
    else pushFillPoint(points, point);
  }
  // A band first filled on this bar has no history: backfill it with the bars
  // it did not exist for, so every band's points stay one per bar.
  for (const key of record.pendingFillPoints.keys()) {
    if (record.fillPointsByKey.has(key)) continue;
    // On a re-delivery of the newest bar that bar is already counted, so it takes the band's first point.
    const points: (FillPoint | null)[] = new Array(replace ? Math.max(0, record.fillBars - 1) : record.fillBars).fill(null);
    points.push(record.pendingFillPoints.get(key) ?? null);
    record.fillPointsByKey.set(key, points);
  }
  if (!replace) record.fillBars += 1;
  if (replace) {
    overwriteBarMarkerCalls(
      record.markerCalls,
      bar.ts_open,
      record.pendingMarkerCalls,
    );
  } else {
    pushMarkerCalls(record.markerCalls, record.pendingMarkerCalls);
  }
  flushTableCells(record.drawOwnerId);
}

function fault(record: ActivationRecord, reason: string, error: unknown): void {
  record.faulted = true;
  console.error(
    `indicator "${record.def.id}" ${reason} and has been disabled until it is re-activated`,
    error,
  );
}

function runOnBar(
  record: ActivationRecord,
function fullReplay(record: ActivationRecord, bars: readonly BarFrame[]): void {
  if (record.faulted) return;
  withNotificationsDeferred(() => replayInto(record, bars));
}

function replayInto(record: ActivationRecord, bars: readonly BarFrame[]): void {
  try {
    record.state = withDrawOwnerScope(record.drawOwnerId, () =>
      record.def.createState(record.settings),
    );
  } catch (error) {
    fault(record, "threw inside createState", error);
    return;
  }
  record.outputs = emptyOutputs(record.def);
  record.colors = emptyColors(record.def);
  record.fillPointsByKey.clear();
  record.fillOptionsByKey.clear();
  record.fillBars = 0;
  record.fills = false;
  resetMarkerCalls(record.markerCalls);
  record.lastTailTsOpen = null;
  const replayBars =
    record.def.maxReplayBars === undefined
      ? bars
      : bars.slice(-record.def.maxReplayBars);
  const lastClosed = replayBars.findLastIndex((bar) => bar.closed);
  for (let index = 0; index < replayBars.length; index += 1) {
    const bar = replayBars[index];
    if (!runOnBar(record, makeCtx(record, bar, false, true, index >= lastClosed, index, replayBars.length))) return;
    pushOutput(record);
    if (record.faulted) return;
    commitDrawFlush(record, bar, false);
    record.lastTailTsOpen = bar.ts_open;
  }
}

function fieldKeyChanged<T>(
  field: SettingsFieldSchema<T>,
  oldSettings: T,
  newSettings: T,
): boolean {
  if (field.kind === "staticHint") return false;
  if (field.kind === "custom") {
    if (field.key === undefined) return false;
    return oldSettings[field.key] !== newSettings[field.key];
  }
  return oldSettings[field.key] !== newSettings[field.key];
}

/**
 * A custom field with no declared `key` can write anywhere in T, so its
 * own change can't be scoped to one property -- the only safe signal is
 * "something, somewhere, changed." A custom field that DOES declare a
 * `key` is handled by the normal per-field sweep below instead, so an
 * unrelated sibling field's change no longer forces its unnecessary
 * whole-object fallback too (found during TA.05's audit: this used to
 * poison every settings change, including purely cosmetic ones, into a
 * full replay whenever any custom recompute field existed at all).
 */
function hasUnkeyedCustomRecomputeField<T>(
  schema: readonly SettingsFieldSchema<T>[],
): boolean {
  return schema.some(
    (field) =>
      field.kind === "custom" &&
      field.affects === "recompute" &&
      field.key === undefined,
  );
}

export interface FillPoint {
  time: number;
  valueA: number;
  valueB: number;
}

export interface FillOptions {
  colorAbove?: string;
  colorBelow?: string;
}

/**
 * The key `ctx.draw.fill` files a band under when it is not given one, so an
 * indicator that fills a single region never has to name it.
 */
export const DEFAULT_FILL_KEY = "fill";

export interface FillSegmentPoint {
  x: number;
  yA: number;
  yB: number;
}

export interface FillSegment {
  points: readonly FillSegmentPoint[];
  color: string;
}

export const DEFAULT_FILL_COLOR_ABOVE = "rgba(38, 166, 154, 0.2)";
export const DEFAULT_FILL_COLOR_BELOW = "rgba(239, 83, 80, 0.2)";

type CoordinateFn = (value: number) => number | null;

export function pushFillPoint(points: (FillPoint | null)[], point: FillPoint | null): void {
  points.push(point);
}

export function overwriteLastFillPoint(points: (FillPoint | null)[], point: FillPoint | null): void {
  if (points.length === 0) {
    points.push(point);
    return;
  }
  points[points.length - 1] = point;
}

export function visibleFillPoints(
  points: readonly (FillPoint | null)[],
  fromMs: number,
  toMs: number,
  margin = 3,
): readonly (FillPoint | null)[] {
  let first = -1;
  let last = -1;
  for (let index = 0; index < points.length; index += 1) {
    const point = points[index];
    if (point === null) continue;
    if (first === -1 && point.time >= fromMs) first = index;
    if (point.time <= toMs) last = index;
  }
  if (first === -1) first = points.length;
  const start = Math.max(0, Math.min(first, last + 1) - margin);
  const end = Math.min(points.length, Math.max(last, first - 1) + margin + 1);
  return points.slice(start, end);
}

export function resetFillPoints(points: (FillPoint | null)[]): void {
  points.length = 0;
}

/**
 * Splits into disjoint segments at every null gap and at every sign flip of
 * (valueA - valueB), so a band that crosses its own other side is drawn with
 * the correct above/below color on each side of the crossing rather than one
 * segment stretched across it.
 */
export function computeFillPolygon(
  points: readonly (FillPoint | null)[],
  timeToCoordinate: CoordinateFn,
  priceToCoordinate: CoordinateFn,
  options: FillOptions = {},
): FillSegment[] {
  const colorAbove = options.colorAbove ?? DEFAULT_FILL_COLOR_ABOVE;
  const colorBelow = options.colorBelow ?? DEFAULT_FILL_COLOR_BELOW;

  const segments: FillSegment[] = [];
  let current: FillSegmentPoint[] = [];
  let currentSide: "above" | "below" | null = null;
  let previousPoint: FillPoint | null = null;

  function flush(): void {
    if (current.length >= 2) {
      segments.push({ points: current, color: currentSide === "below" ? colorBelow : colorAbove });
    }
    current = [];
  }

  for (const point of points) {
    if (point === null) {
      flush();
      currentSide = null;
      previousPoint = null;
      continue;
    }
    const x = timeToCoordinate(point.time);
    const yA = priceToCoordinate(point.valueA);
    const yB = priceToCoordinate(point.valueB);
    if (x === null || yA === null || yB === null) {
      flush();
      currentSide = null;
      previousPoint = null;
      continue;
    }
    const side: "above" | "below" = point.valueA >= point.valueB ? "above" : "below";
    if (currentSide !== null && side !== currentSide && previousPoint !== null) {
      // Split at the true line intersection. Splitting at the next candle
      // paints part of the cloud with the wrong above/below color.
      const previousDelta = previousPoint.valueA - previousPoint.valueB;
      const currentDelta = point.valueA - point.valueB;
      const ratio = previousDelta / (previousDelta - currentDelta);
      const crossingTime = previousPoint.time + ratio * (point.time - previousPoint.time);
      const crossingValueA = previousPoint.valueA + ratio * (point.valueA - previousPoint.valueA);
      const crossingValueB = previousPoint.valueB + ratio * (point.valueB - previousPoint.valueB);
      const crossingX = timeToCoordinate(crossingTime);
      const crossingYA = priceToCoordinate(crossingValueA);
      const crossingYB = priceToCoordinate(crossingValueB);
      if (crossingX !== null && crossingYA !== null && crossingYB !== null) {
        const crossing = { x: crossingX, yA: crossingYA, yB: crossingYB };
        current.push(crossing);
        flush();
        current = [crossing, { x, yA, yB }];
      } else {
        flush();
        current = [{ x, yA, yB }];
      }
      currentSide = side;
      previousPoint = point;
      continue;
    }
    current.push({ x, yA, yB });
    currentSide = side;
    previousPoint = point;
  }
  flush();
  return segments;
}
75:export function nyLocalMinuteOfDay(epochMs: number): number {
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/registry";
import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../types/frames";
import { BINANCE_PERP, MINUTE_MS, RVOL_EQUITY_ANOMALIES, barFrame, countMarkers, judgeBudget, relativeVolumeEquityFixture, replayCapture, summarizeBudget } from "../../../chart/validation";
import { RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, relativeVolumeIndicator } from "../relativeVolume";
import { createRelativeVolumeEngine } from "../relativeVolumeMath";

afterEach(() => {
  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
});

/**
 * Brief, "Baseline comparison": three methods at the same threshold on the same
 * eligible bars. Time of day (slot median) is the indicator; rolling (mean of
 * the previous 20 committed volumes) is the baseline a plain RVOL gives; the
 * same-slot mean separates the benefit of the clock from the benefit of the
 * median. Counts and rates are printed as the table the doc page quotes.
 */

const THRESHOLD = 2;
const METHODS = ["timeOfDay", "sameSlotMean", "rolling"] as const;
type Method = (typeof METHODS)[number];

interface MethodResult {
  eligible: number;
  detections: number;
  anomaliesHit: number;
  falsePositives: number;
  predictableEligible: number;
  predictableFalsePositives: number;
}

export interface ComparisonTable {
  anomalies: number;
  results: Record<Method, MethodResult>;
}

function emptyResult(): MethodResult {
  return { eligible: 0, detections: 0, anomaliesHit: 0, falsePositives: 0, predictableEligible: 0, predictableFalsePositives: 0 };
}

export function compareMethods(fixture: ReturnType<typeof relativeVolumeEquityFixture>): ComparisonTable {
  const { bars, labels } = fixture;
  const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS.sessions, minSessions: RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS.minSessions, clock: "auto" });
  const results: Record<Method, MethodResult> = { timeOfDay: emptyResult(), sameSlotMean: emptyResult(), rolling: emptyResult() };
  const anomalyIndices = new Set(labels.anomalyIndices);
  const predictableSlotIndices = new Set(labels.predictableSlotIndices);
  const evaluationIndices = new Set(labels.evaluationIndices);
  for (let index = 0; index < bars.length; index += 1) {
    const reading = engine.onBar(bars[index]);
    if (!evaluationIndices.has(index)) continue;
    const ratios: Record<Method, number | null> = { timeOfDay: reading.rvol, sameSlotMean: reading.slotMeanRatio, rolling: reading.rolling };
    // Readiness is asserted from actual prior observations per key, never from a calendar day number.
    if (METHODS.some((method) => ratios[method] === null)) continue;
    const anomaly = anomalyIndices.has(index);
    const predictable = predictableSlotIndices.has(index);
    for (const method of METHODS) {
      const result = results[method];
      const detected = (ratios[method] as number) >= THRESHOLD;
      result.eligible += 1;
      if (predictable) result.predictableEligible += 1;
      if (detected) result.detections += 1;
      if (anomaly && detected) result.anomaliesHit += 1;
      if (!anomaly && detected) {
        result.falsePositives += 1;
        if (predictable) result.predictableFalsePositives += 1;
      }
    }
  }
  return { anomalies: labels.anomalies.length, results };
}

function pct(numerator: number, denominator: number): string {
  return denominator === 0 ? "n/a" : `${((100 * numerator) / denominator).toFixed(2)}%`;
}

/** Eligible non-anomaly bars: the denominator of the overall false-positive rate, whatever the recall. */
export function nonAnomalyBars(result: MethodResult, anomaliesEligible: number): number {
  return result.eligible - anomaliesEligible;
}

/** Fraction of eligible non-anomaly bars a method marked; `anomaliesEligible` counts the anomalies among the eligible bars, hit or missed. */
export function falsePositiveRate(result: MethodResult, anomaliesEligible: number): number {
  const denominator = nonAnomalyBars(result, anomaliesEligible);
  return denominator === 0 ? 0 : result.falsePositives / denominator;
}

const METHOD_LABELS: Record<Method, string> = {
  timeOfDay: "Time of day (slot median)",
  sameSlotMean: "Same-slot mean",
  rolling: "Rolling 20 mean",
};

export function formatComparison(title: string, table: ComparisonTable): string {
  // Every anomaly lies in sessions 4..15 and every method is ready there, so all of them are eligible.
  const anomaliesEligible = table.anomalies;
  const lines = [
    `${title} (threshold ${THRESHOLD.toFixed(1)}, ${table.anomalies} anomalies)`,
    "| Method | Eligible bars | Detections | Anomalies hit | Recall | False positives | FP rate | Predictable-slot bars | Predictable-slot FPs | Predictable-slot FP rate |",
    "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|",
  ];
  for (const method of METHODS) {
    const r = table.results[method];
    lines.push(
      `| ${METHOD_LABELS[method]} | ${r.eligible} | ${r.detections} | ${r.anomaliesHit}/${table.anomalies} | ${pct(r.anomaliesHit, table.anomalies)} | ${r.falsePositives} | ${pct(r.falsePositives, nonAnomalyBars(r, anomaliesEligible))} | ${r.predictableEligible} | ${r.predictableFalsePositives} | ${pct(r.predictableFalsePositives, r.predictableEligible)} |`,
    );
  }
  return lines.join("\n");
}

describe("relativeVolume beats the baseline on labelled fixtures (brief: baseline comparison)", () => {
  const fixture = relativeVolumeEquityFixture();
  const table = compareMethods(fixture);
  const tod = table.results.timeOfDay;
  const rolling = table.results.rolling;

  it("the fixture is the frozen one: 15 sessions, 12 anomalies in sessions 4..15 and minutes 90..330", () => {
    expect(fixture.bars).toHaveLength(15 * 390);
    expect(fixture.labels.anomalies).toHaveLength(RVOL_EQUITY_ANOMALIES);
    for (const anomaly of fixture.labels.anomalies) {
      expect(anomaly.session).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4);
      expect(anomaly.session).toBeLessThanOrEqual(15);
      expect(anomaly.minute).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(90);
      expect(anomaly.minute).toBeLessThanOrEqual(330);
    }
    expect(fixture.labels.evaluationIndices).toHaveLength(12 * 390);
    expect(fixture.labels.predictableSlotIndices.filter((index) => index >= 3 * 390)).toHaveLength(12 * 20);
  });

  it("every evaluation bar is eligible for all three methods", () => {
    console.log(formatComparison("Equity fixture", table));
    expect(tod.eligible).toBe(12 * 390);
    expect(table.results.sameSlotMean.eligible).toBe(tod.eligible);
    expect(rolling.eligible).toBe(tod.eligible);
  });

  it("pins the published counts: time of day 13/12/1 with 0 of 240 predictable, same-slot mean 12/12/0, rolling 32/12/20 with 19 of 240 predictable", () => {
    const counts = (r: MethodResult) => [r.detections, r.anomaliesHit, r.falsePositives, r.predictableEligible, r.predictableFalsePositives];
    expect(counts(tod)).toEqual([13, 12, 1, 240, 0]);
    expect(counts(table.results.sameSlotMean)).toEqual([12, 12, 0, 240, 0]);
    expect(counts(rolling)).toEqual([32, 12, 20, 240, 19]);
    // Overall false-positive rates divide by the eligible non-anomaly bars (4,668), not by eligible minus hits.
    expect(nonAnomalyBars(tod, table.anomalies)).toBe(4_668);
    expect(falsePositiveRate(tod, table.anomalies)).toBeCloseTo(1 / 4_668, 12);
    expect(falsePositiveRate(rolling, table.anomalies)).toBeCloseTo(20 / 4_668, 12);
    expect(formatComparison("x", table)).toContain("| 20 | 0.43% | 240 | 19 | 7.92% |");
  });

  it("hand-computed: a method that misses an anomaly still divides its false positives by the non-anomaly bars", () => {
    // 100 eligible bars, 3 anomalies, the method hits 2 of them and marks 4 other bars.
    const missed: MethodResult = { eligible: 100, detections: 6, anomaliesHit: 2, falsePositives: 4, predictableEligible: 10, predictableFalsePositives: 1 };
    expect(nonAnomalyBars(missed, 3)).toBe(97);
    expect(falsePositiveRate(missed, 3)).toBeCloseTo(4 / 97, 12);
    // `eligible - anomaliesHit` would be 98, the wrong denominator once an anomaly is missed.
    expect(missed.eligible - missed.anomaliesHit).toBe(98);
    const line = formatComparison("missed", { anomalies: 3, results: { timeOfDay: missed, sameSlotMean: missed, rolling: missed } }).split("\n")[3];
    expect(line).toBe("| Time of day (slot median) | 100 | 6 | 2/3 | 66.67% | 4 | 4.12% | 10 | 1 | 10.00% |");
  });

  it("time-of-day recall on the 12 anomalies is at least 11/12 and at least rolling recall minus one", () => {
    expect(tod.anomaliesHit).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(11);
    expect(tod.anomaliesHit).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(rolling.anomaliesHit - 1);
  });

  it("time-of-day predictable-slot false positives are at most 10 percent of those bars and at most half of rolling's rate", () => {
    const todRate = tod.predictableFalsePositives / tod.predictableEligible;
    const rollingRate = rolling.predictableFalsePositives / rolling.predictableEligible;
    expect(todRate).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.1);
    expect(todRate).toBeLessThanOrEqual(rollingRate / 2);
  });

  it("the time-of-day method has strictly fewer false positives than rolling at equal or better recall", () => {
    expect(tod.falsePositives).toBeLessThan(rolling.falsePositives);
    expect(tod.anomaliesHit).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(rolling.anomaliesHit - 1);
  });

  it("the indicator's rvol line equals the engine's ratio bar for bar", () => {
    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, fixture.bars, { ...RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, showRolling: true });
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
    fixture.bars.forEach((bar, index) => {
      const reading = engine.onBar(bar);
      expect(capture.outputs.rvol[index]).toBe(reading.rvol);
      expect(capture.outputs.rolling[index]).toBe(reading.rolling);
    });
  });

  it("control experiment: flat seasonality, anomalies from every minute (reported, not a gate)", () => {
    const control = relativeVolumeEquityFixture({ flatSeasonality: true });
    expect(control.labels.regime).toBe("relativeVolumeEquityFlat");
    const controlTable = compareMethods(control);
    console.log(formatComparison("Control fixture (flat seasonality)", controlTable));
    expect(controlTable.results.timeOfDay.eligible).toBe(12 * 390);
    // Published control counts: when the clock explains nothing the three methods agree on the anomalies and rolling stops misfiring.
    const counts = (r: MethodResult) => [r.detections, r.anomaliesHit, r.falsePositives];
    expect(counts(controlTable.results.timeOfDay)).toEqual([13, 12, 1]);
    expect(counts(controlTable.results.sameSlotMean)).toEqual([12, 12, 0]);
    expect(counts(controlTable.results.rolling)).toEqual([12, 12, 0]);
    expect(controlTable.results.rolling.predictableFalsePositives).toBe(0);
  });
});

/** Eligible bars: the ones with a ratio; a budget judged on an indicator that drew nothing proves nothing. */
function eligibleOutputs(bars: readonly BarFrame[]): number {
  return replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, bars, RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS).outputs.rvol.filter((value) => value !== null).length;
}

function minuteBars(count: number, volumeAt: (index: number) => number): BarFrame[] {
  const t0 = Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2);
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => barFrame(t0 + index * MINUTE_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: volumeAt(index) }, BINANCE_PERP));
}

describe("relativeVolume marker budget (success criterion 5 and protocol check 5)", () => {
  it("emits fewer than 2 hot markers per 100 evaluated bars on the equity fixture, anomalies included: 13 over 4,680", () => {
    const { bars, labels } = relativeVolumeEquityFixture();
    const budget = countMarkers(relativeVolumeIndicator, bars, RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    const evaluated = labels.evaluationIndices.length;
    const per100 = (budget.total / evaluated) * 100;
    console.log(`${summarizeBudget("relativeVolume equity fixture", budget)}; ${budget.total} over ${evaluated} evaluated bars = ${per100.toFixed(2)} per 100`);
    expect(eligibleOutputs(bars)).toBe(evaluated);
    expect(budget.total).toBe(13);
    expect(per100).toBeLessThan(2);
    expect(budget.maxPerBar).toBe(1);
    expect(budget.byShape).toEqual({ circle: budget.total });
    expect(budget.byPosition).toEqual({ aboveBar: budget.total });
    expect(judgeBudget(budget, 200, 3).ok).toBe(true);
    expect(judgeBudget(budget, 40, 3).ok).toBe(true);
  });

  it("a session-wide volume shift marks every eligible bar (1,000 per 1,000), which is the correct reading of such a session; a one-hour burst marks exactly that hour", () => {
    const shift = minuteBars(4 * 1_440, (index) => (index < 3 * 1_440 ? 100 : 250));
    const shiftBudget = countMarkers(relativeVolumeIndicator, shift, RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    const shiftEligible = eligibleOutputs(shift);
    expect(shiftEligible).toBe(1_440);
    expect(shiftBudget.total).toBe(1_440);
    expect(shiftBudget.maxPerBar).toBe(1);
    console.log(`${summarizeBudget("session-wide shift (3 days at 100, then 250)", shiftBudget)}; ${shiftBudget.total} of ${shiftEligible} eligible = ${((shiftBudget.total / shiftEligible) * 1_000).toFixed(0)} per 1,000 eligible`);

    const budget = countMarkers(relativeVolumeIndicator, quiet, RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    expect(eligibleOutputs(quiet)).toBe(1_440);
    expect(budget.total).toBe(0);
    expect(judgeBudget(budget, 40, 3).ok).toBe(true);
  });

function noNaN(outputs: Readonly<Record<string, readonly (number | null)[]>>): void {
  for (const values of Object.values(outputs)) {
    for (const value of values) expect(value === null || Number.isFinite(value)).toBe(true);
  }
}

describe("relativeVolume -- replay versus live equivalence (protocol check 2)", () => {
  it("ends on the same committed values and markers whether fed live with forming revisions or replayed", () => {
    const report = checkLiveReplayEquivalence(relativeVolumeIndicator, EQUITY.bars, SETTINGS, { revisionsPerBar: 5 });
    console.log(summarizeEquivalence("relativeVolume equity fixture", report));
    expect(report.mutations.filter((mutation) => mutation === "REPLACED_EDGE")).toHaveLength(EQUITY.bars.length * 5);
    expect(report.outputsEqual).toBe(true);
    expect(report.markersEqual).toBe(true);
    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
    expect(report.live.markers.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    noNaN(report.live.outputs);
    noNaN(report.replay.outputs);
  }, 120_000);

  it("a bar whose close never arrived contributes nothing: every other bar equals a replay without it, its own reading and marker stay provisional (rule 7(b) withdrawn)", () => {
    // Every anomaly bar loses its closed dispatch: the forming revisions arrive, then the next bar.
    const anomalyIndices = new Set(EQUITY.labels.anomalyIndices);
    const arrivals: Arrival[] = [];
    EQUITY.bars.forEach((bar, index) => {
      const frames = formingRevisions(bar, 3);
      if (anomalyIndices.has(index)) frames.pop();
      for (const frame of frames) arrivals.push({ bar: frame });
    });
    const live = driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, arrivals, SETTINGS);
    const without = EQUITY.bars.filter((_, index) => !anomalyIndices.has(index));
    const replayWithout = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, without, SETTINGS);
    // Outputs of the bars that did close equal a replay in which the never-closed bars do not exist at all.
    let cursor = 0;
    EQUITY.bars.forEach((_, index) => {
      if (anomalyIndices.has(index)) {
        expect(live.outputs.rvol[index]).not.toBeNull();
        return;
      }
      expect(live.outputs.rvol[index]).toBe(replayWithout.outputs.rvol[cursor]);
      expect(live.outputs.rolling[index]).toBe(replayWithout.outputs.rolling[cursor]);
      cursor += 1;
    });
    expect(cursor).toBe(without.length);
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
    for (const arrival of arrivals) engine.onBar(arrival.bar);
    for (const index of anomalyIndices) {
      const bar = EQUITY.bars[index];
      expect(engine.slotRing(slotKeyOf(bar.ts_open, "newYork")!)!.items.some((item) => item.tsOpen === bar.ts_open)).toBe(false);
      expect(engine.rollingRing.items.some((item) => item.tsOpen === bar.ts_open)).toBe(false);
    }
    const provisional = live.markers.filter((call) => call.color.startsWith("rgba("));
    const confirmed = live.markers.filter((call) => !call.color.startsWith("rgba("));
    expect(provisional.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const call of provisional) expect(anomalyIndices.has(EQUITY.bars.findIndex((bar) => bar.ts_open === call.time))).toBe(true);
    for (const call of confirmed) expect(anomalyIndices.has(EQUITY.bars.findIndex((bar) => bar.ts_open === call.time))).toBe(false);
  }, 120_000);

  it("a replay whose tail is a forming bar keeps the tail pending and provisional", () => {
    const bars = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 5 * 390 + 100);
    const tailOpen = { ...bars[bars.length - 1], closed: false, volume: Math.round(bars[bars.length - 1].volume / 2) } as BarFrame;
    const withTail = [...bars.slice(0, -1), tailOpen];
    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, withTail, SETTINGS);
    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
    for (const bar of withTail) engine.onBar(bar);
    expect(engine.tail!.committed).toBe(false);
    expect(engine.tail!.confirmed).toBe(false);
    expect(engine.current!.status).toBe("forming");
    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[withTail.length - 1]).toBe(engine.current!.rvol);
    expect(engine.current!.rvol).not.toBeNull();
    noNaN(capture.outputs);
  });
});

describe("relativeVolume -- gap, duplicate and late-event behavior (protocol check 3)", () => {
  const session5 = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 4 * 390 + 60);

  it("duplicate closed bar is idempotent", () => {
    const report = checkDuplicateClosedIdempotent(relativeVolumeIndicator, session5, SETTINGS, session5.length - 20);
    expect(report.duplicateMutation).toBe("REPLACED_EDGE");
    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
    const budget = countMarkersOver(relativeVolumeIndicator, duplicateClosedArrivals(EQUITY.bars, EQUITY.labels.anomalyIndices[0]).arrivals, SETTINGS);
    expect(budget.total).toBe(countMarkers(relativeVolumeIndicator, EQUITY.bars, SETTINGS).total);
  }, 60_000);

  it("late bar rebuilds to the replay", () => {
    const report = checkRebuiltEquivalence(relativeVolumeIndicator, session5, SETTINGS, 4 * 390 + 10);
    expect(report.sawRebuilt).toBe(true);
    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
    expect(report.live.outputs.rvol).toHaveLength(session5.length);
  }, 60_000);

  it("a correction to an older bar arrives as a REBUILT and every successor equals a fresh replay (amendment 1)", () => {
    const corrected = 3 * 390 + 200;
    const arrivals: Arrival[] = session5.map((bar) => ({ bar }));
    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...session5[corrected], volume: session5[corrected].volume * 4 }, label: "correction" });
    const report = checkArrivalsAgainstReplay(relativeVolumeIndicator, arrivals, SETTINGS);
    expect(report.mutations[report.mutations.length - 1]).toBe("REBUILT");
    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
    const correctedSeries = session5.map((bar, index) => (index === corrected ? { ...bar, volume: bar.volume * 4 } : bar));
    const fresh = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, correctedSeries, SETTINGS);
    expect(diffOutputs(report.live.outputs, fresh.outputs)).toEqual([]);
    expect(diffOutputs(replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, session5, SETTINGS).outputs, fresh.outputs).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  }, 60_000);

  it("changed-volume closed replacement: both rings take the new volume, the ratio uses the stored denominator, the marker follows, and later bars match a replay that only saw the final volume", () => {
    const index = 4 * 390 + 30;
    const bar = session5[index];
    const prefix = session5.slice(0, index);
    const base = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
    for (const earlier of prefix) base.onBar(earlier);
    const denominator = base.onBar({ ...bar, volume: 100 }).rvol;
    expect(denominator).not.toBeNull();
    const median = 100 / denominator!;
    const hotVolume = Math.ceil(median * 2.2);
    const arrivals: Arrival[] = prefix.map((earlier) => ({ bar: earlier }));
    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: 100 } });
    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: hotVolume }, label: "closed again" });
    for (const later of session5.slice(index + 1)) arrivals.push({ bar: later });
    const report = checkArrivalsAgainstReplay(relativeVolumeIndicator, arrivals, SETTINGS);
    expect(report.mutations[index + 1]).toBe("REPLACED_EDGE");
    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
    expect(report.live.outputs.rvol[index]).toBeCloseTo(hotVolume / median, 12);
    expect(report.live.markers.some((call) => call.time === bar.ts_open)).toBe(true);
    // Back below the threshold on a third close: the marker disappears and the ratio still uses the stored median.
    const back = driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, [...arrivals.slice(0, index + 2), { bar: { ...bar, volume: 100 } }], SETTINGS);
    expect(back.outputs.rvol[index]).toBe(denominator);
    expect(back.markers.some((call) => call.time === bar.ts_open)).toBe(false);
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
    for (const earlier of prefix) engine.onBar(earlier);
    engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: 100 });
    engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: hotVolume });
    const key = slotKeyOf(bar.ts_open, "newYork")!;
    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items[engine.slotRing(key)!.items.length - 1]).toEqual({ tsOpen: bar.ts_open, volume: hotVolume });
    expect(engine.rollingRing.items[RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH - 1]).toEqual({ tsOpen: bar.ts_open, volume: hotVolume });
    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items.filter((item) => item.tsOpen === bar.ts_open)).toHaveLength(1);
  }, 60_000);

  it("valid to invalid to valid with both rings full (14 prior per slot) equals a replay that saw only the final state (amendment 2)", () => {
    const index = 14 * 390 + 45; // session 15: every slot already holds 10 observations, the rolling ring 20
    const bar = EQUITY.bars[index];
    const prefix = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, index);
    const arrivals: Arrival[] = prefix.map((earlier) => ({ bar: earlier }));
    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: bar.volume + 1 } });
    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: Number.NaN }, label: "invalid" });
    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: bar.volume * 3 }, label: "valid again" });
    for (const later of EQUITY.bars.slice(index + 1, index + 40)) arrivals.push({ bar: later });
    const report = checkArrivalsAgainstReplay(relativeVolumeIndicator, arrivals, SETTINGS);
    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
    for (const earlier of prefix) engine.onBar(earlier);
    const key = slotKeyOf(bar.ts_open, "newYork")!;
    const slotBefore = JSON.stringify(engine.slotRing(key)!.items);
    const rollingBefore = JSON.stringify(engine.rollingRing.items);
    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items).toHaveLength(10);
    engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: bar.volume + 1 });
    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.evictedByTail).not.toBeNull();
    const invalid = engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: Number.NaN });
    expect(invalid.rvol).toBeNull();
    expect(invalid.status).toBe("noVolume");
    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.slotRing(key)!.items)).toBe(slotBefore);
    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.rollingRing.items)).toBe(rollingBefore);
    const valid = engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: bar.volume * 3 });
    expect(valid.rvol).not.toBeNull();
    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items).toHaveLength(10);
    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items[9]).toEqual({ tsOpen: bar.ts_open, volume: bar.volume * 3 });
  }, 60_000);

  it("time gap: missing minutes file nothing, nothing before the gap changes, the rolling comparison carries across", () => {
    const { bars, labels } = timeGapBars({ count: 600 });
    const gapped = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, bars, SETTINGS);
    const prefix = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, bars.slice(0, labels.gapAfterIndex + 1), SETTINGS);
    expect(diffOutputs(gapped.outputs, prefix.outputs, 0, labels.gapAfterIndex)).toEqual([]);
    const after = labels.firstBarAfterGapIndex;
    const previous = bars.slice(after - RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH, after);
    const mean = previous.reduce((sum, bar) => sum + bar.volume, 0) / RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH;
    expect(gapped.outputs.rolling[after]).toBeCloseTo(bars[after].volume / mean, 9);
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
    for (const bar of bars) engine.onBar(bar);
    for (let missing = 1; missing <= labels.missingBars; missing += 1) {
      const key = slotKeyOf(bars[labels.gapAfterIndex].ts_open + missing * 60_000, "utc")!;
      expect(engine.slotRing(key)).toBeUndefined();
    }
    noNaN(gapped.outputs);
  });
});

describe("relativeVolume -- warm-up, empty input and NaN (protocol check 4)", () => {
  it("warm-up is null, never NaN or zero: the first ratio appears when a slot has minSessions prior observations", () => {
    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 4 * 390), SETTINGS);
    for (let index = 0; index < 3 * 390; index += 1) expect(capture.outputs.rvol[index]).toBeNull();
    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[3 * 390]).not.toBeNull();
    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[3 * 390]).not.toBe(0);
    for (let index = 0; index < RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH; index += 1) expect(capture.outputs.rolling[index]).toBeNull();
    expect(capture.outputs.rolling[RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH]).not.toBeNull();
    noNaN(capture.outputs);
    const shorter = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 3 * 390), SETTINGS);
    expect(shorter.outputs.rvol.every((value) => value === null)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("handles an empty bar array", () => {
    const { perBar } = replayBars(relativeVolumeIndicator, [], RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    expect(perBar.rvol).toEqual([]);
    expect(perBar.rolling).toEqual([]);
    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, [], RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
  });

  it("a bar with no usable volume yields null and never poisons later bars", () => {
    const bars = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 4 * 390 + 5).map((bar, index) => (index === 4 * 390 + 2 ? ({ ...bar, volume: Number.NaN } as BarFrame) : bar));
    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, bars, SETTINGS);
    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[4 * 390 + 2]).toBeNull();
    expect(capture.outputs.rolling[4 * 390 + 2]).toBeNull();
    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[4 * 390 + 3]).not.toBeNull();
    noNaN(capture.outputs);
  });

  it("the rolling line is off by default: its output is null while the table value is still computed", () => {
    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 100), RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    expect(capture.outputs.rolling.every((value) => value === null)).toBe(true);
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
    let last = null;
    for (const bar of EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 100)) last = engine.onBar(bar);
    expect(last!.rolling).not.toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("relativeVolume -- rollover and reconnect (ruling 2): the latest ratio can change when a replay holds fewer prior observations than live state did", () => {
  /** Eleven days of one-minute crypto: 100 per minute for five days, then 300, the last bar 450. */
  function elevenDays(): BarFrame[] {
    const t0 = Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2);
    return Array.from({ length: 11 * 1_440 }, (_, index) =>
      barFrame(t0 + index * 60_000, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: index === 11 * 1_440 - 1 ? 450 : index < 5 * 1_440 ? 100 : 300 }, BINANCE_PERP),
    );
  }

  it("rollover and reconnect: live 2.25x against ten prior; a replay of the retained seven days and an older duplicate (REBUILT) both read 1.5x; a settings update that changes no recompute key changes nothing", () => {
    const bars = elevenDays();
    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, []);
    try {
      for (const bar of bars) session.dispatch({ bar });
      expect(session.bars).toHaveLength(10_080);
      const live = session.capture();
      const lastOpen = bars[bars.length - 1].ts_open;
      expect(live.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(2.25);
      expect(live.markers.some((call) => call.time === lastOpen && !call.color.startsWith("rgba("))).toBe(true);
      // A reconnect replays the retained bars: six prior observations at 300 for the last slot, median 300.
      const retained = [...session.bars];
      const replay = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, retained, SETTINGS);
      expect(replay.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(1.5);
      expect(replay.markers.some((call) => call.time === lastOpen)).toBe(false);
      // An update that changes no recompute key must not replay: every output and marker stays.
      session.updateSettings({ ...SETTINGS });
      expect(session.capture().outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(2.25);
      expect(session.capture().markers).toEqual(live.markers);
      // An identical closed duplicate of an older retained bar is a REBUILT and replays the retained bars.
      session.dispatch({ bar: { ...retained[100] } });
      expect(session.mutations.at(-1)).toBe("REBUILT");
      expect(session.capture().outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(1.5);
      expect(session.capture().markers.some((call) => call.time === lastOpen)).toBe(false);
    } finally {
      session.close();
    }
  }, 120_000);
});

describe("relativeVolume -- crypto fixture: rollover and REBUILT equivalence at sessions = 3 (amendment 7)", () => {
  const settings: RelativeVolumeSettings = { ...SETTINGS, sessions: 3, minSessions: 3 };

  it("a REBUILT replay over the retained 10,080 bars reproduces live outputs for every bar whose full lookback lies within them", () => {
    const retained = CRYPTO.bars.slice(CRYPTO.labels.rebuiltFromIndex);
    expect(retained).toHaveLength(10_080);
    const live = driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, CRYPTO.bars.map((bar) => ({ bar })), settings);
    expect(live.bars).toHaveLength(10_080);
    expect(live.bars[0].ts_open).toBe(retained[0].ts_open);
    const replay = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, retained, settings);
    const priorByKey = new Map<number, number>();
    let compared = 0;
    let withheld = 0;
    retained.forEach((bar, index) => {
      const key = slotKeyOf(bar.ts_open, "utc")!;
      const prior = priorByKey.get(key) ?? 0;
      priorByKey.set(key, prior + 1);
      if (prior >= 3 && index >= RVOL_ROLLING_LENGTH) {
        compared += 1;
        expect(replay.outputs.rvol[index]).toBe(live.outputs.rvol[index]);
        expect(replay.outputs.rolling[index]).toBe(live.outputs.rolling[index]);
        expect(live.outputs.rvol[index]).not.toBeNull();
      } else if (replay.outputs.rvol[index] !== live.outputs.rvol[index]) {
        withheld += 1;
      }
    });
    console.log(`crypto REBUILT equivalence: ${compared} bars compared equal, ${withheld} early retained bars differ because live state kept observations from discarded bars`);
    expect(compared).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(compared).toBe(10_080 - 3 * 1_440);
    expect(withheld).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    noNaN(live.outputs);
  }, 120_000);

  it("the runtime's own REBUILT path on the retained bars equals a clean replay, with a non-zero count of compared bars", () => {
    const retained = CRYPTO.bars.slice(CRYPTO.labels.rebuiltFromIndex);
    const report = checkRebuiltEquivalence(relativeVolumeIndicator, retained, settings, 5_000);
    expect(report.sawRebuilt).toBe(true);
    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
    const compared = report.live.outputs.rvol.filter((value) => value !== null).length;
    expect(compared).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  }, 120_000);

  it("live versus replay equivalence holds on the first 10,080 crypto bars with forming revisions", () => {
    const report = checkLiveReplayEquivalence(relativeVolumeIndicator, CRYPTO.bars.slice(0, 10_080), settings, { revisionsPerBar: 2 });
    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
    expect(liveArrivals(CRYPTO.bars.slice(0, 2), 2)).toHaveLength(6);
  }, 120_000);
});
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../../types/frames";
import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
import { resetDrawRegistryForTests, tablesForOwners } from "../../../../chart/draw/registry";
import type { TableData } from "../../../../chart/draw/table";
import { HISTORICAL_INDICATOR_POLICIES, INDICATOR_REGISTRY, historicalIndicatorAvailability } from "../../../../chart/indicatorRegistry";
import { resampleBars } from "../../../../chart/resampleBars";
import {
  BINANCE_PERP,
  DXFEED_EQUITY,
  barFrame,
  driveLive,
  equityUnsidedBars,
  openLiveSession,
  relativeVolumeEquityFixture,
  replayCapture,
  type Arrival,
} from "../../../../chart/validation";
import { nyLocalMinuteOfDay } from "../../../killzones/killzones";
import { RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, RELATIVE_VOLUME_SETTINGS_SCHEMA, relativeVolumeIndicator, type RelativeVolumeSettings } from "../../relativeVolume";
import { HOUR_MS, MINUTE_MS, RVOL_MIN_SESSIONS_SETTING, createRelativeVolumeEngine } from "../../relativeVolumeMath";
import { medianOf } from "./reference";

// Validation loop 1: the brief's rules as amended by the PM's rulings on the
// Codex G3 gate (PM/cycle1/brief-rvol-clock.md, "Rulings"). Tests under
// "amended brief" describe the required behaviour and FAIL against build
// ce8d11c until the builder's fix round; they are the settling tests.

afterEach(() => {
  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
});

const SETTINGS: RelativeVolumeSettings = { ...RELATIVE_VOLUME_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, showRolling: true };
const EQUITY = relativeVolumeEquityFixture();
const T0 = Date.UTC(2026, 2, 2);

function hourly(day: number, hour: number, volume: number, closed = true): BarFrame {
  return barFrame(T0 + day * 24 * HOUR_MS + hour * HOUR_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume }, BINANCE_PERP, HOUR_MS, closed);
}

function tableTexts(owner: string): string[] {
  const grid = (tablesForOwners([owner])[0].data as TableData).committedGrid;
  return ["0,0", "1,0", "2,0"].map((key) => grid[key]?.text ?? "");
}

describe("status precedence (rule 11) through the runtime's table, including the median-zero case the builder did not drive", () => {
  it("unsupported, no usable volume, learning, no usable baseline, forming, ready, in that order", () => {
    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, []);
    const owner = () => session.runtime.getDrawOwnerId(relativeVolumeIndicator.id)!;
    try {
      // Hourly UTC bars, slot 0 only carries information: zero volume on days 1..3, so its median is 0 on day 4.
      for (let day = 0; day < 3; day += 1) {
        for (let hour = 0; hour < 24; hour += 1) session.dispatch({ bar: hourly(day, hour, hour === 0 ? 0 : 100) });
      }
      expect(tableTexts(owner())[2]).toBe("Learning · 2 of 3 prior");
      session.dispatch({ bar: hourly(3, 0, 50) });
      expect(tableTexts(owner())).toEqual(["RVOL — · 3 prior", "Rolling 0.5× · 20 bars", "No usable baseline"]);
      expect(session.capture().outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBeNull();
      session.dispatch({ bar: hourly(3, 1, 70, false) });
      expect(tableTexts(owner())).toEqual(["RVOL 0.7× · 3 prior", "Rolling 0.7× · 20 bars", "Forming · vs full bar"]);
      session.dispatch({ bar: hourly(3, 1, Number.NaN, false) });
      expect(tableTexts(owner())).toEqual(["RVOL — · 3 prior", "Rolling — · 20 bars", "No usable volume"]);
      session.dispatch({ bar: hourly(3, 1, 120) });
      expect(tableTexts(owner())).toEqual(["RVOL 1.2× · 3 prior", "Rolling 1.2× · 20 bars", ""]);
      const twoHour = barFrame(T0 + 4 * 24 * HOUR_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: Number.NaN }, BINANCE_PERP, 2 * HOUR_MS);
      session.dispatch({ bar: twoHour });
      // Unsupported wins over an unusable volume.
      expect(tableTexts(owner())).toEqual(["RVOL — · 0 prior", "Rolling — · 20 bars", "Needs 1m to 1h"]);
      expect(session.capture().faulted).toBe(false);
    } finally {
      session.close();
    }
  });
});

describe("older-than-tail dispatch, duplicate close and the forming tail, through the indicator def", () => {
  it("a dispatch older than the tail changes no output, no ring and no marker", () => {
    const bars = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 4 * 390 + 30);
    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, []);
    try {
      for (const bar of bars) session.dispatch({ bar });
      const before = session.capture();
      // The runtime would classify this REBUILT; drive the def directly to reach the engine's own guard.
      const older = { ...bars[3 * 390 + 5], volume: 9_999_999, closed: true };
      const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
      for (const bar of bars) engine.onBar(bar);
      const snapshot = JSON.stringify({ tail: engine.tail, slot: engine.slotRing(575), rolling: engine.rollingRing });
      const reading = engine.onBar(older);
      expect(reading).toBe(engine.current);
      expect(JSON.stringify({ tail: engine.tail, slot: engine.slotRing(575), rolling: engine.rollingRing })).toBe(snapshot);
      expect(session.capture()).toEqual(before);
    } finally {
      session.close();
    }
  });

  it("a replay whose tail is forming leaves the tail provisional: marker at 50 percent alpha, status Forming, nothing filed", () => {
    const bars = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, 4 * 390 + 10);
    const last = bars[bars.length - 1];
    const prior = EQUITY.bars.filter((bar, index) => index < bars.length - 1 && nyLocalMinuteOfDay(bar.ts_open) === nyLocalMinuteOfDay(last.ts_open)).map((bar) => bar.volume);
    const median = medianOf(prior.slice(-10));
    const tail = { ...last, closed: false, volume: Math.ceil(median * 2.5) } as BarFrame;
    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, [...bars.slice(0, -1), tail]);
    try {
      const capture = session.capture();
      expect(capture.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBeCloseTo(tail.volume / median, 12);
      const marker = capture.markers.find((call) => call.time === tail.ts_open);
      expect(marker).toMatchObject({ shape: "circle", position: "aboveBar", color: "rgba(37,99,235,0.5)" });
      const owner = session.runtime.getDrawOwnerId(relativeVolumeIndicator.id)!;
      expect(tableTexts(owner)[2]).toBe("Forming · vs full bar");
      const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
      for (const bar of [...bars.slice(0, -1), tail]) engine.onBar(bar);
      expect(engine.tail!.committed).toBe(false);
      expect(engine.slotRing(nyLocalMinuteOfDay(tail.ts_open))!.items.some((item) => item.tsOpen === tail.ts_open)).toBe(false);
    } finally {
      session.close();
    }
  });
});

describe("corrections through the runtime (amendments 1 and 2), judged against a feed that only saw the final state", () => {
  const index = 14 * 390 + 100; // session 15: every slot holds 10, the rolling ring 20
  const bar = EQUITY.bars[index];
  const prefix = EQUITY.bars.slice(0, index);
  const suffix = EQUITY.bars.slice(index + 1, index + 30);

  function finalStateFeed(finalVolume: number): ReturnType<typeof driveLive> {
    return driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, [...prefix, { ...bar, volume: finalVolume } as BarFrame, ...suffix].map((b) => ({ bar: b })), SETTINGS);
  }

  it("valid(a) → valid(b) → invalid → valid(c) → next bars equals a feed that only ever saw c", () => {
    const arrivals: Arrival[] = prefix.map((b) => ({ bar: b }));
    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: 500 } }, { bar: { ...bar, volume: 900 } }, { bar: { ...bar, volume: Number.NaN } }, { bar: { ...bar, volume: 3_000 } });
    for (const later of suffix) arrivals.push({ bar: later });
    const corrected = driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, arrivals, SETTINGS);
    const clean = finalStateFeed(3_000);
    expect(corrected.outputs).toEqual(clean.outputs);
    expect(corrected.markers).toEqual(clean.markers);
    expect(corrected.outputs.rvol[index]).toBeGreaterThan(2);
  });

  it("valid → invalid, then the next bar arrives: the observation stays out and what its insertion evicted stays restored", () => {
    const arrivals: Arrival[] = prefix.map((b) => ({ bar: b }));
    arrivals.push({ bar: { ...bar, volume: 500 } }, { bar: { ...bar, volume: Number.NaN } });
    for (const later of suffix) arrivals.push({ bar: later });
    const corrected = driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, arrivals, SETTINGS);
    const clean = finalStateFeed(Number.NaN);
    expect(corrected.outputs).toEqual(clean.outputs);
    expect(corrected.outputs.rvol[index]).toBeNull();
    // The same slot one session earlier still counts: ten prior observations for the next bar of this slot would have been 10 either way.
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 3, clock: "auto" });
    for (const b of prefix) engine.onBar(b);
    const key = nyLocalMinuteOfDay(bar.ts_open);
    const before = engine.slotRing(key)!.items.map((item) => item.tsOpen);
    engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: 500 });
    engine.onBar({ ...bar, volume: Number.NaN });
    engine.onBar(suffix[0]);
    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.items.map((item) => item.tsOpen)).toEqual(before);
    expect(engine.slotRing(key)!.evictedByTail).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("the resampler's 15:30 hourly bar", () => {
  it("keys 930 through the def and divides only by earlier 15:30 bars, never by 15:00 bars", () => {
    const hourly: BarFrame[] = [];
    for (let session = 0; session < 5; session += 1) hourly.push(...resampleBars(EQUITY.bars.slice(session * 390, (session + 1) * 390), HOUR_MS));
    expect(hourly).toHaveLength(35);
    const capture = replayCapture(relativeVolumeIndicator, hourly, SETTINGS);
    const lastHalfHour = hourly[34];
    expect(nyLocalMinuteOfDay(lastHalfHour.ts_open)).toBe(930);
    expect(lastHalfHour.interval_ms).toBe(HOUR_MS);
    const earlier930 = hourly.filter((bar, index) => index < 34 && nyLocalMinuteOfDay(bar.ts_open) === 930).map((bar) => bar.volume);
    const earlier870 = hourly.filter((bar, index) => index < 34 && nyLocalMinuteOfDay(bar.ts_open) === 870).map((bar) => bar.volume);
    expect(earlier930).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[34]).toBeCloseTo(lastHalfHour.volume / medianOf(earlier930), 12);
    expect(capture.outputs.rvol[34]).not.toBeCloseTo(lastHalfHour.volume / medianOf(earlier870), 3);
    // The half-hour bucket is smaller than the full 15:00 hour even with the closing surge, so the two denominators differ.
    expect(medianOf(earlier930) / medianOf(earlier870)).toBeLessThan(0.9);
  });
});

describe("amended brief (PM rulings on Codex G3): FAIL against ce8d11c until the builder's fix round", () => {
  it("rule 7(b) withdrawn: a bar superseded without ever closing contributes NO observation to either ring", () => {
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 10, minSessions: 2, clock: "utc" });
    for (let day = 0; day < 2; day += 1) for (let hour = 0; hour < 24; hour += 1) engine.onBar(hourly(day, hour, 60));
    engine.onBar(hourly(2, 0, 30, false)); // half the bucket arrived, then the source went quiet
    engine.onBar(hourly(2, 1, 60)); // the next bucket supersedes it
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.items.map((item) => item.volume)).toEqual([60, 60]);
    expect(engine.rollingRing.items.some((item) => item.tsOpen === T0 + 2 * 24 * HOUR_MS)).toBe(false);
    const next = engine.onBar(hourly(3, 0, 60));
    expect(next.priorCount).toBe(2);
    expect(next.rvol).toBe(1);
  });

  it("rule 7(b) withdrawn, through the runtime: a resampled bucket truncated by a source gap is not committed when the next bucket arrives", () => {
    // Source minutes: day 1 hours 0..2 complete; day 2 hour 0 has only its first 30 minutes, then minutes resume at hour 1.
    const minutes: BarFrame[] = [];
    const push = (day: number, hour: number, minute: number) =>
      minutes.push(barFrame(T0 + day * 24 * HOUR_MS + hour * HOUR_MS + minute * MINUTE_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume: 1 }, BINANCE_PERP));
    for (let hour = 0; hour < 3; hour += 1) for (let minute = 0; minute < 60; minute += 1) push(0, hour, minute);
    for (let minute = 0; minute < 30; minute += 1) push(1, 0, minute);
    for (let hour = 1; hour < 3; hour += 1) for (let minute = 0; minute < 60; minute += 1) push(1, hour, minute);
    for (let hour = 0; hour < 3; hour += 1) for (let minute = 0; minute < 60; minute += 1) push(2, hour, minute);
    const resampled = resampleBars(minutes, HOUR_MS);
    const truncated = resampled.find((bar) => bar.ts_open === T0 + 24 * HOUR_MS)!;
    expect(truncated).toMatchObject({ volume: 30, closed: false });
    const live = driveLive(relativeVolumeIndicator, resampled.map((bar) => ({ bar })), { ...SETTINGS, minSessions: 2 });
    const day3hour0 = resampled.findIndex((bar) => bar.ts_open === T0 + 2 * 24 * HOUR_MS);
    // Amended rule: prior observations for slot 0 are [60] only, below minSessions 2, so day 3 hour 0 is still learning (null), not 60 / median([60, 30]) = 1.33.
    expect(live.outputs.rvol[day3hour0]).toBeNull();
  });

  it("sessions has a minimum of 3 in the schema and in the engine's clamp", () => {
    const field = RELATIVE_VOLUME_SETTINGS_SCHEMA.find((candidate) => candidate.kind !== "staticHint" && candidate.key === "sessions");
    expect(field && field.kind === "numeric" ? field.min : null).toBe(3);
    expect(RVOL_MIN_SESSIONS_SETTING).toBe(3);
    const engine = createRelativeVolumeEngine({ sessions: 2, minSessions: 2, clock: "utc" });
    for (const volume of [100, 1_000, 250, 100]) engine.onBar(hourly(engine.tail === null ? 0 : (engine.tail.tsOpen - T0) / (24 * HOUR_MS) + 1, 0, volume));
    expect(engine.slotRing(0)!.capacity).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
  });

  it("a cosmetic setting never changes a number: changing a colour after a rollover leaves every output and marker as it was", () => {
    // Eleven days of one-minute bars: 100 per minute for five days, then 300, the last bar 450 (Codex item 1).
    const bars: BarFrame[] = [];
    for (let index = 0; index < 11 * 1_440; index += 1) {
      const volume = index === 11 * 1_440 - 1 ? 450 : index < 5 * 1_440 ? 100 : 300;
      bars.push(barFrame(T0 + index * MINUTE_MS, { open: 1, close: 1, high: 1, low: 1, volume }, BINANCE_PERP));
    }
    const session = openLiveSession(relativeVolumeIndicator, SETTINGS, {}, []);
    try {
      for (const bar of bars) session.dispatch({ bar });
      const before = session.capture();
      expect(before.outputs.rvol.at(-1)).toBe(2.25);
      // Every setting the schema calls cosmetic, changed one at a time; if the builder removes the pickers the loop is empty and the rule holds trivially.
      const cosmetic = RELATIVE_VOLUME_SETTINGS_SCHEMA.filter((field) => field.kind !== "staticHint" && field.affects === "cosmetic");
      for (const field of cosmetic) {
        if (field.kind === "staticHint") continue;
        const key = field.key as keyof RelativeVolumeSettings;
        const value = field.kind === "color" ? "#123456" : field.kind === "boolean" ? !SETTINGS[key] : SETTINGS[key];
        session.updateSettings({ ...SETTINGS, [key]: value });
        const after = session.capture();
        expect(after.outputs.rvol.at(-1), `after changing cosmetic ${String(key)}`).toBe(2.25);
        expect(after.outputs.rvol).toEqual(before.outputs.rvol);
        expect(after.markers.map((call) => call.time)).toEqual(before.markers.map((call) => call.time));
        session.updateSettings(SETTINGS);
      }
    } finally {
      session.close();
    }
  });
});
